 Catalog number : I00042
Uniform title: Sahasrāgama
Manuscript : IFP manuscript bundle RE15540
Description:
RE15540, this manuscript bundle titled Śaivāgama, however it is a text of Sahasrāgama. The codex primarily focused the Karṣaṇādi-pratiṣṭhāntabhāgaḥ (From drawing to installation) part of this Mūlāgama. It begins with the Aṣṭabandhanavidhi and continues with detailed chapters such as Anāvartapratiṣṭhāvidhi, Āvartapratiṣṭhāvidhi, Punarāvartapratiṣṭhāvidhi, Ācāryalakṣaṇavidhi and Dravyabhāgakrama of the Sahasrāgama. It further includes rituals such as Viśeṣatarpaṇavidhi, Padanyāsavidhi, Yāgaśālānirmāṇavidhi, Trikhaṇḍaśodhanavidhi, Nāḍīsandhānahomvidhi, Ākarṣaṇavidhi, Yātrāhomavidhi, Yātrādānavidhi, and Āhutipramāṇavidhi, etc.

The bundle contains 88 folios and there are 9-11 lines in each folio. The size of the folio is 24x4 cm. This manuscript serves as a meticulous guide to Āgamic consecration and related rituals, offering a structured account of practices ranging from initial preparations to advanced ritualistic processes. Its inclusion of unique topics such as Nāḍīsandhāna and Yātrādānam reflects the depth and diversity of Śaivāgama traditions.
Notes:

உ
oṃ śrī gurubhyo namaḥ
śrīsundaragaṇapataye namaḥ
sahasratantram
oṃ
aṣṭabandhanam - re 15540


notes:

hariḥ om
athātassaṃpravakṣyāmi aṣṭabandhavidhi kramam । (i.4b)
sarvapāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvasiddhikaraṃ śubham ॥
rājarāṣṭravivṛdhyatvaṃ sarvalokaśubhapradam । (2)
sarvadoṣanivṛtyatvamaṣṭabandhanamācaret ॥
aṣṭabandhaṃ yadā hīne rājā durjayamāpnuyāt । (3)
yakṣarākṣasapaiśācailloka pīḍā vrajāyate ॥
aṣṭabandhanahīne tu pūjā naivedyameva ca । (4)
bali nityotsavaṃ caiva namaskāra pradakṣiṇī(ṇa)m ॥
nṛttagītaṃ ca vādyaṃ ca nityaṃ naimittikaṃ tathā । (5)
upacārāṇi sarvāṇi niṣphalaṃ tu vidhīyate ॥
pūrve tu kalahaṃ vidyādāgneyyāmagnidāhakam । (6)
yāme tu vyādhī pīḍā ca naiṛtyāṃ grāmanāśanam ॥
vāruṇyāṃ varṣanāśaṃ syādvāyavyāṃ sasya nāśanam । (7)
saumye tvattha vināśaṃ ca tvaiśānyāṃ putra nāśanam ॥
parito bandhahīne tu sarva nāśakaraṃ bhavet । (8)
śakti sthāneṣu nirmālyaṃ śaivavargo vini(na)śyati ॥
viṣṇubhāge tu nirmālyaṃ nṛpavargo vini(na)śyati । (9)
brahmabhāge tu nirmālyaṃ brahmavarga vināśanam ॥ 9 ॥
nandyāvarta śilābhāge viṭ śūdrāti(di) vini(na)śyati ।
kūrmasthāne tu nirmālyaṃ sarvanāśakaraṃ bhavet ॥ (10)
tasmāt sarva prayatnena tvaṣṭabandhanamācaret ।
prathamaṃ sadyabandhaṃ syāt kramādbandhaṃ dvitīyakam ॥ (i.5a)
parito bandhahīnaṃ syāt tṛtīyaṃ bandhana kramam ।
yavādyaṣṭa vicchinne sadyobandhanamācaret ॥ (2)
nityasnapana vedyāyāṃ śivāgre sthaṇi(ṇḍi)laṃ kṣipet ।
tadūrdhve kalaśāsthāpya snapanoktaṃ prapūjayet ॥ (3)
ghaṭāgre homakarmāṇi hutvā tu vidhivat budhaḥ ।
sandhānamaṣṭabandhena kṛtvā gavyābhiṣecanam ॥ (4)
kalaśairabhiṣekaṃ ca kṣīrabhārābhiṣecanam ।
naivedyaṃ dāpayecchambho sadyobandhanamīritam ॥ (5)
dvādaśāṅgula vicchihnai(nne) kramādbandhanamucyate ।
toraṇaṃ kuṇḍa vo(ve)diṃ ca yāgaśālāṃ prakalpya ca ॥ (6)
liṅgamūrtiṃ samādāya kumbhe saṃyojayetkramāt । (7)
yāgapūjā prakāreṇa pūjāhomaṃ samapya(rpya) ca ॥
pūrvavadbandhanaṃ kṛtvā rātriśeṣaṃ samarpya ca । (8)
abhiṣekaṃ prabhāte ca kramādbandhaṃ samācaret ॥
parito bandhavicchinne nandyāvarta vihīnake । (9)
liṅgasyādhārapāde ca liṅge calanasaṃyute ॥
tadrāṣṭrānāśanaṃ proktaṃ nṛpati stvātyayā(nyathā) bhavet । (10)
tasmāt sarva prayatnena punaḥ sandhānamācaret ॥
atha bandhakriyā sarva kathyate kramaśe(śo)dhana । (i.5b)
saptāhamuttamaṃ proktaṃ navāhaṃ madhyamaṃ bhavet ॥
pakṣāhamadhamaṃ jñeyaṃ liṅge svāyambhuvasya tu ।
devike divya liṅge ca śreṣṭha saptāhamuttamam ॥ (2)
ārṣaliṅgeṣu pañcāha navāhaṃ gāṇape tathā ।
rākṣase mānuṣe caiva viśeṣa madhunocyate ॥ (3)
mānuṣānāṃ tu liṅgānāmantrakārya ghaṭasthitiḥ ।
tadūrdhvaṃ kumbhasaṃsthaṃ ced deveśaḥ ssarvadoṣa kṛt ॥ (4)
tasmāt sarva prayatnena māsānte vātha pūrvake ।
kumbhasthaṃ yojayelliṅge tvabhiṣekamathācaret ॥ (5)
aṅkurāhaṃ samārabhya tripañcānavasaptake ।
yāgaṃ kuryādviśeṣeṇa tadvidhāna mahocyate ॥ (6)
dvikālaṃ tu trayāhe tu ṣaṭkālaṃ dina pañcake ।
sāyaṃ prātaḥ krameṇaiva dinamekaṃ praśasyate ॥ (7)
athavānya prakāreṇa kārayeddinasaṃkhyakam ।
catuṣṣkālaṃ trayāhe tu pañcāheṣṭau prakīrtitāḥ ॥ (8)
saptāhe dvādaśaḥ proktaḥ navāhe ṣoḍaśaḥ smṛtaḥ ।
aṣṭāviṃśati kālastu pakṣāheti prakīrtitaḥ ॥
māsacaiṣṭa pañcāśatkālabhedāḥ prakīrtitāḥ । (9)
aṅkurādi pratiṣṭhāntaṃ dinasaṃkhyā prakīrtitāḥ ॥
sadyodhivāsane caiva niśī cevāgnihomayet । (10)
sarvamāseṣu kartavyamaṣṭabandhanamācaret ॥
dinavārama(mu)hūrtādīn varjayettu vicakṣaṇaḥ । (11)
aṣṭabandha tribandhaṃ ca pañcabandhanameva ca ॥
liṅgapīṭhāntare caiva pratimāyāṃ tathaiva ca ।
bandhayettadvidhāne tvaṣṭa pañca tribandhanam ॥ (i.6a)
sṛṣṭisthitiṃ ca saṃhāraṃ kārayettu vicakṣaṇaḥ ।
bandhanaṃ cāṣṭabandhasyā śrīkaraṃ pañcabandhanam ॥ (2)
mokṣaṃ caiva tribandhaṃ syāt śrīkaraṃ pañcabandhanam ।
me(mo)kṣaṃ caiva tribandhaṃ syāt vaṃśasandhāna vṛddhikṛt ॥ (3)
kāṣāyaṃ śarkarā cūrṇaṃ raktakārpāsa tulyakam ।
lākṣācūrṇaṃ madhuściṣṭaṃ hiṃgulaṃ kundariṣkakam ॥ (4)
māhiṣaṃ navanītaṃ cāhyaṣṭadravyamihocyate ।
tribhāgaṃ śarkarā cūrṇaṃ dvibhāgaṃ kundariṣkakam ॥ (5)
bhāgaika lākṣacūrṇaṃ ca kāṣāyaṃ ca tripādakam ।
madhuściṣṭaṃ tripādaṃ ca tathāvai raktatūlakam ॥ (6)
navanītaṃ tripādaṃ ca hiṃgulaṃ pādamucyate ।
hiṃgulaṃ sa vihīne tu bandhanaṃ balahīnakam ॥
etadravyaṃ ca prastena bhāgaṃ kuryādyathākramam । (7)
karābhyāmardayitvātha bandhayeddeśikottamaḥ ॥
aathavānya prakāreṇa kartavyaṃ bandhanakramam । (8)
kundariṣkaphalaṃ nāḍi bāṇa bhāgā tu śarkarā ॥
phalamekaṃ tu kāṣāyaṃ lākṣārtha phalamucyate । (9)
madhuściṣṭaṃ tadardhaṃ tu tadardhaṃ gulamucyate ॥
tasyardhaṃ tūlamevaṃ tu navanītena miśram । (10)
tulāyāḥ palamātreṇa hastāhlāṃ miśramardayet ॥
aṣṭabandhanamevoktaṃ pañcabandhamataḥ param । (i.6b)
lākṣāśarkarapāṣāṇaṃ māhiṣaṃ kundariṣkakam ॥
madhuściṣṭa samāyuktaṃ pañcabandhana mucyate ।
śarkarā kundariṣkaṃ ca navanītā tribandhakam ॥ (2)
svāyabhurdevike bāṇe bandhaye ca tribandhakam ।
divye ca gāṇave caiva ārṣake pañcabandhakam ॥ (3)
mānuṣe rākṣase caiva pratimāyāṃ ca pīṭhake ।
parivārādi sarveṣu bandhaye cāṣṭabandhanam ॥
svarṇena rajatenaiva sīsenaiva tu bandhayet । (4)
apakvaṃ pakvamevaṃ tu dvividhaṃ bandhana kramam ॥
pakvaṃ tu pācayogyaṃ syād apakvaṃ mardanaṃ bhavet । (5)
pakvāpakvamidaṃ jñātvā bandhayet sudṛḍhaṃ budhaḥ ॥
apakvaṃ devikaṃ proktaṃ supakvaṃ madhunocyate । (6)
tatoṣṭabandhanaṃ kuryāt tailaṃ gaulaṃ ca gaurikam ॥
guggulaṃ sikataṃ sisthaṃ sajalaṃ sajjalākṣakam । (7)
vyomapakṣāgni ṛgbāṇa rasamanya tritāgatim ॥
tailātproktāḥ kramādbhāgāt pākaṃ citkaṇavadbhavet । (8)
śaṅkhacūrṇatilaiścaiva triphalaṃ kundariṣkakam ॥
kadali dadhisaṃyuktaṃ sapakvaṃ cāṣṭabandhanaṃ । (9)
pīṭhitaṃ śivātmakaṃ kṛtvā śivamārādhayentaraḥ ॥
bhaktyā vittānusāreṇa prakuryādaṣṭabandhanam ।
kalpayeddhitavā tulyaṃ phalamāḍhyaṃ daridrayoḥ ॥ (10)
vittaśāḍhyena yaḥ kuryāddraoyenāḍyo vimānavāḥ ।
na catat balamāpnoti lobhenākrāntamānasāḥ ॥ (i.7a)
svasyadravya tridhābhajya jīvananaidhākāmādiśet ।
bhāgadvayaṃ ca dharmārthastato nityaṃ ca jīvati ॥ (2)
dharmadravyatāmrapātre sthāpayitvā śivāya ca ।
sa hiraṇyaṃ pradātavyaṃ prītipūrvaṃ śivārpitam ॥ (3)
bhaktyā stvatyantadhanikā rājānovā svakairdhanaiḥ ।
śivadharma prakurvantu tatra śāḍhyanatejanāḥ ॥
tasmāntalobhaye vrājñā vittaśāḍhyaṃ tu doṣakṛt । (4)
alpavitto daridraśca yācayitvā dhanaṃ bahu ॥
sa grāhyai kukriyurmiṣṭā śivadharmācaraṃ tu te । (5)
śivadharmasamuddiśyā yācayitvā dhanaṃ bahu ॥
śivadharmān prakurvantu vittaśāḍhyaṃ na kārayet । (6)
yācakādhanadāścaiva phalamagnanti bhuridaḥ ॥
ena konāpyuvāyena dhanaṃ sañcaya mānavāḥ । (7)
śivadharma prakurvantu śivadharmohi durlabhaḥ ॥
durlabho mānuṣaṃ janma śivadharmohi durlabhaḥ । (8)
bahujanma kṛtāt puṇyāt jīvopyubhayabhāgbhavet ॥
enakenapyuvāyena śivadharmāṃ caranti ye । (9)
iṣṭabhogān paraṃ prāpya mahīyate janā paraiḥ ॥
śivaloke śivenaiva sahavāsaṃ caranti vai । (10)
śivadharmasamuddiśya pratigṛṇhanti ye janāḥ ॥
dhanaṃ dadanti yo lokāḥ āpnuyāsthāmubhāvapi । (i.7b)
iṣṭasiddhiṃ ca sīmīpya trikārya vicāraṇā ॥
śivadharma samuddiśya yācakadhanadāstathā ।
prerakā modakā caiva kartārakārakastathā ॥ (2)
āpnuyāsyuḥ parante te śīvasāmīpya mañjasā ।
śivadharma samuddiśya sammārjita dhanañjanāḥ ॥ (3)
dravyabhāgaḥ
tāmrapātre sthāpayitvā dravyaśuddhiṃ samācaret ।
astreṇa ca śataṃ japtvā homayedaiśivottamāḥ ॥ (4)
tena dravyasya śuddhiṃ syācchuddha dravyaṃ śivāya ca ।
nivedya ca guruṃ paścācchiva dharmā samācaret ॥
śivasaṅkalpitaṃ dravyamekodaśavibhāvayet । (5)
bhāgatrayaṃ ca yāgārtha ācāryāya tathaiva ca ॥
viprāṇāṃ bhojanaṃ yasyāt bhāgadvitīyamevahi । (6)
tathā bhāgadvayaṃ jñeyaṃ japadānārthakaṃ bhavet ॥
tripakṣayajanārthāya hyavaśiṣṭaṃ samāpayet । (7)
yāgārthamaṇḍapaṃ kuryāt yāmyāgrevātha pārśvayoḥ ॥
aiśānyāṃ pāvake deśe athavāvānya deśake । (8)
bhūmiṃ saṃśodhyajānvantaṃ samaṃ kṛtyopalipya ca ॥
muṣṭimātra samutsedhaṃ darpaṇodarasannibham । (9)
śiva yajñe viśeṣeṇa pañcahasta pramāṇataḥ ॥
eka hasta vividhyā tu manuhasta samāvidhi ।
caturaśraṃ samaṃ kṛtvā padavinyāsamācaret ॥ (10)
pūrvāgraṃ ravisūtraṃ syāt udagāgraṃ tathaiva ca ।
ekaviṃśādhika śataṃ padabhāga samīritam ॥ (i.8a)
madhye tvekapade brahmye pradhānaṃ vardhanītathā ।
devikoṣṭapade kumbhān paiśācaṃ ṣoḍaśaṃ bhavet ॥ (2)
pūjārthaṃ ca caturdikṣu vidikṣu stambhamucyate ।
śiṣṭa maṣṭapade caiva hyaṣṭamaṅgulamīritam ॥ (3)
ṣaṭ pañcāśattato bāhye rākṣase ghaṭa toraṇam ।
ācārya śiṣya sañcāraṃ yogyaśiṣṭapadaṃ bhavet ॥ (4)
stambha sthānaṃ pade bāhye ityevaṃ padasaṃsthitaḥ ।
ṣoḍaśa stambha saṃyuktaṃ dvādaśastambhameva ca ॥
maṇṭapaṃ vā catuṣkaṃ vā śalāṃ vāpi prakalpayet । (5)
maṇṭapasya tu tanmadhye padenante tu vedikā ॥
vistārārtha samutsedhaṃ caturaśra samaṃ tataḥ । (6)
dvādaśāṅgula samutsedhaṃ ṣaḍaṅgulaṃ tu vistaram ॥
mukurodara saṅkāśamupavediṃ ca kārayet । (7)
kuṇḍāni paritaḥ kuryāt mānuṣe tu padehitaḥ ॥
yugāśraṃ yoni śaśabhṛt tryaśraṃ bāṇāśrameva ca । (8)
ṣaḍaśraṃ padmamaṣṭāśraṃ aindrādiṣu prakalpayet ॥
rudrakhaṇḍalayormadhye mūlapīṭhākṛtīyyatho । (9)
tathā kuṇḍākṛtī proktā pradhānaṃ kuṇḍamucyate ॥
pañcāgnyāyatane kuṇḍaṃ vedyāḥ paścimato nyaset ।
indraṃ ca navakuṇḍaṃ tu tvaṣṭabhogādi siddhidaṃ ॥ (11)
pañcakuṇḍaphalaṃ proktaṃ iṣṭasiddhi pradāyakam ।
śrīkaraṃ tvekakuṇḍaṃ ca gomayenopalepayet ॥
puṇyāhaṃ vācayettatra brāhmaṇān bhojayettataḥ । (i.8b)
paścāduścchiṣṭamudvāsya punaḥ puṇyāhamācaret ॥
aṅkurāt pūrva divase tvācāryo ṛtyubhissaha । (2)
vapanaṃ dehaśuddhyarthamācarettu yathākramam ॥
adhivāsa dināt pūrva nityapūjāvasānake । (3)
vighneśaṃ mūlaberaṃ ca saṃpūjya ca viśeṣataḥ ॥
uttarābhimukho bhūtvā tatrādau gaṇanāyakaḥ । (4)
dhyātvā saṅkalpayeddhīmān vidhivaddeśikottamaḥ ॥
dhūpadīpadināt pūjya tāmbūlaṃ nālikerakam ।
nivedya mūrtipaisārthaṃ anujñāṃ kārayedguruḥ ॥ (5)
vighnānapoha yātrāśu prasīda gaṇanāyaka ।
prārthyevaṃ śubhanakṣatre sudine caralagnake ॥ (6)
mūlaliṅge samāsādya hyuttarābhimukhasthitaḥ ।
prāṇāyāmaṃ purā kṛtvā saṅkalpaṃ ca tataḥ param ॥ (7)
aṣṭapuṣpai samabhyarcya tāmbūlādi nivedayet ।
śiva sarva jagannātha sadāśiva namostute ॥ (8)
aṣṭabandha vidhānārthaṃ anujñāṃ dehi me prabho ।
tadhāstviti śivenoktaṃ labdhānujñā śivājñayā ॥ (9)
devyādi bandhavicchinne tadantikamanuṃ vrajet ।
anujñāṃ kārayedvidvān mṛtsaṅgrahaṇamārabhet ॥ (10)
etān devān puraskṛtya caṇḍamastraṃ viśeṣataḥ ।
saṃpūjya tābhyāṃ gatvātha sarvavādya samanvitaḥ ॥ (11)
uttamaṃ prāgudīcīsyāt madhyamaṃ dakṣiṇasmṛtaḥ ।
adhamaṃ paścimaṃ proktaṃ nivṛje deśikottamaḥ ॥ (i.9a)
nadītīre bilvamūle taṭāke tulasīvane ।
kedāre darbhamūle ca valmīke nandane vane ॥
aśvathamūladeśe vā gatvācāryo vidhānataḥ । (2)
śuddhadeśe manuprāpya puṇyāhaṃ vācayedguruḥ ॥
śālibhiḥ sthaṇḍilaṃ caiva kalpaye sthaṇḍilaṃ tilaiḥ । (3)
tanmadhyeṣaḍdalaṃ nyasya sthāpayet sarvakumbhakān ॥
kūrca pallava saṃyuktān sarakta(saratna) salilānvitān । (4)
madhyāddalāgra kumbheṣu pūjayet sapta vāridhīn ॥
athavā tveka kumbhaṃ tu vinyaset sapta vāridhīn । (5)
naivedya dhūpadīpādīpādi kalpayettu viśeṣataḥ ॥
navakoṣṭān samālikhya kumbhāgre sthaṇḍilopari । (6)
madhye brahmāṇamabhyarcya anantādi jayantakam ॥
pūrvādi koṣṭha madhye tu indrādīnarcayedbudhaḥ । (7)
pūjayitvā yathānyāyaṃ svāhānte balimācaret ॥
balimaṇḍala pūrve tu bhūmideviṃ samarcayet । (8)
khanitraṃ pūjayettatra prāṅmukhovāpyudaṅmukhaḥ ॥
prāk śirordhvānanāṃ devi citrākārāṃ vicintayet । (9)
kukṣideśe mṛdaṃ śuddhaṃ mastake rāṣṭranāśanam ॥
pārśvayornṛpa nāśaṃ ca pāde kartā vinaśyati । (10)
gale tu sthāpako nāśaḥ kaṭyāṃ stapati naśyati ॥
parito grāma nāśaṃ ca mṛdaṃ kukṣau tu saṅgṛhet ।
khanitreṇa tridhā dhātvā (khātvā) mṛdaṃ saṅgrāhya pātrake ॥ (i.9b)
vāhayitvā yathoktena vastreṇācchādayedbudhaḥ ।
bhūmi devyāḥ kukṣideśe ghātadoṣa nivṛttaye ॥ (2)
prāṇopaśamanārthāya secayetsaptavāridhīn ।
astikumbhān samāsādaya madi(mṛdi)vinyasya secayet ॥ (3)
paricārakamāhūya śibikāyāṃ gaje'pivā ।
vāhayitvā mṛdaṃ paścānnṛtta gīta samanvitaḥ ॥
grāma pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā prāsādaṃ praviśet guruḥ । (4)
ravedastamanādūrdhvaṃ pañcanāḍyaṃ su lagnake ॥
athavā sapta nāḍyante kārayedaṅkurārpaṇam । (5)
anaṅkuraṃ tu yatkarma tatkarmaṃ niṣphalaṃ bhavet ॥
aṅkurādaṅkuraṃ nāsti kṝtaṃ cet karma nāśanam । (6)
ekālaye dvandvayāgau vapanaṃ vāstuśāntikam ॥
ekatraiva prakartavyaṃ utsavādau vinācaret । (7)
prāsadasyottare deśe dakṣiṇe vānya deśake ॥
aiśānyāṃ yāgaśālayāṃ vāyavyāṃ vāpayedguruḥ । (8)
sthaṇḍilaṃ śālibhiḥ kuryāt padavinyāsa saṃyutam ॥
prāṅmukhaṃ pañcasūtrāṇi pyudaṅmukhaṃ tathaiva ca । (9)
madhye catuṣpadaṃ kṛtvā soma kumbhaṃ prapūjayet ॥
arpayitvāsana dhyāne mūlenāvāhya pūjayet ।
naivedya dhūpadīpādi datvā tatbahi pālikān ॥ (10)
pālikā ghaṭikāśceti śarāvāstu kramādguruḥ ।
īśānādi samārabhya pratyekaṃ yugasaṃkhyayā ॥ (i.10a)
brahma viṣṇuśca rudraśca pālikādyadhidevatāḥ ।
krameṇa dvādaśādityān pālikādiṣu pūjayet ॥ (2)
tila sarṣapa mudgāni māṣa śiṃbāḍhakāni ca ।
kulutthaṃ yava godhūmaṃ nava bījāni saṅgrahet ॥ (3)
jalena kṣālayet dravyaṃ kṣīrasecanavāpayet ।
diśāṃ pratīci mantraṃ ca yājātā iti mantrataḥ ॥
abhimantrya tataḥ śaktiṃ saṃpūjya vāpayecchivam । (4)
candrakumbhastitoyena secayet pālikādikān ॥
kumbhe calāsanaṃ pūjya pūrvokta vidhinā guruḥ । (5)
gulakhaṇḍaṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ pāyasādi nivedayet ॥
dhūpadīpopacāraiśca praṇamya prārthayedguruḥ । (6)
japaṃ samarpya vidhivat paścāt sūtrasya dhāraṇam ॥
utsave ca caturviṃśat pavitre ca ṣoḍaśasmṛtam । (7)
saṃprokṣaṇādyaṣṭabandhena pālikā dvādaśasmṛtaḥ ॥
vratādi niyamārthāya rakṣāsūtrasya dhāraṇam । (8)
tāmbūla bhasmasaṃyuktaṃ taṇḍulaṃ nālikerakam ॥
svarṇādi nirmitaṃ sūtre hyaṣṭanāgān prapūjayet । (9)
uttamaṃ homamākhyātaṃ madhyamaṃ homavarjitam ॥
homakumbhau vinā yatra sūtrakāraṃ tu kanyasam ।
nirdiṣṭadivasātpūrva rātrau kautukabandhanam ॥ (10)
abhāve vaddine vāpi kriyātpūrve yavādguruḥ ।
tatkarma mantra siddhyarthaṃ sūtra pāvaśyakaṃ bhavet ॥ (i.10b)
ahanyahani saṃpūjya nirdiṣṭadivasāt vidhiḥ ।
nirdiṣṭadivase prāpte somamudvāsayet sudhiḥ ॥ (2)
aṅkurāśca jale kṣiptvā kautukaṃ ca visarjayet ।
yāgamaṇṭapa śuddhyarthaṃ vāstudevān samarcayet ॥ (3)
yāgasya maṇṭape madhye balipīṭhāgrake'pivā ।
brahmasthāne maṇṭapādau vāstu yāgaṃ samācaret ॥ (4)
navahasta samāyuktaṃ vedi kuṇḍa samanvitam ।
caturdvāra samāyuktaṃ maṇṭapaṃ kārayettataḥ ॥ (5)
ekāśīti pade madhye vedyā brahmādikānyajet । (6)
pūrvasyāṃ diśi kuṇḍaṃ tu puṇyāhaṃ pāvake diśi ॥
dharmarāja gurosthānaṃ naiṛtyāṃ pañcagavyakam । (7)
paryagnikaraṇaṃ pāśau vayau yogopasādhanam ॥
uttare brahmakumbhaṃ syāt aiśānyāṃ astrarājakam । (8)
brahmāṇaṃ prārgakhobhyarcya ghaṭe ca maṇḍale yajet ॥
tadagre vahnikāryaṃ tu vahniṃ abhimukhānanam । (9)
rātrau kuryādbaliṃ vidvān ādigrāmadi vāstuṣu ॥
rātrau vā vidivā vāpi devadevyālayādiṣu । (10)
maṇḍūkaṃ devatānāṃ ca vāstūnāṃ ca śivaṃ yajet ॥
devīnāṃ anyaśaktīnāṃ padaṃ paramaśāyikam ।
vighnanidhaṃ nityāśu iṣṭakāmyārtha siddhidam ॥ (i.11a)
tatkarma mantra siddhyarthaṃ vāstuyāgaṃ samācaret ।
vedi madhye viśeṣeṇa taṇḍulaiśca tilairapi ॥ (2)
prāgagraṃ navasūtrāṇi udagagraṃ tathaiva ca ।
catuṣṣaṣṭi padaṃ kuryāt karṇa sūtra samanvitam ॥ (3)
catuśśūlaṃ caturvṛttaṃ rajvāṣṭaka vibhūṣitam ।
marmadvādaśakopetaṃ maṇḍūkaṃ parikalpayet ॥ (4)
saṅkalpya vighnarāṭ pūjāṃ puṇyāhodaka secanam ।
pañcagavyārghya salilai secayedvidhivadguruḥ ॥ (5)
madhye catuṣpade brahmā caturdikṣu yathākramaḥ ।
ṣaṭṣaṭ padāni saṃyojya saṃyamādīnyathākramaḥ ॥ (6)
āpāpatsaka dvandvaṃ uktasthānaṃ prapūjayet ।
īśānādi koṇārdhāpadānābhogina kramāt ॥ (7)
bahirvidhi catuṣkoṇeṣvardhekapada bhoginaḥ । (8)
anyatvaikaikamekaṃ ca śūla vṛttaṃ tathaiva ca ॥
tatra sthita tripañcāśaddevatāvāhanaṃ kramāt ।
gandha puṣpai samabhyarcya tāmbūlādi nivedayet ॥ (9)
sūtra madhye baliṃ vidvān trayodaśa pade kṣipet । (10)
pada madhye baliṃ kuryāt dvātriṃśat mūrtisaṃkhyayā ॥
vikarārtha pade caiva balimaṣṭau samācaret । (i.11b)
tripañcāśat baliṃ dadyādyathoktaṃ balinā guruḥ ॥
dadhyājya gula saṃmiśraṃ śālyantaṃ brahmaṇe baliḥ । (2)
samayyā caturṇāṃ tu pāyasaṃ balirucyate ॥
vidikṣvādi devānāṃ aṣṭānāṃ kṛsarānnakam । (3)
īśāvṛta devānāṃ mudgānaṃ bali rucyate ॥
aṣṭānāṃ brahmadevānāṃ māsānnaṃ bali rucyate । (4)
alābhe māṣa saṃmiśraṃ balyantaṃ samudāhṛdam ॥
evaṃ saṃpūjayet paścāt kalaśān sthāpayet guruḥ । (5)
uttare paścime vā'pi hyuktavastaṇḍilaṃ kuru ॥
snapanokta prakāreṇa vāstu kumbhān prapūjayet । (6)
madhye brahmāṇamabhyarcya vāstu devān samarcayet ॥
bhāratī tasya vāme tu pūrvādi kalaśān nyaset । (7)
trayīmayaśca trimūrtiśca padmākṣaḥ padmayonikaḥ ॥
caturmukhaśca dhātā ca pitāmahamajastathā । (8)
mūrtyaṣṭau samabhyarcya hyathavā lokapālakaḥ ॥
gandhaṃ puṣpai samabhyarcya naivedyāni nidāpayet । (9)
samadhyakapade tatra homa sthānaṃ vidhīyate ॥
kuṇḍe vā sthaṇḍile vā'pi agnikāryaṃ samācaret । (10)
vāstubrahmādi devādi agnimadhye prapūjayet ॥
pūrvamājyāhutīrhutvā hyaṣṭāviṃśaścatādhikam । (i.12a)
samillājanna dravyādi daśāśaṃ homayetkramāt ॥
dravyānte vyāhṛtiṃ hutvā paścāddevān hunet kramāt । (2)
samayyakādi devānāṃ hutvā vai taddaśāṃśakam ॥
āpādīnāmadhāṣṭānāṃ aṣṭau pratyeka māhutim । (3)
īśānādyādi devānāṃ pratyekaṃ pañca pañca ca ॥
carakyādaṣṭadevānāṃ homaṃ kuryāt trayāhutiḥ । (4)
taddeva caruṃ hutvā carakyai kravyamāhunet ॥
hunet pratinidhi dravyai kūśmāṇḍādibhireva ca । (5)
māṃsa bhāve tathā kuryāt deśīko bīja varjayet ॥
kūśmāṇḍaṃ khaḍga mantreṇa bhitvā hutvā sruveṇa tu । (6)
pūrṇāhutiṃ tato hutvā dhūpa dīpaṃ samācaret ॥
agnisthaṃ ca pade yojyā padasthaṃ kumbhameva ca । (7)
kumbhasthaṃ hṛdaye yojya paryagnikaraṇaṃ matam ॥
paryagniśuddhirākhyātā sarvadoghadāhakam । (8)
kumbhaṃ vidhyaśudhyarthaṃ homaṃ kramavivardhanam ॥
palāśa śuṣkadarbhādyai muṣṭiṃ kṛtvā dṛḍhaṃ punaḥ । (9)
saṃprokṣya hṛdayenaiva saṃprajvālya tadagninā ॥
yāgadhāmādi śuddhyarthaṃ paryagni karaṇaṃ kuru । (i.12b)
pañcagavye samaṃ prokṣya vardhanī toyasaṃyutam ॥
astramabhyarcya vidhivat dyutvā tajjala saṃyutam । (2)
sarvātodya samāyuktaṃ astradeva samanvitam ॥
evaṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā ulkāṃ bāhye tyajet budhaḥ । (3)
puṇyāhaṃ vācayedvivān sarvatra prokṣayedguruḥ ॥
prabhāte vimale dhīmān vidhivat snānamācaret । (4)
vastrairābharaṇairbhūṣyā nityakarmaṃ samācaret ॥
śambho prasanna pūjārthaṃ snapanaṃ tu samācaret । (5)
prāyaścittaṃ purā kṛtvā homasaṃkhyā hunet kramāt ॥
garbhagṛhaṃ praveśyātha pūjayettu yathāvidhi । (6)
naivedyaṃ dhūpadīpādi upacārān samarpya ca ॥
prasannayajanaṃ certha kṛtvālaṅkāraṇāya ca । (7)
yāgaśālāṃ viśeddhīmān kṛtvālaṅkāraṇādikam ॥
caturdikṣu caturdvāraṃ catustoraṇa bhūṣitam । (8)
aṣṭadikṣu dhvajānaṣṭau lambaye ca pradakṣiṇam ॥
pūrve nīlāṃśukaṃ proktaṃ āgneryāṃ raktacelakam । (9)
dakṣiṇe kṛṣṇacelaṃ syāddhūmracelaṃ tu naiṛte ॥
śaṃkhābhaṃ vāruṇe vastraṃ vāyavyāṃ śabalaṃ tathā । (i.13a)
uttare svarṇabhācelāhyaiśānyāṃ pītavarṇakam ॥
trayodaśāṅgulāyāmaṃ viṃśatyāṅgula dairghikam । (2)
vistāreṇa samaṃ pṛcchaṃ tadardhaṃ śikharaṃ bhavet ॥
catuḥ pṛcchaṃ tripṛcchaṃ vā pṛcchadvaya mathā'pivā । (3)
dhvaja madhye likheddhīmān diggajāśca viśeṣataḥ ॥
pārśve tu kadalīkāyuktaṃ vetṛdvaya samāyutam । (4)
navahastāṣṭahastaṃ ca saptahastamathā'pivā ॥
śeṣaṃ ca dhvajavat kuryāt nānāvastraiśca kārayet । (5)
patāka lakṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ kṣudra dhvaja vidhiṃ śruṇu ॥
bāṇāṅgulaṃ tu vistāraṃ dvādaśāṅgula dairghikam । (6)
vaitradvaya samāyuktaṃ śeṣaṃ vaktre tu vadguru ॥
kṣudra dhvaja vidhiṃ proktaṃ śikhare puccha saṃyutam । (7)
tithi hastāyutaṃ vā'pi arkahastamathā'pi vā ॥
randhre hastayutaṃ vā'pi carūrajju samāyutam । (8)
nānāvastra samāyuktaṃ nānārūpa samāyutam ॥
evaṃ taraṅgamākhyātaṃ saṅgaveṣṭanamucyate । (9)
kṛṣṇaṃ śvetaṃ pūrva (dik) bhāge śveta raktaṃ tu dakṣiṇe ॥
rakta pītaṃ paścime syāt pītakṛṣṇamudagdiśi । (10)
gātrayāmaṃ tu samaṃ vastraṃ niścidraṃ tena veṣṭayet ॥
rakta paṭṭa dukūlādyai kuryād kārpāsa jātinā । (i.13b)
ṣaḍviṃśadasta dīrghaṃ syāddhaṣṭatālaṃ tu vistaram ॥
vitānaṃ lambayormadhye yāgameśvani deśikaḥ । (2)
raṃsāmra nālikeraiśca muktādāma supuṣpakai ॥
ikṣu pravāla tāmbūlaṃ latāviśca vibhūṣayet । (3)
bhūtavedāhi darbhaiśca vyomahastaṃ pralambayet ॥
antaraṃ caturaṅgulyaṃ cūtapallava śobhitam । (4)
aṅguṣṭaparināhaṃ syāt darbhamālābhirāvṛtam ॥
gomayenaivalipyā'tha raṅgavalyādi kārayet । (5)
nānādīpa samāyuktaṃ aṣṭamaṅgalasaṃyutam ॥
daśāyudha samāyuktaṃ puṇyāhāmbubhirūṣyayet । (6)
sthaṇḍilaṃ śālibhiḥ kuryāt vedikopari madhyame ॥ (7)
dviśatai pañcaviṃśatyā vrīhibhiḥ pūjitaṃ tu yat ।
śaktimātramiti jñeyaṃ ānānte naiva kārayet ॥ (8)
śakti sthalaṃ prakūjaṃ ca prasṛtiḥ kuḍupaṃ tathā ।
tathā tasya dañjaliprastaṃ pādakaṃ cāḍhavaivakam ॥ (9)
droṇaṃ ghāri kramāproktaṃ dviguṇaṃ tu pṛthak pṛthak ।
khāri trayaṃ tu bhāraṃ syāt bhārādūrdhvaṃ nāmānakam ॥ (10)
śālibhiḥ stenamānena taṇḍulairūḍusannibhiḥ ।
daśābharaiśca saṃpūrya uttamaṃ tat prakīrtitam ॥ (i.14a)
aṣṭatriṃśaṃ tu droṇaṃ syāt madhyamaṃ ceti kīrtitam ।
adhamaṃ triṃśati droṇaiḥ kartavyaṃ deśikena tu ॥ (2)
śālyārdha taṇḍulaṃ cai taṇḍulārtha tilaṃ tathā ।
tilārdha lāja mevaṃ tu saptadhānyaṃ śivatrayam ॥ (3)
āḍhakaṃ mudgamāṣaṃ ca godhī sarṣapameva ca ।
caṇaniṣpāvakaṃ caiva saptadhānyai prapūjayet ॥ (4)
tanmadhye padmamālikhya sāṣṭapatraṃ sakarṇikam ।
bhānvādi parivārāṇāṃ droṇa droṇa prakīrtitam ॥ (5)
taṇḍulaṃ ca tilaṃ caiva tasyārdhārdhaṃ tathaiva ca ।
droṇa dvayaṃ ghaṭaṃ śambho svarṇenaiva tu kārayet ॥ (6)
sudṛḍhaṃ rajatenaiva droṇa pūrṇaṃ tu vardhanīm ।
śivapūrṇa ghaṭānaṣṭau tāmreṇaiva tu kārayet ॥ (7)
āḍhakeṇaiva pūrṇāsyuḥkalaśā saṃprakīrtitāḥ ।
sarvaṃ tāmreṇa kartavyaṃ kāṃsyaṃ vā mṛṇmayaṃ tu vā ॥ (8)
kartā vittānusāreṇa lakṣaṇoktaṃ prakalpayet ।
uttamaṃ tilamātreṇa madhyamaṃ yava mātrakam ॥ (9)
adhamaṃ guli mātreṇa hyathavā gajanetravat ।
ekasūtraṃ bhavedvyādhi dvisūtraṃ cābhicārake ॥ (10)
trisūtraṃ sarvasiddhiṃ syāt veṣṭayettu pradakṣiṇam ।
trisūtrairveṣṭayet kumbhān vardhanīṃ ca dvisūtrakam ॥ (11)
kalaśāneka sūtrāṇi bhāṇḍeṣu kalaśeṣu ca ।
gavākṣa sadṛśaṃ veṣṭya sadyena kavacena ca ॥
kṣālanaṃ vāmadevena dhūpitaṃ ghoramantrataḥ । (i.14b)
puruṣeśānayo mantraṃ hyathomukhordhvakanyaset ॥
āpovā iti mantreṇa vāri kumbheṣu pūrayet । (2)
nādeyānātha kaubeya śucinācchāda vāriṇā ॥
mṛdbhāṇḍe pūrayettoyaṃ sītalena sugandhinā । (3)
snāna dravyaṃ ca saṅgṛhya vastrapūtena pūrayet ॥
gajyaṃ mṛgamadaṃ caiva karpūraṃ kuṅkumaṃ tathā । (4)
kastūri rocanā caiva ṣaḍ gandhaṃ ceti kīrtitam ॥
himāmbunā ca saṃyuktaṃ kumbhamadhye supūjayet ।
uttamaṃ daśaniṣkaṃ syāt madhyamaṃ tu tadardhakam ॥ (5)
tadardhamadhamaṃ jñeyaṃ pratimāṃ kārayedbudhaḥ ।
manonmanyāstu pratimā tasyārdhaṃ caiva kārayet ॥ (6)
vidyeśvaraghaṭānāṃ tu tasyārdhaṃ hemapaṭṭakam ।
parivāraghaṭānāṃ tu tasyārdhaṃ hemapaṭṭakam ॥ (7)
yādṛśaṃ pratimākāraṃ tādṛśaṃ bhāvyabuddhimān ।
sadāśivaśca gauryāśca vidyeśānāṃ tvaṇuṃ likhet ॥ (8)
tattat kumbhasya tanmadhye nikṣipet deśikottamaḥ ।
śreṣṭaṃ dvādaśaniṣkaṃ syāt madhyamaṃ cāṣṭaniṣkakam ॥ (9)
kanyasaṃ pañcaniṣkaṃ syāt pradhānasya mahotphala ।
nīlotpalaṃ tu tasyardhaṃ vardhanyāṃ nikṣipetsudhīḥ ॥ (10)
vidyeśānāṃ tu tasyārdhaṃ hemaśūlaṃ vidhīyate ।
māṇikkamindranīlaṃ tu vajraṃ marakataṃ tathā ॥ (11)
vaiḍhūryaṃ sphaṭikaṃ caiva padmarāgaṃ ca mauktikam ।
pravāla navaratnāni kumbhamadhye vinikṣipet ॥ (i.15a)
vajraṃ vaiḍūryakaṃ caiva indranīlaṃ ca mautikam ।
pravālaṃ pañcaratnāni vardhanyāṃ nikṣipet kramāt ॥ (2)
māṇikkaṃ sphaṭikaṃ vajraṃ triratnakalaśeṣvapi ।
svarṇa yajñopavītaṃ ca pañcaniṣkeṇā kārayet ॥ (3)
saptaniṣkaṃ suvarṇena rakṣāsūtraṃ tu kārayet ।
kautukaṃ śātakumbhaṃ ca rājatenāthatantunā ॥ (4)
liṅganāha samaṃ sūtraṃ pīṭhe bere ca dāvahi ।
svalkādhika pramāṇandhyaṃ nāgarāja phaṇānvitam ॥ (5)
tatpṛccha saṃyutaṃ sūtraṃ paṭṭākāra samaṃ tu vā ।
ṣaṭtriṃśatdarbha saṃkhyaiśca nirmitaṃ kūrcamutamam ॥ (6)
dakṣiṇāvarta rūpeṇa granthiṃ kūryādvayaṅgulam ।
agraṃ vedāṅgulaṃ proktaṃ nālamaṣṭadaśāṅgulam ॥ (7)
snigdhaṃ samāśraṃ śyāmaṃ ca śivakumbhe vinikṣipet ।
caturdaśāṅgulaṃ nālaṃ pañcaviṃśati darbhakaiḥ ॥ (8)
agra granthiṃ ca pūrvoktaṃ vardhanyāṃ kūrcamīritam ।
pañcadaśadalairdarbhairagragranthi trayāṅgulam ॥ (9)
trayodaśāṅgulaṃ nālaṃ pramāṇāsadṛśaṃ yutam ।
vidyeśa kalaśānāṃ tu kūrcaṃ kṣiptvā yathākramam ॥ (10)
ūrdhvāgraṃ pauṣṭikaṃ proktaṃ madhograṃ śāntikaṃ bhavet ।
kuryāt kūrca krameṇaiva granthi vā mukhamācaret ॥ (i.15b)
kumbhasya cūtapatrāṇi pañcaviṃśati vinyaset ।
vardhanyāṃ cūtapatrāṇi pañcādaśamiti smṛtam ॥
kalaśeṣvaṣṭapatrāṇi ṣoḍaśāṅgula dairghikam । (2)
athavā dvādaśāṅgulyaṃ sāgaṃ caiva prakīrtitam ॥
kumbhakaṇṭhasamaṃ vṛttaṃ śaravā kṛtivatkṛtam । (3)
tanmadhye randhrasaṃyuktaṃ vinyase sāpidhānakam ॥
nālikeraṃ tu kumbhānāṃ uttamāṅgaṃ tu kathyate । (4)
ḍāḍimīphala madhyaṃ syāt cūtāraṃ bhāramāravet ॥
devājñāṃ ca tathā proktaṃ yathā lābhaṃ prakalpayet । (5)
nālikeraphala proktaṃ śiraḥ kumbhasya satva ca ॥
tanmukhaṃ vaktramevaṃ tu mukhe pūjāprakalpayet । (6)
ekatriṃśaddalaiḥ kūrcaṃ kṛtvā kumbhe nyaset budhaḥ ॥
ekaviṃśati darbhaiśca vardhnanyāṃ vinyasedbudhaḥ । (7)
ekādaśadalaiḥ kuryāt granthirekhāṅgulaṃ bhavet ॥
caturaṅgulamānaṃ hi granthigrasyābhiśasyate । (8)
agragranthi mūlamāna mūrdhādyaṃ padamiṣyate ॥
ityete lakṣaṇaṃ jñeyaṃ lambakūrcasya dhīmatā । (9)
uttamaṃ lambahastaṃ syāt madhyamaṃ daśahastakam ॥
adhamaṃ cāṣṭahastaṃ vā pañcatālasuvistaram । (10)
vastradvayena saṃveṣṭya tatsamaṃ ca dukūlakam ॥
pītaṃ vā sudṛḍhaṃ sūkṣmaṃ śivakumbheṣu yojayet । (i.16a)
navāṣṭa hasta sapto vā śreṣṭha madhyama kanyasam ॥
tadvaddukūla raktādyai vardhanyāṃ veṣṭayedguruḥ । (2)
vidyeśa kalaśānāṃ tu ṣaṭkaraṃ ca pṛthak pṛthak ॥ (3)
kalaśānāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ caturhastaṃ vidhīyate ।
triśaddhastaṃ cottamaṃ syāt madhyamaṃ pañcaviṃśatiḥ ॥ (4)
adhamā viṃśati proktaḥ ṣaṭ tālaṃ ca suvistaram ।
vedyā pratisaraṃ vastraṃ niścidraṃ tena veṣṭayet ॥ (5)
rudrākṣamupavītaṃ coṣṇīṣaṃ cottarīyakam ।
bhasmālayaṃ ca śaivānāṃ pañcamudrāprakīrtitāḥ ॥ (6)
vibhūti phalavardhāraya dvātriṃśatkaṇṭha deśake ।
aṣṭottaraśataṃ proktaṃ muromālā vibhūṣyate ॥ (7)
akṣasūtra prakartavyaṃ saptaviṃśatibhiḥ kramāt ।
ṣaṭ saptaṃ ca haste vā bāṇa bhāgaikavistṛtam ॥ (8)
suślaṣṇaṃ sudṛḍhaṃ sūkṣmaṃ sitaṃ romādi varjitam ।
nirasta cibukenaiva karṇābhyāṃ caiva kārayet ॥ (9)
pucchaṃ vai vāmakarṇe tu veṣṭayettu pradakṣiṇam ।
veṣṭalatvaikamevaṃ tu śeṣaṃ śirasi veṣṭayet ॥ (10)
uṣṇīṣa lakṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ vastralakṣaṇa mucyate ।
yajane sthāpane cāgnikāryo śaivotsave tathā ॥ (i.16b)
uttamaṃ daśahastaṃ syāt madhyamaṃ nava hastakam ।
adhamaṃ cāṣṭahastaṃ syāt pañcatāla suvistaram ॥ (2)
evaṃ vastradvayopeta ācāryo dhārayet kramāt ।
pītaṃ kṣaumaṃ ca raktaṃ syādalābhenya parigṛhet ॥ (3)
agnidagdhaṃ sakeśaṃ ca mūṣike dūṣitaṃ tathā ।
purātanaṃ ca jīrṇaṃ ca vastrapañca vivarjayet ॥ (4)
dagdhaṃ tu rāṣṭranāśāya mūṣikaghnaṃ bhayāna ca ।
romaṃ kleśa pravṛttāya ghananaśāya jīrṇakam ॥ (5)
purātanaṃ tu vastraṃ ca yajamāna vināśanam ।
tasmāddoṣaṃ parityajya dāpanneva vastrakam ॥ (6)
vastralakṣaṇamevoktamuttarīyamathocyate ।
bāṇahastayutaṃ vāpi devahastāya tantu vā ॥ (7)
dvihastamātravistāraṃ baddhayitvā dvipucchakam ।
yajñopavītavardhāyyamuttarīyaṃ viśeṣya ca ॥ (8)
dukūla muttamaṃ proktaṃ vṛkṣasūtraṃ tu madhyamam ।
athavātantu kāryaṃ vā trividhā yogapaṭṭikā ॥ (9)
tritīyaṅgula vistāraṃ tanmānamupavītakam ।
daśāṅgulasamaṃdairghyaṃ pañcāṅgulasuvistaram ॥ (10)
dukūladvayasaṃyuktaṃ golakā kṛtivatkṛtam ।
sitabhasmasamāyuktamācāryāya pradāpayet ॥
hemāṅgulīya kaṭakaṃ kaṭisūtraṃ ca kuṇḍalam । (i.17a)
hemayajñopavītaṃ ca pañcāṅgabhūṣaṇaṃsmṛtam ॥
pavitraṃ pañcaniṣkaṃ syāt kaṭakaṃ dvādaśaṃ bhavet । (2)
niṣkāṣṭaṃ kaṭisūtraṃ ca kuṇḍalaṃ daśaniṣkakam ॥
hemayajñopavītaṃ ca pañcaniṣkī prakīrtitam । (3)
ityevaṃ dhārya vidhivadgurūrāvāhayecchivam ॥
rātrāvāhayeddevaṃ sthiralagnaṃ vināhani । (4)
ādāya kumbhe saṃyojya rātrau yajana homakam ॥
sāyaṃ prātaḥ krameṇaiva kuryādeśika sattamam । (5)
garbhagṛhaṃ tato gatvā sthaṇḍilaṃ caturaśrakam ॥
madhye tu śivakumbhaṃ syāt vardhanīṃ tasya vāmake । (6)
aṣṭakumbhān samādāya paritaḥ sthāpatatkramāt ॥
sakalīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā saṅkalpañca tataḥ param । (7)
puṇyāhaṃ vācayettatra darbhaiḥ puṣpaiḥ paristaret ॥ 270 ॥
lambakūrcaṃ tu saṅgṛhya liṅgasyopari vinyaset । (8)
āsanā pariṇādīni kalpayet pūrvavadguruḥ ॥
nālikeraṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ dāpayeddhūpa dīpakam । (9)
kumbhe calāsanaṃ kalpya śaktyantaṃ tvarcayet kramāt ॥
āsanaṃ mūrti vinyasya darbhanāḍī prakalpayet ।
naivedya dhūpadīpau ca nyāsaṃ kṛtvā tataḥ param ॥ (10)
adhva ṣaṭkaṃ ca vinyasya aṣṭatriṃśat kalānyaset ।
vidyādehaṃ samāvāhya ghaṭe netraṃ prakalpayet ॥ (i.17b)
gurūpadiṣṭamārgeṇa mantramuccārayecchanaiḥ ।
sarvāvaraṇa saṃyuktaṃ hṛdayādi samanvitam ॥ (2)
liṅgasthaṃ devamāvāhya kumbhe saṃyojayecchanaiḥ ।
tāmbūlaṃ gulakhaṇḍaṃ ca kadalīphalameva ca ॥ (3)
pādyamācamanārghyaṃ ca dhūpa dīpaṃ ca dāpayet ।
pīṭhaśaktiṃ samāvāhya vardhanyāṃ ca suyojayet ॥ (4)
anantādīṃśca pūrvādi tattat kumbhe suyojayet ।
gandhapuṣpaisamabhyarcya naivedyādi pradāpayet ॥ (5)
yātrā homaṃ tadagre tu kuṇḍe vā sthaṇḍilopari ।
pūrvokta vidhinā caiva homaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ ॥ (6)
puṣpeṇādāya tanmūrtiṃ śivakumbhe niveśayet ।
yātrādānaṃ tataḥ paścāt gṛhaprītiṃ ca dāpayet ॥ (7)
paricārakamāhūya dīkṣitaṃ vinayānvitam ।
vastreṇācchādya śirasā gandhādyaiḥ saṃvibhūṣya ca ॥ (8)
vāhayitvā tataḥ śambhu kumbhaṃ kṣaumādyalaṅkṛtam ।
vidyeśa kumbhanaśāya dīkṣitairvāhayet kramāt ॥ (9)
ācāryāstraṃ samuccārya sakuśaṃ spṛśayedghaṭam ।
svastighoṣa samāyuktaṃ dīpajvālā samanvitam ॥ (i.18a)
chatracāmarabudaiśca sarvatodya samanvitam ।
śālāṃ gatvā'tha deveśa vedikopari madhyame ॥ (2)
paścimābhimukhaṃ sthāpya sāvadhānena buddhimān ।
devasya vāmapārśve tu vardhanyāṃ vinyasedbudhaḥ ॥ (3)
vidyeśvarāṃśca pūrvādi pradhānābhimukhānyaset ।
bandhane tu pratiṣṭāyāṃ utsave prokṣaṇe tathā ॥ (4)
paścimadvāramevaṃ tu śālāyāṃ tu prakalpayet ।
yasyāṃ diśi bhavedvāraṃ prācīntaṃ parikalpayet ॥ (5)
tadvārābhimukhaṃ vaktraṃ ūrdhvāṃ cābhimukhaṃ kramāt ।
yāgeśaṃ ca śivāgniṃ ca sthāpayetpaścimānanam ॥ (6)
nitya dvārasya pūjāṃ ca hyarcaye deśikottamaḥ ।
hṛdayaṃ vāyudeśe tu śiro vai naiṛto yajet ॥ (7)
śīkhāmīśāna digbhāge kavacaṃ pāvake yajet ।
īśānamaścimāstrasyānnayanaṃ yakṣa pūrvake ॥ (8)
evameva samabhyarcya dhūpadīpa samanvitam ।
dvitīyāvaraṇādīni prāgādiṣu prakalpayet ॥ (9)
toraṇaṃ dvārakumbhāśca lokapālaghaṭānapi ।
maṅgalāṣṭa kuṇḍāni daśāstraṃ pūrvato yajet ॥ (10)
vidyeśvarāṃśca pūrvādi tathā kuṇḍāni homayet ।
divā yāge ca saṃprāpte purataḥ sūryamarcayet ॥ (i.18b)
rātriyāge ca saṃprāpte purataḥ somamarcayet ।
paścāddivakāraṃ pūjya dvārādi tadanantaram ॥ (2)
divā pāpiniśīthinyāṃ pūjārambhaṃ praśasyate ।
homārambhe niśīthinyāṃ sadyodivāsanaṃ dṛte ॥ (3)
praśasya tatonyastra homārambhādimācaret ।
śāntī nandī mahākālaṃ kālarātriṃ ca pūrvake ॥ (4)
indrānyerantare bhānurāgneryāṃ agnipūjanam ।
vidyā bhṛṅgi gaṇeśaṃ ca yamaṃ yāmye prapūjayet ॥ (5)
naiṛtyāṃ vāstu brahmāṇaṃ rākṣasaṃ viṣṇumeva ca ।
nivṛtiṃ vṛṣabhaṃ skandaṃ vāruṇaṃ paścime yajet ॥ (6)
kṣetreśaṃ vāyu vighneśaṃ vāyavyāṃ diśi pūjayet ।
pratiṣṭhā gauri caṇḍeśaṃ ghanadaṃ saumyadeśake ॥ (7)
aiśānyāṃ gurumīśānaṃ brahmāṇaṃ ca prapūjayet । (8)
kubereśānayormadhye astramastreśvari tathā ॥ 300 ॥
maṇṭapārādhanaṃ kuryāt purvoktenaiva vartmanā । (9)
prārthayedgurumīśānaṃ śivakuṇḍāntikaṃ vrajet ॥
kuryādadhyayanaṃ dikṣu śaivamantrān vidikṣu ca । (10)
ṛgvedādhyayanaṃ pūrve yajurvedaṃ tu dakṣiṇe ॥
uttare sāmavedaṃ tu paścime'tharvaṇaṃ tathā । (i.19a)
prāsādamīśakoṇe tu vahnikoṇedaghorakam ॥
mṛtyuñjayaṃ tu naiṛtyāṃ vāyavyāṃ astrameva ca । (2)
evameva prakartavyaṃ viśeṣamadhunocyate ॥
śivasaṅkalpakaṃ caiva tatomā rudrasūktakam ।
kadrudrāya tvamagne ca pūrvabhāge japet budhaḥ ॥ (3)
śrīsūktaṃ pavamānaṃ ca puruṣasūktaṃ tryambakam ।
śāntidvayaṃ japeddhīmān dakṣiṇe tu viśeṣataḥ ॥ (4)
kālasūktaṃ śannavargaṃ viṣṇusūktaṃ tu vāruṇam । (5)
paścime tu japeddhīmān pavamānaṃ hiraṇyakam ॥
devavṛtajyeṣṭhasāmaṃ tathaiva puruṣāvṛtam । (6)
vāmadevyā virūpākṣaṃ cottare tu viśeṣataḥ ॥
agnau śopaniṣadraudraṃ puraṇa nipaṭhe kramāt । (7)
āgamādhyayanaṃ dikṣu vidikṣu ca viśeṣataḥ ॥
bhārataṃ rāmagāyaṃ ca gītamāhātmyamīśvarīm । (8)
ācāryo mūrtipaiḥsārdhaṃ tato homaṃ samācaret ॥
homārambhasya pūrve tu sruksruvau cendhanaṃ tuṣam । (9)
paridhīścaiva darbhāśca samidājyacarūstathā ॥
homadravyāṇi sarvāṇi gandhapuṣpaṃ ca dhūpakam । (10)
dīpaṃ vai dravyapātrāṇi tvaraṇiṃ ca kamaṇḍulum ॥
vardhanīṃ akṣataṃ darvīṃ saṃprāpte homasādhane । (i.19b)
āhṛtya homasambhārānyevaṃ kuṇḍa pratikramāt ॥
pradhāne deśikaṃ caiva tvanyatkuṇḍe tu mūrtipāḥ । (2)
kuṇḍasaṃskāra pūrvaṃ tu śaivāgniṃ sādhayettataḥ ॥
saṃskṛteṣu kuṇḍeṣu hutaṃ bhavati niṣphalam । (3)
tasmātkuṇḍasya saṃskārān kuryānmantreṇa deśikaḥ ॥
dvitālamātramāyāmaṃ kaniṣṭāṅgulināhakam । (4)
avakrānyapramāṇaṃścaiva śyāmavaridhayastathā ॥
purvāgro cottarāgraiśca pūrvādi mekhalopari । (5)
triṃśaddarbhasamāyuktānvitastī dvayadīrghikān ॥
veṇitāvattirānyāpi viṣṭarānadrasaṃyutān । (6)
dakṣavartagrathitān vinyaset madhyamekhale ॥
ṣaṭtriṃśāṅgulamāyāmaṃ granthi pakṣāṅgulasmṛtam । (7)
agraṃ vedāṅgulaṃ proktaṃ darbhai ṣoḍaśabhiryutam ॥
paristaraṇamevaṃ syāt mekhalāyo kṣipedguruḥ । (8)
muṣṭimātraṃ kṣipellājaṃ mekhalordhve caturdiśi ॥
vikarailājapuṣpaiśca kuṇḍaśeṣamaśeṣakam । (9)
brahmāṇamacyutaṃ rudramanantaṃ paridhau yajet ॥
viṣṭareṣu ca lokeśān vajrādīn staraṇe yajet । (10)
evameva krameṇaiva pūjayeddeśikottamaḥ ॥
pradhānakuṇḍaṃ prathamaṃ śivāgniṃ ca samudbhavam । (i.20a)
āgneryāṃ vidhinā kṛtvā tattatkuṇḍeṣu taṃ kṣipet ॥
tatra tatrāsanaṃ kalpya tattanmūrtiṃ prakalpayet । (2)
tattatkuṇḍasthavahnīnāṃ nāmavacmī yathākramam ॥
pradhāne śivavahnīṃ syāt tataḥ prāgādiśaikakam । (3)
pūrvedāhavanīyaṃ syāt agnau vṛddhāgni rucyate ॥
dakṣiṇe dakṣiṇāgniṃ syānnaiṛtyāṃ yauvanānalaḥ । (4)
paścime gārhapatyāgni bālāgnirvāyu koṇake ॥
kevalopyuttarāgniṃ syāt sāmānyāṃ tvīśako iti । (5)
pāvako nāmnanāṃ kṛtvā tattatddhyānaṃ samācaret ॥
pañcavaktrayutaṃ raktaṃ saptajihvāvirājitam । (6)
daśahastaṃ triṇetrañca sarvābharaṇa bhūṣitam ॥
raktavastraparīdhānaṃ paṅkajopari saṃsthitam । (7)
baddhapadmāsanāsīnaṃ mekhalātriya pādayuk ॥
śūlaṃ vajraṃ ca khaḍgaṃ ca śaktiṃ ca tṛṇi dakṣiṇe । (8)
gadā daṇḍau ca pāśaṃ ca padma cakraṃ ca vāmake ॥
ūrdhvavaktre sṛjihvā śeṣaḥ prāgāditaḥ kramāt । (9)
svāhā devi svadhā caiva dakṣyaṇye vāmano nyaset ॥
śaivāgni dhyāna muddiṣṭaṃ śiva kuṇḍe prakīrtitam । (10)
eka vaktraṃ triṇetrañca trimekhala tripādakam ॥ (i.20b)
akṣamālā triśūlaṃ ca srucaṃ vai dakṣiṇe kare ।
aṅkuśaṃ ḍamaruṃ caiva sṛcaṃ vai vāma hastake ॥ (2)
raktavarṇaṃ sukhāsīnaṃ pitavastreṇa veṣṭitam ।
dhyāyedāhavanīyāgniṃ pūrvasyāṃ kuṇḍa madhyame ॥ (3)
caturbhujaṃ triṇetraṃ ca hyekavaktraṃ jaṭādharam ।
triśūlaṃ paraśuṃ caiva abhayaṃ varadānvitam ॥
śvetābhaṃ vṛṣabhārūḍhaṃ vṛdhāgniṃ pāvake yajet । (4)
dvimukhaṃ svarṇavarṇābhaṃ tripādaṃ saptahastakam ॥
hiraṇyādi catasrañca jihvā dakṣiṇa vaktrake । (5)
suprabhādi trayojihvā vāma vaktre tu saṃsthitāḥ ॥
śaktimagniṃ sruvaṃ caiva sruk savye ghṛtapātrakam । (6)
tomaraṃ paraśuṃ vāme tvajavāhana saṃyutam ॥
evaṃ dhyātvā dakṣiṇāgniṃ nyaseddakṣiṇa kuṇḍake । (7)
triṇetraṃ pañcavadanaṃ raktāṅgaṃ daśahastakam ॥
śūlaṃ paraśu khaḍgaṃ cābhayavaktrau ca dakṣiṇe । (8)
ṭaṅkapāśābja cakrāṇi varadaṃ caiva vāmake ॥
hiraṇyā kanakā sadyetvīśetveśeṣamevakam । (9)
yauvanāgni vṛṣārūḍhaṃ naiṛtyāṃ diśivinyaset ॥
raktāṅgaṃ raktavastrāḍhyaṃ varadābhaya saṃyutam । (10)
sruksruvau cordhvahastau tu muktābharaṇabhūṣitam ॥
saptajihvā samāyuktaṃ eka vaktraṃ jaṭādharam । (11)
aśvārūḍhaṃ gārhapatyaṃ dhyāyet paścima kuṇḍake ॥
dvimukhaṃ caika hṛdayaṃ catuśrotraṃ dvināsikam ।
catuśṛṅgaṃ vṛṣārūḍhaṃ bālāditya samaprabham ॥ (i.21a)
sruksruvau cākṣamālāṃ ca śaktiṃ dakṣiṇa pārśvake ।
tomaram vyajanaṃ caiva ghṛtapātraṃ tu vāmake ॥ (2)
bālāgnirūpamevaṃ tu vinyasedvāyu kuṇḍake ।
dvinetraṃ dvibhujaṃ caiva varadābhaya saṃyutam ॥ (3)
śuklavastra samāyuktaṃ śvetaraktasamadvitim ।
sarvālaṅkāra saṃyuktaṃ paṅkajāsana saṃsthitam ॥ (4)
kevalāgnimiti dhyātvā hyuttare kuṇḍa madhyame ।
dvimukhaṃ vedaśṛṅgaṃ ca hyabhayaṃ śakti sruksruvau ॥
varadaṃ khaḍgamanalaṃ vahantaṃ savya vāmake । (5)
sāmānyāgnisvarūpaṃ tu vinyasetvīśa kuṇḍake ॥
pūrvoktenaiva mārgeṇa tattatkuṇḍeṣu deśikaḥ । (6)
dhyātvā vahnipradhāne tu yajecchambhumānanyadhiḥ ॥ 350 ॥
pradhāne cāsanaṃ kalpya mūrtidhyānaṃ ca vinyaset । (7)
pañcavaktraṃ daśabhujaṃ gokṣīra dhavaḻaprabham ॥
sarvasiddhipradaṃ dhyāyet vāsinaṃ paścimānanam । (8)
śivāgnau śivamāvāhya nityavat pūjayecchivam ॥
pañcāvaraṇa saṃyuktaṃ pādyadhūpasamanvitam । (9)
evaṃ saṃpūjayetpūrvaṃ kṣmāmūrtyādi pūjayet ॥
kṣmāvahni yajamānājjala vāyu niśākaraḥ । (10)
vyomānta mūrtyaścāṣṭau kuṇḍānāṃ mūrtidhārakāḥ ॥
bhavaścograśca śarvaśca mahādevaśca rudrakaḥ ।
bhīmaḥ paśupatiścaiva tvīśānāṃ cāṣṭamūrtayaḥ ॥ (11)
bhavaśca pūrvakuṇḍe tvāgneryāṃ mugrameva ca ।
śarvaśca dakṣiṇe kuṇḍe mahādevastu naiṛte ॥ (12)
rudraśca paścime bhāge bhīme vai vāyugocare ।
paśupatiścottare kuṇḍe tvaiśānyāṃ mīśayeva ca ॥ (i.21b)
triṇetrāścaturbhujāścaiva jaṭāmakuṭa dhāriṇaḥ ।
sarvābharaṇa saṃpūrṇa kuṇḍānāmūrtidhāsmṛtāḥ ॥ (2)
itthaṃ ca mūrtipā proktāḥ mūrtīśānamathocyate ।
puruṣādyā dikṣukuṇḍe vidikṣu hṛdayādikān ॥ (3)
īśānaṃ netramastraṃ ca mulaṃ caiva pradhānake ।
īśānaṃ sphaṭikābhāsaṃ śivakuṇḍe yajedguruḥ ॥ (4)
puruṣaṃ pūrvakuṇḍe tu pītavarṇa samanvitam ।
aghoramañjanābhāsaṃ dhyāyeddakṣiṇakuṇḍake ॥
saumyakuṇḍe yajedvāraṃ raktavarṇasamanvitam । (5)
sadyaṃ paścimakuṇḍe tu śvetavarṇa samadvitim ॥
hṛdivahniramedhyāya striṇetrāṃ ca caturbhujā । (6)
varadābhaya saṃyuktā ṭaṅkaśūlasamanvitāḥ ॥
sarvābharaṇasaṃpūrṇā śvetavastrānu dhāriṇaḥ । (7)
hṛdayaṃ śveta varṇābhaṃ agnikuṇḍe yajecchubham ॥
īśakuṇḍe nyasedvidvān pītābhaṃ ca śīrocyate । (8)
śikhāṃ naiṛti digbhāge śyāmavarṇa samanvitam ॥
kavacaṃ dhūmravarṇābhaṃ vāyu kuṇḍa nyasedbudhaḥ । (9)
netraṃ śyāmalākāraṃ astraṃ vai kṛṣṇavarṇakam ॥
dvayaṃ ca śiva kuṇḍe tu vinyaseddeśikottamaḥ ।
hṛdayādyastraparyanta triṇetrāśca caturbhujāḥ ॥ (10)
pāśāṅkuśavarābhīti pītāmbaradharālame ।
sadāśivasvarūpaṃ tu jñātavya deśikena tu ॥ (11)
tatkuṇḍe ime nyasya stadbāhye vasavastathā ।
stadbāhyemarutaścaiva lokapālāśca tadbahi ॥ (i.22a)
lokapālai vinā cetu madhyamo homamīritaḥ ।
marudbhivasubhīhīnāsuraitekannyasammataṃ ॥ (2)
kuṇḍādi devatāḥ sarvā kuṇḍa madhye nyasedguruḥ ।
pādyādi dhūpaparyantaṃ arcayedvidhinā tataḥ ॥
hastekamānakuṇḍe tu juhuyātsarpiṣā kramāt । (3)
palāśa carulājaiśca homaṃ kuryātpradhānake ॥
anyāni samidādīni pañcaviṃśati saṅkhyayā । (4)
śivakuṇḍe huneddevaṃ ṛtvik kuṇḍeṣu taḥ param ॥
pūrvādīśāna paryantaṃ yaje brahmaṣaḍaṅgakān । (5)
pratikuṇḍa ghṛtaṃ hutvā pañcāśatdviśatānikam ॥
tadardha samidhā hutvā caruṃ tatsaṅkhyayā tathā । (6)
tattat kuṇḍokta dravyeṇa hutvā pūrvokta saṅkhyayā ॥
anyadravyāṇi sarvāṇi yathālābhaṃ hunetkramāt । (7)
kapilāyāṃ ghṛtaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ miśrayonyāśca madhyamam ॥
meṣājyaṃ kanyasaṃ proktaṃ māhiṣaṃ varjayeddhṛtam । (8)
sahasraṃ vā tripādaṃ vā sahasrārdhamathāpi vā ॥
uttamādi krameṇa pradhāne homayedguruḥ । (9)
dikvidikṣu ca kuṇḍeṣu pañcabrahmaṣaḍaṅgakān ॥
pradhāna homasaṅkhyāya hyardhārdhaṃ homamācaret ।
prokterādi devānāṃ homamāvaraṇātmanām ॥ (10)
pradhānakuṇḍaṃ jñeyaṃ hi śatāṃśaṃ homasaṅkhyayā ।
catukhāri ca bhāraṃ ca khāridvau saṅgṛhe ghṛtam ॥ (11)
pratikālaṃ hunedvidvān uttamādi krameṇa tu ।
dvigavya grāhi tarjanyāṃ bindurityabidhīyate ॥ (i.22b)
binduṣaṭkaṃ ca sarṣārdhaṃ tadvimaṃ karṣakaṃ bhavet ।
karṣadvayaṃ tu muṣṭiṃ syāt tadvayaṃ prasṛtirbhavet ॥ (2)
tadvayaṃ kuḍupaṃ proktaṃ tadvayaṃ dhāraṇaṃ bhavet ।
dhāraṇādvitayaṃ prastaṃ dvātriṃśāhuti prastakam ॥ (3)
karṣapūrṇa ghṛteṇaiva ekāhutimathācaret ।
kuñjasyavā'tha māṣasya viṃśatidhāraṇa smṛtā ॥ (4)
dhāraṇāṣṭau phalaṃvā'tha muṣṭirvadha bhavedihi ।
uktānyatara cā kṛṣṇaṃ kuḍumbarīcakṣate ॥ (5)
prasthaṃ kṛttacatuṣkaṃ syāt taccatuṣkaṃ tu cāḍhakaṃ ।
navadhānyādikān sarvān ḍakena pṛthak pṛthak ॥ (6)
dviprastaṃ sarṣapaṃ proktaṃ tilamañjalimātrataḥ ।
evameva pramāṇena pratikālaṃ hunet kramāt ॥ (7)
ghṛtena vyāhṛdi proktaṃ pañcapakṣamihocyate ।
pūrve vā'havanīyāgni dakṣiṇāgnistu dakṣiṇe ॥ (8)
paścime gārhapatyāgni kevalāgnistu cottare ।
īśāne tu śivāgnistu pañcāgniriti kīrtitaḥ ॥
pradhāne vyomamūrtiṃ syāt vāyumūrtiśca pūrvake । (9)
dakṣiṇe vahnimūrtiṃ syājjalamūrtiśca saumyake ॥
kṣmāmūrti paścime kuṇḍe kuṇḍānāmūrtidhārakāḥ । (10)
tattat mūrtīśvarān vacmī pradhānāt paścimāntakam ॥
pañcapakṣe ca mūrtīnāṃ rūpadhyānamihocyate । (i.23a)
īśe sadāśiva proktaṃ rūpadhyānaṃ ca pūrvavat ॥
pañcāvaraṇa mārgeṇa pūjaye deśikottamaḥ । (2)
pūrvakuṇḍe trinayanaṃ ekavaktraṃ smitānanam ॥
vyāghracarma parīdhānaṃ nāgayajñopavītinam । (3)
śuddhasphaṭikasaṅkāśaṃ candrārdhakṛtaśekharam ॥
akṣamālāṃ triśūlaṃ ca khaḍgabhīti ca savyake । (4)
bāṇaṃ kamaṇḍuluṃ cābjaṃ varaṃ vāme dadhānakam ॥
itthaṃ dhyātveśvaraṃ vidvān sarvābharaṇa bhūṣitam । (5)
rudraṃ dakṣiṇa deśe tu bhasmoddhūlita vigrahaḥ ॥
caturbhujaṃ triṇetraṃ ca jaṭāmakuṭamaṇḍitaḥ । (6)
māṇikkāneka varṇābhaṃ pītavastreṇa veṣṭitam ॥
savye vyākhyānamanalaṃ vāme pustakaśūladhṛk । (7)
dhyāyemuttara kuṇḍe tu viṣṇuṃ vai nīlasannibham ॥
ekavaktraṃ dvinayanaṃ kirīṭamakuṭojvalam । (8)
pītāmbaradharaṃ śaṅkhaṃ gadācakrābjadhāriṇam ॥
sarvalakṣaṇa saṃyuktaṃ muktābharaṇa bhūṣitam । (9)
brahmāṇaṃ paścime kuṇḍe mauñjīdarbhairalaṅkṛtam ॥
caturbhujaṃ caturvaktraṃ prativaktraṃ dvilocanam । (10)
pītavastraṃ suvadanaṃ citravastrasamanvitam ॥ 400 ॥
sarvāvayava saṃpūrṇaṃ sṛgdaṇḍākṣa kamaṇḍalum ।
evaṃ mūrtīśvarāḥ proktāḥ mantraiścevadhunocyate ॥ (i.23b)
pūrve puruṣasṛkproktaṃ yāmye ghoraśirastathā ।
pāśaṃ sadyakavacaṃ saumye vāmaśikhāmatā ॥ (2)
netrāstreśānadevā syurarcya prādhānya kuṇḍake ।
purvādi kuṇḍe dau devān varṇadhyānaṃ ca pūrvavat ॥ (3)
tattat kuṇḍe samāvāhya tattanmantreṇa homayet ।
evaṃ pañcavidhaṃ proktaṃ śeṣaṃ pūrvavadācaret ॥ (4)
kalpayedyeka kuṇḍaṃ cedyathā vittānusārataḥ ।
homaṃ kuryādyathoktena brahmāṅgānāṃ daśāṃśakam ॥ (5)
ityevaṃ trividhaṃ proktaṃ samidravyamihocyate ।
vāruṇeśāna paryantaṃ jihvāstānaṃ prakīrtitam ॥ (6)
hiraṇyāyaṃ samiddhomaṃ madhudugdhaṃ tathaiva ca ।
kanakāyāṃ ghṛtaṃ proktaṃ dadhīcaiva ca śarkarā ॥ (7)
raktāyāṃ tu caruṃ hutvā phalāpūpaṃ tathaiva ca ।
lājaṃ hutvā tu kṛṣṇāyāṃ nīvāraṃ caṇakameva ca ॥ (8)
suprabhāyāṃ hunesattu niṣpāvaṃ tvāḍhakaṃ tathā ।
yavaṃ hutvā'tiraktāyāṃ veṇuṃ vai mudgameva ca ॥ (9)
bahurūpe tilaṃ māṣaṃ siddhārthaṃ gośamantathā ।
dravyāṇyanyāni sarvāṇi kanakāyā hunet kramāt ॥ (10)
karṣapūrṇena sarpistu samiddho dvādaśāṅgulāḥ ।
kāśamṛttāmbujāstālī saṅgṛhedvidhimānataḥ ॥ (11)
saptāṣṭa taṇḍulaṃgrāhya deśikastu vacedgurum ।
vidhipakvaṃ tadanyadvāgrāsa mātreṇa saṃhunet ॥ (i.24a)
vīthyā pakvaṃ caruṃ homaṃ kuryāsaṃvāda saṃskṛtiḥ ।
inḍānādi kṣipedagnau kubjahastena lājakam ॥ (2)
pṛthukaṃ dhanukaṃ saktuṃ muṣṭimātreṇa homayet ।
godhūmaṃ vaiṇavaṃ caiva nīvāraṃ śaktimātrataḥ ॥ (3)
niṣpāvaṃ nabhiḥ proktaṃ caṇaṃ daśabhireva ca ।
pañcāṅgulyā huneddhīmān āḍhakaṃ rājamāṣakam ॥
kulasthaṃ māṣa mudgaṃ ca priyaṅguśyāmakaṃ tathā । (4)
śakti mātreṇa hutvā tu śaktyardhaṃ yavasarṣapam ॥
hutvā tilaṃ tu mudgaṃ ca dravyānte vyāhṛtiṃ hunet । (5)
pañcānnārthāya caivoktaṃ āḍhakaṃ ca pṛthak pṛthak ॥
tattadravyoktamānena taṇḍulañca kramāt pacet । (6)
agnaudaghrāhuneddhīmān śuddhānnaṃ mudgamannakam ॥ (7)
kṣīrānnaṃ kṛsarānnaṃ ca gulānnaṃ cākṣamātrataḥ ।
svapramāṇanyapūpāni catuḥ kṛtvānyathā hunet ॥ (8)
payo dadhi madhu kṣīra krūrekṣu janmavastūni ।
sruva pūrṇāhutiṃ hutvā phunnāga phalavadgulaṃ ॥ (9)
ikṣu parva vidhāne kadalyardhena homayet ।
hutvā cūtaṃ svapramāṇaṃ bhitvā ṣaunnālikerakam ॥ (10)
likucaṃ svapramāṇena kuṇḍakatya visṛjya ca ।
triṃśadaṃśena bhāgena bhitvā tu panasaṃ hunet ॥
kālaṃ sūraṇaṃ bālaṃ padmakandaṃ ca nīlakam । (i.24b)
vibhīti phalamātreṇa kundaṃ bhitvā tu homayet ॥
candanaṃ caṇamātreṇa tato mṛgamadadvayam । (2)
candra kuṅkuma kāśmīraṃ kastūri kṛṣṇagandhakam ॥
māṣava guggulu dhūpaṃ badarīphalavaddhunet । (3)
uśīra jalapiṅgaṃ ca marutāntaṃ na tālaka ॥
dvayaṅgulalatānāṃ ca śrīvatraṃ pañcapuṣpakam । (4)
jātīphalaṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ yelālavaṅgasaṃyutam ॥
kareṇa juhuyāddravya sruvapūrṇenavāhutim । (5)
srucāvaṭena saṃpūrṇa pūrṇāhutimathācaret ॥
palāśaṃ sadyamantreṇa mūlenājyaṃ tu homayet । (6)
aghoreṇa caruṃ hutvā lājaṃ tatpuruṣena tu ॥ (7)
saktuṃ ca vāmamantreṇa tvīśānayanakaṃ hunet ।
samiddravyāni sarvāṇi pūrvaṃ hutvā pradhānake ॥ (8)
paścāt pūrvādi kuṇḍeṣu homaśeṣaṃ tato hunet ।
hutvā tvīśe tu niṣpāvaṃ apāmārgaṃ pradhānake ॥ 430 ॥ (9)
pūrveśvatthaṃ godhūmaṃ yāmye tilamudumbaraṃ ।
āḍhakaṃ ca vaṭaṃ saumye plakṣe mudgaṃ ca paścime ॥ (10)
śiraṃ ca sarṣapaṃ vahnau tvaiśānyāṃmudgamāṣakam ।
khādiraṃ śāli naiṛtyāṃ vāyavyā caṇa bilvakam ॥ (i.25a)
netrasyāmraṃ ca nīvāraṃ śiva kuṇḍe hunedbudhaḥ ।
atrasya vaiṇavaṃ dūrvā tathā cā śivakuṇḍake ॥ (2)
dikvidikṣu ca kuṇḍeṣu tattanmantreṇa homayet ।
samidhā ca caruṃ lājān digdravyaṃ ca samānakam ॥
anyadravyāṇi sarvāṇi nyune vāpyadhiko'pi vā । (3)
yathā sambhavamānena homayeddeśikottamaḥ ॥
rānye yavadhānyaṃ ca mūlena rājamāṣakam । (4)
śikhāyā śyāmakaṃ hutvā priyaṅguṃ ca gulasthakam ॥
aghoreṇa tu pañcānnmapūpaṃ śirasā hunet । (5)
netreṇa pāyasaṃ hutvā dugdhamīśena homayet ॥
dadhi tatpuruṣenaiva madhuṃ vai vāmamātrataḥ । (6)
gulaṃ ca ghoramantreṇa mūle vaikṣudaṇḍakam ॥
īśānena phalādīni kandādi mūla mantrataḥ । (7)
mūlena candanaṃ caiva kastūri kṛṣṇagandhakam ॥
gorocanaṃ ca kāśmīraṃ kuṅkumaṃ mṛganābhikam । (8)
himatoyaṃ ca karpūraṃ sarvaṃ ca juhuyātkramāt ॥
bījamukhyaiśca śrīberamuśiraṃ maru guggulam । (9)
daśākṣareṇa puṣpāṇi bilva pāśupatena vai ॥
aghoreṇa tu tāmbūlaṃ vāsanā dravya saṃyutam । (10)
anyāni homayogyāni mūlenaiva tu homayet ॥
dravyaṃ prati viśeṣeṇa vyāhṛtiṃ ca punaḥ punaḥ । (i.25b)
pūrvādi vāyukuṇḍāntaṃ homakarma samarpya ca ॥
pūrṇāhutiṃ tataḥ kṛtvā vyāhṛtyā ca hunet kramāt । (2)
dhūpa dīpau dadetpaścāt tadante mekhalātraye ॥
tatatsvanāma mantreṇa lokeśādi baliṃ kṣipet ।
nāḍīsandhāna mārgeṇa tattatkuṇḍādi devatān ॥ (3)
akṣataṃ ca puṣpaṃ ca gṛhītvā utthujaṃ kramāt ।
śivakuṇḍagāgnistha śive saṃyojayetparam ॥ (4)
sarvatodyasamāyuktaṃ vedaghoṣa samanvitam ।
mahā pūrṇāhutiṃ hutvā vyāhṛtyā juhuyāttataḥ ॥ (5)
pradarśya dhūpadīpādīn antarbāhyabaliṃ dadet ।
śrotrābhivandanaṃ kṛtvā tvahaṃ rakṣopadetvayā ॥ (6)
sakalīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā japaṃ kṛtvā samarpayet ।
tattat kuṇḍagatāgniśca tattat kuṇḍe tu rakṣayet ॥ (7)
utthāne tvāsanā krodhe kṣudheniṣṭīvane kramāt । (8)
vāyucābharayet pannabhāṣaṇe cāpya dīkṣitaiḥ ॥
śyoṣmādgāre ca santāpe kriyavismṛti sambhave । (9)
yantrokte mantrakārye tu vyāhṛtyāhuti mācaret ॥
tato kumbhāntikaṃ gatvā vedyā dakṣiṇa bhāgataḥ । (10)
puṣpāñjalyā pūjayitvā tatvatritaya mantrataḥ ॥
dhūpa dīpau dadetpaścāt mahānaivedyamācaret ।
caturhastaṃ tadāpātraṃ catuṣpātraṃ tu prastakam ॥ (i.26a)
āḍhakaṃ tu catuṣṣkaṃ syāddroṇaṃ tasya catuṣkakam ।
dvidroṇadaṣṭadroṇaṃ ca nityameva dvitīyate ॥ (2)
naimityadaṣṭa droṇaṃ tu bhāraṃ dvādaśāntakaṃ ।
prārthitaṃ yaistu saṃkhyābhiḥ tai saṃkhyābhistu kāmyakam ॥ (3)
evaṃ krameṇa naivedyaṃ dāpayeddeśikottamaḥ ।
haviścaṣṭavidhaṃ deyaṃ pāyasaṃ śuddhamannakam ॥ (4)
gulānnaṃ kṛsarānnaṃ ca dadhyānnaṃ mudgamannakam ।
pratidravyeṇa saṃyuktaṃ āḍhakadvaya taṇḍulam ॥ (5)
aneka vyañjanairyuktamapūpādi samanvitam ।
vrīhireka phalaṃ caiva śālirdaśa guṇo bhavet ॥ (6)
priyaṅgu śataguṇo proktaṃ nīvāraṃ tu sahasrakam ।
godhūmaṃ tu bhavellakṣa anantaṃ vaiṇavaṃ bhavet ॥ (7)
bhakṣya bhojyaṃ ca veyaṃ ca lehya śoṣyaṃ ca pañcadhā ।
nānāphalaiśca saṃyuktaimapūpaiśca samanvitam ॥ (8)
sthālikāṃ kṣālanaṃ kṛtvā naivedyaṃ ca prapūjayet ।
vyañjanairāvṛtaṃ caiva pūrayedannakopari ॥ (9)
pradhāya śuddhavanyatreṇa pācako paricārayet ।
vādyaghoṣa samāyuktaṃ yāgaśālāṃ praveśayet ॥ (10)
śivāgre sthāpayitvā tu ambunā prokṣayettataḥ ।
devasyatveti mantreṇa naivedyaṃ dāpayettataḥ ॥ (i.26b)
dhūpadīpādi vyañjanānta hyupacārān samācaret । (2)
mantrapuṣpādinābhyarcya dadyāddeśika bhasma ca ॥
trikālaṃ cānnaliṅgaṃ ca nityotsavādi karma ca ।
nityahomādi kāryaṃ ca bhāgaśālāya niveśanam ॥ (3)
sarvaṃ nityotsavaṃ kuryācchivayāgasya maṇṭape ।
tīrtha nemittikaṃ caiva śivayāge vivarjayet ॥ (4)
akurādyabhiṣekāntaṃ madhyāhne bhojayedvijān ।
sahasrādyayuta paryantaṃ yathāvittānusārataḥ ॥ (5)
sarvadoṣa nivṛtyarthaṃ kuryā brāhmaṇa bhojanam ।
bhaktitaḥ saptarātraṃ vā pañcarātra trirātrakam ॥ (6)
ekarātramathosadya kuryāddevādhivāsanam ।
hotavyaṃ pratyahaṃ svasvamantreṇaiva yajecchivam ॥ (7)
evamevavidhānena sāyaṃ prātadine dine ।
ityevaṃ pūjayedrātrau prabhāte vimale sati ॥ (8)
snātvā gurūrvidhānena nityakarmasamāpayet ।
yāgaśālāṃ praviśyātha ṛtvibhiḥ parivārakaiḥ ॥ (9)
vighnarājaṃ ca saṃpūjya saṅkalpaṃ ca tataḥ param ।
puṇyāhaṃ pañcagavyaṃ ca kumbhaṃ saṃsthāpayedguruḥ ॥
sakalīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā bhūtaśuddhādikaṃ caret । (10)
dvārādi gurupūjāntaṃ pūrvavat pūjayet kramāt ॥
……………śivakumpādyādi sthāpayedguruḥ । (i.27a)
nirmālyaṃ ca visṛjyātha kalpayesthiramāsanam ॥
mūrtidhyānaṃ ca vinyasya mūlenāvāhya deśikaḥ । (2)
pādyādi dhūpadīpau ca tāmbūlādi nivedayet ॥
darpaṇevā'tha kūrce vā kalpayeccalamāsanam ।
tailādi kumbhaparyantaṃ hyabhiṣekaṃ samarpya ca ॥ (3)
tanmūrtiṃ yojayetkumbhe gandha mālyaiśca dhārayet ।
mantrodanaṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ dāpayeddhūpa dīpadhṛk ॥ (4)
hemaṃ kuryādvidhānena paścānte vedyamācaret ।
pūrvoktavidhimārgeṇa upacārān samarpya ca ॥ (5)
puṇyāhamabhiṣekaṃ ca dvārādyarcana homakān ।
dvikāle kārayedeva manyakāle vidhīyate ॥ (6)
nirmālyaṃ ca visarjāya naivedya dhūpadīpayuk ।
ahanyahani saṃpūjya nirdiṣṭadivasā vidhi ॥ (7)
paścādgarbhagṛhaṃ prāpya sāmānyayajanaṃ kuru ।
aṣṭabandhanakāle tu nandyāvartavihīnake ॥ (8)
sthalakarmaṇi viśyiṣṭe sudhākarmavihīnake ।
kṣaṇakāle gṛhītvā tu punaḥ sandhānamācaret ॥ (9)
śāntikumbhaṃ samāprokṣya pūjayeddeśikottamaḥ ।
nirdiṣṭadivasāt pūrve sāyāhomāvasānake ॥
mūlaliṅgāntikaṃ gatvā śodhayeddeśikottamaḥ । (10)
pīṭhāṃśaṃ ṣoḍaśaṃ kṛtvā caikāṃśaṃ navaratnakam ॥
daśāṃśaṃ saikatairyuktaṃ pañcāṃśaṃ bandhayetkramāt । (11)
trayaṅgulapramāṇena svaṇena caturaśrakam ॥
vyalikha śivabījena madhye randhravaṭe kṣipet । (i.27b)
māṇikkamadhyamenyasya śakremarakataṃ nyaset ॥
indranīlaṃ tu yāmyāyāṃ sphāṭikaṃ vāruṇe bhavet । (2)
vajraṃ tu saumyadigbhāge vaiḍūryaṃ vahnigocare ॥
mauktikaṃ naiṛte deśe padmarāgaṃ ca mārute । (3)
pravālamīśadeśe tu catuḥsaṃskārakānyaset ॥
mūlamantreṇa saṃpūjya ṣaḍutthāsanamarcayet । (4)
mukulīṃ padmamudrāṃ ca śodhaniṃ ca pradarśayet ॥
madhyādīśāna paryantaṃ vinyasenmūlamantrataḥ । (5)
pūrayetsaikavitaṃ liṅgaṃ pīṭhaṃ caiva dṛḍhīkṛtam ॥
prāsadasyāgrake yāmye pāvake vānya deśake । (6)
gomayenopalipyātha caturaśradvayaṃ tataḥ ॥
sthaṇḍilaṃ tatra kartavyaṃ aṣṭapatraṃ sakarṇikam । (7)
navapātrasamāyuktamathavā caikapātrakam ॥
puṇyāhaṃ vācayettatra sarva dravyāṇi prokṣayet । (8)
tripañcadivasasthānabhaṅgaṃ cecchivaliṅgake ॥
tatpūjādikaṃ sarvaṃ pūjayettu śivādhvare ।
mūlaliṅge viśeṣeṇa śāntihoma samanvitam ॥ (9)
naivaidyaṃ gandhapuṣpaṃ ca dhūpadīpa samanvitam ।
aṣṭadravyāṇi saṃpūjya aṣṭavidyeśvarān kramāt ॥ (10)
prathamaṃ pañcamūrtiṃ ca dravyaṃ ca trimūrtinā ।
etanmūlamantreṇa dravyāṇyevābhimantrayet ॥
arcayitvā yathoktena kartavyaṃ sthaṇḍilāntakam । (11)
ulūkhalaṃ musalaṃ caivaprokṣayecchuddhavāriṇā ॥
ṣaḍutthāsanamabhyarcya tasyopari nidhāpayet । (i.28a)
lūkhalaṃ brahmadaivatyaṃ musalaṃ viṣṇu daivatam ॥
vastradarbhairalaṅkṛtya lūkhalaṃ sa musalasya ca । (2)
gandhapuṣpādinābhyarcya dhūpadīpādikaṃ dadet ॥
āhūya paricārau dvau soṣṇīṣau cottarīyakaṃ । (3)
lūkhalasya tu tanmadhye tadravyaṃ nikṣipedguruḥ ॥ 500 ॥
musalaṃ ca gṛhītvātha tāḍayet sudṛḍhaṃ punaḥ । (4)
samyak sukuṭya musalaiḥ supkvaṃ vīkṣya buddhimān ॥
pūjāṃśaṃ ṣoḍaśaṃ kṛtyā prāyaścittaṃ hunet kramāt । (5)
śilpisparśādidoṣāya sthāpayecchāntikumbhakam ॥
prajvālya śiravahnau tu śāntimantraṃ śataṃ hunet । (6)
darbhamūle tu valmīke kṣetre mṛdgrāhyakarkaṭe ॥ (7)
vṛṣaḥ śṛṅge bilvamūle nadī mṛdgajantike ।
aṣṭamṛdgrāhyate dhīmān bhāṇḍa madhye nidhāpayet ॥ (8)
aśvatthamāmbū jambū ca vaṭamaudumbaraṃ tathā ।
pañcatvak ca samākhātamekabhāṇḍa nidhāya ca ॥
palāśodumbarāśvattha jambūnyagrodha pallavam । (9)
pañcapallavamākhyātaṃ tatpātre nidhāpayet ॥
bilvakapitthaniguṇḍi saralī vāruṇaṃ tathā । (10)
pañcabilvamitikhyāta pātramadhye vinikṣipet ॥
vidhivat pañcagavyaṃ ca sthāpayeddeśikottamaḥ । (11)
vrīhīṃtadagre nikṣipya tilataṇḍula saṃyutam ॥
tattatkumbhānyaseddhīmān cūtapallavasaṃyutam । (i.28b)
ṣaḍūtthāsanamabhyarcya saṃhitāpūjayet kramāt ॥
vyomavyāpi japettatra dhūpadīpau dadedguruḥ । (2)
nikṣipet taṇḍulaṃ pātre kautukadvaya saṃyutam ॥
bhasmaṃ sitaṃ nālikeraṃ ca tāmbūlena samanvitam । (3)
krameṇa dravyamabhyarcya kautukena sukinyaset ॥
saṃpādaṃ ca hunedvidvān vādya ghoṣa samanvitam । (4)
brahmaghoṣasamāyuktaṃ śivaliṅgāntikaṃ vrajet ॥
tailābhyaṅgaṃ tato devakuryāddhīmān viśeṣataḥ । (5)
pañcagavyena saṃsnāpya liṅgapīṭhāntaraṃ kramāt ॥
aṣṭamṛtsalilaiścaiva kaṣāyaiścāntikumbhakaiḥ । (6)
pañcapallavabilvaiśca pratyekamabhiṣecayet ॥ (7)
candanāvailipyātha rakṣāsūtraṃ prabandhayet ।
evaṃ rātrau prakartavyaṃ prabhāte snānamācaret ॥ (8)
garbhagṛhaṃ tataḥ prāpya puṇyāhaṃ prokṣaṇaṃ kramāt ।
vyapohyoṣata puṣpāṇi kautukaṃ vastra saṃyutam ॥ (9)
abhiṣekaṃ tataḥ kuryādyupacārairprakalpayet ।
adhvaṣaṭkaṃ ca vinyasya hyaṣṭatriṃśatkalānyaset ॥ (10)
mantranyāsaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā dhūpadīpādikanyajet ।
yāgaśālāṃ punaḥ prāpya ṛtvigbhiparicārakaiḥ ॥ (i.29a)
yāgapūjā prakāreṇa śivaṃ sāṅgaṃ prapūjayet । (2)
agniṃ prajvālya saṃpūjya homaṃ pūrvavadācaret ॥
prāyaścittaṃ purāhutvā jayādi juhuyāttataḥ । (3)
bhūrādiśca śirasā pūrṇāhutvā vidhānataḥ ॥
dhūpadīpau dadet paścādantarbāhyabaliṃ dadet । (4)
śrotrābhivandanaṃ kṛtvā varmaṇā paridhīndahet ॥
viṣṭarāṇi samādāyamukta granthiṃ visarjayet । (5)
darbhastaraṇamānīya sarpiṣā secanaṃ dahet ॥
tadbhasmaṃ gṛhītvā tu dhāraye tu lalāṭake । (6)
sakalīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā japaiḥ paścāt samarcayet ॥
nāḍīsandhānamārgeṇa kuṇḍādehaṃ samācaret । (7)
kumbhe saṃyojayedvidvān dhūpadīpādi saṃyutam ॥ (8)
agniṃ visarjayet paścāt astrakumbhaṃ ca vardhanī ।
dvārapālādi lokeśān visṛje sakalānapi ॥ (9)
sumuhurte samanuprāpte yātrādānaṃ gurāḥ karaiḥ ।
devājñāya gṛhaprītinādāna nandyāviśeṣataḥ ॥ (10)
ge(go)bhūtilahiraṇyājya vāsodhānya gulāni ca ।
raupyaṃ lavaṇamityāhuḥ daśadānānitavai ॥ (i.29b)
kanyādānaṃ copanayanaṃ dānaṃ bhasmaṃ tathaiva ca ।
rudrākṣaṃ śivaliṅgaṃ syādānānyatyāni dāpayet ॥ (2)
ācāryaṃ pūjayet paścāt vastrābharaṇa kuṇḍalaiḥ ।
śayyā ca vāhanaṃ dāsi raktā(tnā)ni vividhāni ca ॥ (3)
sasye pūrṇāṃ vasumatīṃ saṃvatsvāṅgaṃ payasvini ।
etāniguravadyadyāt paścāt tāmbūla dakṣiṇām ॥ (4)
triṃśaniṣkaṃ tu śreṣṭaṃ syād dviṃśaniṣkaṃ tu madhyamaṃ ।
adhamaṃ daśaniṣkaṃ syāt ācāryasya tu dakṣiṇā ॥ (5)
ācāryārdha sādhakāya tadardhaṃ vāstuhemine ।
ṛtvijānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ janaṃ prati pṛthak pṛthak ॥ (6)
dadyadravyantadardhaṃ ca tatsamāśilpi dakṣiṇā ।
tadardhaṃ astrajapāṇāṃ tatsamaṃ vedapāṭhine ॥ (7)
devājñāya samaṃ dadyāt tathā paurāṇikāya ca ।
sthānīnāṃ paricārāṇāṃ yathā śraddhānudakṣiṇā ॥ (8)
gītavādyaka nṛttanāṃ dāsīnāṃ tu tathaiva ca ।
anyeṣāṃ śivabhaktānāṃ prītiṃkuryādviśeṣataḥ ॥ (9)
bhūridānaṃ viśeṣeṇa krameṇa kuru te guruḥ ।
ācaryamāna santuṣṭiḥ devasantoṣa kāraṇam ॥
ācāryaḥ manasaḥ kopaḥ devatā kopameva ca । (10)
tasmāt sarvaṃ prayatnena hyācāryāṃ toṣayet prabhuḥ ॥
samidbhāraṃ ca sahasrāṇi ghṛtakumbha daśāni ca । (11)
dakṣiṇā hīna mātreṇa kartunāśāya kalpayet ॥
ācāryo mantrahīnaṃ syāt kriyāhīnaṃ tu śilpinam । (i.30a)
dātāraṃ dravyahīnaṃ syāt trivargaṃ kulanāśanam ॥
evaṃ tu vidhivat kuryādyathāvittānu sārataḥ । (2)
vittaśāḍhyena yaḥ kuryā katau bhaktavinaśyati ॥
tasmāt śāstrokta mārgeṇa yathāvittānusārataḥ । (3)
āhūya deśiko viprau kalāśodhana śodhitam ॥
bhasmarudrākṣa samyuktaṃ śirovastrasamanvitau । (4)
pradhāna kumbhamuddhṛtya vardhanīṃ ca śiropari ॥
aṣṭakumbhān samuddhṛtya paricāra śirasya ca । (5)
ācāryo hasta kūrceṇa spṛśyamūlaṃ japedguruḥ ॥
vādyaghoṣa samāyuktaṃ chatracāmara saṃyutam । (6)
vedadhvani samopetaṃ stotra maṅgala vācakaiḥ ॥
harmya pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā sarvabhaktajanaiḥ saha । (7)
dhūpadīpaṃ purā kṛtvā puṣpalājān prārayet ॥
pādātpādāntaraṃ gatvā dvāradeśe praveśayet । (8)
arghyaṃ datvātha tāmbūlaṃ nīrāñjana tato guruḥ ॥
liṅgāgre śālinākalpya sthaṇḍilaṃcaturaśrakam । (9)
kramāt kumbhā ca saṃsthāpya prasannātma gurūstathā ॥
sakalīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā puṇyāha prokṣaṇaṃ kuru । (10)
āsanaṃ mūrti vinyasya tvaṣṭatriṃśatkalānvitam ॥
dhyātvā sadāśivaṃ liṅge puṣpamādāya deśikaḥ । (i.30b)
kumbhādbījaṃ samādāya liṅgamūrdhni ca vinyaset ॥
vardhanī bījamādāya pīṭhkāyā niyojayet । (2)
aṣṭavidyeśvarān kumbhān tattsthāne niyojayet ॥
śivakumbhaṃ samuddhṛtya śivaliṅgebhiṣecayet । (3)
tattatsthānebhiṣicyātha mantrānnaṃ ca nivedayet ॥
pañcāmṛtādi snapanaiḥabhiṣekaṃ yathākramam । (4)
vastrābharaṇa saṃyuktaṃ sarvāṅgaṃ gandhalepanam ॥
sarvapuṣpairalaṅkṛtya mahāhavirnivedayet । (5)
dhūpadīpa samāyuktaṃ śivaṃ prārthya gurūttamaḥ ॥
brahmaghoṣasamāyuktamāśīrvādaṃ tu kārayet । (6)
mahābhiṣeka kartavyaṃ mahānaivedyameva ca ॥
somāskandādi mūrtīnāṃ utsavaṃ kārayettataḥ । (7)
apare divase dhīmān nityasnānādikaṃ kuru ॥
snapanaṃ tatra kurvīta tvaṣṭottarasahasradhṛk । (8)
aṣṭottara śataṃ vāpi śāntihomasamācaret ॥
tripakṣaṃ vā dvipakṣaṃ vā pakṣamekaṃ yajetkramāt । (9)
śāntihomaṃ hunennityaṃ rudraikādaśapāraṇam ॥
astrai brahmaṣaḍaṅgaiśca japaṃ kuryādviśeṣataḥ । (10)
brāhmaṇān bhojayennityaṃ yathā vibhavavistaram ॥
dine dine sahasraṃ vā pañcādaśamathāpi vā । (i.31a)
saṃpūjya brāhmaṇān paścāt annaikāmanutarpaye ॥
evameva prakāreṇa prasannārthaṃ śivasya ca । (2)
doṣāṇāṃ parihārārthaṃ uṣṇadoṣamanuttaye ॥
evameva prakartavyaṃ deśikaiśca śubheśubhe ।
ācāryaṃ pūjayet paścāt vastrahemāṅgulīyakaiḥ ॥ (3)
mokṣārthī labhate mokṣaṃ putrārthī labhate sutam ।
arthārthī camatvamāpnoti yajarthī yajamāpnuyāt ॥ (4)
ihaloke sukhaṃ prāpya sonte sāyujyamāpnuyāt ।

॥ iti sahasratantra kriyāpāde aṣṭabandhanavidhiḥ samāptaḥ ॥ (5)

śrī pārvatīkucābhoga kuṅkumāṅgita vakṣasam । (i.31b)
vandehaṃ devadeveśaṃ śaṅkaraṃ lokanāyakam ॥
caturvidhapratiṣṭhāṃ tu proktāmunivarairdhruvam । (2)
kāmikādyāgamoktena vidhinātha pradarśyate ॥ (3)
āvartaṃ punarāvartaṃ anāvartamitikramāt ।
paścādantaritaṃ ceti caturthā procyate budhaiḥ ॥
anāvarta svarūpaṃ tatpravakṣye śruṇu ṣaṇmukha । (4)
nāsti devālayo liṅgaṃ yatra grāme kadāpi ca ॥
tatrālayo liṅgamapi yatpratiṣṭāpyate nṛbhiḥ । (5)
tadanāvartamākhyātamanāvartamatha vakṣyate ॥
sa(sā)gareṇa nadībhirvā liṅgamunmūlitaṃ bhavet । (6)
yatra vanyairmṛgairvā tatpratiṣṭāpyatamanyataḥ ॥
āvartamitivikhyātaṃ kāmikāgamakovidaiḥ । (7)
yatrālayo liṅgamapi jīrṇamāsīt puraivahi ॥
tatrālayo liṅgamapi saṃpratiṣṭāpyato yadi । (8)
tadapyāvartamityāhuḥ munayaḥ śāstrakovidāḥ ॥
yatrālayo nāstiliṅgamastyāvartamidaṃ jaguḥ । (9)
yatrālayo sitaliṅga nāstyāvartamitīryute ॥
etadāvartamityuktaṃ punarāvartamucyate । (i.32a)
vimāne varṇahīne vā pīṭhe vā bandhahīnake ॥
navīnaṃ kriyate yatra punarāvartamucyate । (2)
ityuktaṃ punarāvartamathāntaritamucyate ॥
caṇḍālaiḥ patitairvāpi saṃspṛṣṭaṃ vāthadasyubhiḥ । (3)
taddoṣaśāntaye yātṛprokṣaṇaṃ kriyate nṛbhiḥ ॥
tadantaritamākhyātaṃ budhairāgamapāragaiḥ । (4)
pratiṣṭātrividhā proktā antaritamanokayā ॥
pratiṣṭāntaritayorbhedaḥ athākarṣame hi । (5)
yatrastyākarṣaṇaṃ samyak tatpratiṣṭeti kathyate ॥
yatra tvākarṣaṇaṃ nāstittadantaritamucyate । (6)
pratiṣṭā tritayepyatra bālālayamathācaret ॥ (7)
anāvarta pratiṣṭhāyā vidhiṃ vakṣye yathā mata ।
saṅgṛhya sakalāsamyagāgamān gurvanugrahāt ॥ (8)
ācārya stapatī dharmi brāhmaṇā ṛtvijastathā ।
maṅgalairgītavāditrai śubhe lagne śubhe dine ॥ (9)
gatvā śailaṃ śilāṃ dṛṣṭvā dhyāyecchivamayaṃ girim ।
bimbārdhaṃ tu śilākhaṇḍaḥkhaṇḍyate yatra deśikaḥ ॥ (i.32b)
tatra pūjā prakurvīta śivamāvāhayedguruḥ ।
kumbhe cāvāhya taṃ devaṃ śailāddeśika sattamaḥ ॥ (2)
yādṛśaṃ tu śilāliṅgaṃ dharmi nirmāṇa iṣyati ।
tannakṣatraṃ tatpramāṇaṃ dāruṇā hrasvaliṅgakam ॥ (3)
caraliṅgaṃ kārayitvā yathā śāstraṃ hi deśikaḥ । (4)
kumbhe dārumaye liṅge śivamāvāhayedgurum ॥
tādṛśaṃ dāruliṅgam tat svadeśaṃ prāpayedguruḥ । (5)
sāvadhānena manasā nityaṃ tatpūjayet kramāt ॥
ānitaṃ tu śilākhaṇḍaṃ śailācchilpivaśaṃ nayet । (6)
kariṣyate yaccilā liṅgaṃ tattāraṃ tatpramāṇakam ॥ (7)
dāruṇā kārayelliṅgaṃ yathāśāstraṃ vicakṣaṇāḥ ।
etaliṅgaṃ ca munayaḥ balaliṅgaṃ jagurbudhaḥ ॥ (8)
bālālaye sthāpayitvā bālaliṅgaṃmidaṃ guruḥ ।
caraliṅgācchivaṃ kumbhe cāvāhya vidhivadguruḥ ॥ (9)
kumbhācchivaṃ bālaliṅge samyagāvāhya deśikaḥ ।
catuḥṣaṣṭikriyābhistu bālaliṅgaṃ pratiṣṭhalyet ॥ (i.33a)
śilpinā deśikoliṅgaṃ kārayitvāyathāvidhi ।
devalayaṃ praveśyātha liṅgametadyathā vidhi ॥ (2)
etasmin hi śilāliṅge bālādāvāhayecchivam ।
parivāradi devānāmaivamevācaret kramāt ॥ (3)
anāvartavidhi proktā cevam munivarairdhruvam । (4)
anāvarta pratiṣṭheyaṃ kalau nāstyeva niścayaḥ ॥
kalāpanāvarta vidhiḥ bāṇa liṅgaṃprakīrtitam ।
athavā sphaṭikādyairva anāvarta prakīrtitam ॥ (5)
parivārādi devānāṃ anāvartavidhi kramaḥ । (6)
śilākhaṇḍaṃ śilpimukhāt ānīyya vidhivadguruḥ ॥
sāvā्hya śilākhaṇḍe devaṃ pūrvoktamārgataḥ । (7)
śilākhaṇḍāddevamevaṃ kumbhe cāvāhayedguruḥ ॥
kumbhādāvāhayeddevaṃ pūrvavadbālaliṅgake । (8)
athāvarta vidhiṃ vakṣye śruṇu ṣaṇmukha bhaktitaḥ ॥ (9)
sagareṇa nadībhirvā mṛgairvā vanasambhavai ।
yattadunmūlitaṃ liṅgaṃ tasmātkumbhe tu yojayet ॥ (i.33b)
bālālayaṃ sthāpayitvā yathā śastraṃ yathā vidhiḥ ।
paścāt kumbhe samāvāhya pūrvaliṅge tataḥ param ॥ (2)
etadāvartamākhyātamadanyadapi kathyate ।
yatrādayo nāstiliṅgaṃ asyāvartamidaṃ jaguḥ ॥ (3)
atra pratiṣṭhā vakṣye'haṃ nūnaṃ lokahitecchayā ।
mṛttikā liṅgamevatra saṃpūjya vidhivadguruḥ ॥ (4)
tasmāt kumbhe samāvāhya pūrvavadbālaliṅgake ।
etadāvarta saṃjñāsyāt pratiṣṭhāgama kovidaiḥ ॥ (5)
athātrapunarāvarta pratiṣṭhāṃ śrṛṇu ṣaṇmukha । (6)
liṅgātkumbhe samāvāhya kumbhe tadbālaliṅgake ॥
bālālaya pratiṣṭhāyāḥ vidhiṃ śruṇvihavistarāt । (7)
devālaya pratinidhimanyamālayamācaret ॥
devālayantare vā'tha yathā saṃbhavamācaret । (8)
santiyāvanti bibāni ālaye tatra ṣaṇmukha ॥
paṭeṣu citravastreṣu khaṭge darpaṇake tathā । (9)
dārubimbeṣu cāvāhya yathā saṃbhavamācaret । (i.34a)
vimānaṃ sthūlamityuktaṃ sūkṣmaṃ tanmūlaliṅgakam ॥
sthūlaṃ vimāna sūkṣmantu sūkṣmanna sthūlamantaram । (2)
aṅgāṅgībhāvamanayoḥ bhedonāsti dvayoriha ॥
sthūlaṃ sūkṣmadvayaṃ cāpi samākṛṣya yathā vidhi । (3)
anantaraṃ śilpikāryaṃ kārayedgurusattamaḥ ॥ (4)
bālālaye vimānasya vimāne yojanaṃ bhavet ।
abhāve tu paṭādau vā yathāyuktaṃ samācaret ॥ (5)
ityuktaṃ punarāvartapratiṣṭhā saṅgṛhaṃ guhā ।

॥ iti punarāvartapratiṣṭhāvidhissamāptā ॥

kailāsaniyaṃ śambhuṃ gaurīśaṃ bhaktavatsalam । (i.34b)
viṣṇvādyarcitapādābjaṃ vande'haṃ sarvadā śivam ॥ (2)
pratiṣṭhānukramaṃ vakṣye saṅgṛhya sakalāgamān ।
yajamānastu santuṣṭo dhanadhānyasamardhimān ॥ (3)
ācāryo niścayedbudhyā sakalāgamapāragān ।
yajamānastu parayedyajamānadvijotaraḥ ॥ (4)
prayojya yajjamāne sāvācāryaṃ pārayetkramāt ।
ācāryān ṛtvijaścaiva sādhakān sodhakaṃ tathā ॥ (5)
ācārya prathamaṃ kuryādāgamoktaprakārataḥ । (6)
devānujñāṃ dvijānujñāṃ vighnarājasya pūjanam ॥
dravyasaṅgrahaṇaṃ śuddhiṃ dravyabhāgaṃ ca buddhimān । (7)
dīkṣitānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ sodhakaḥ kārayetkramāt ॥
madhuvargapavitrādi karmaṇi sakalānyapi । (8)
bodhakastu prakurvīta samyagācārya mandire ॥
madhuvargaprāśanaṃ syāt prathamaṃ parikīrtitam । (9)
tailenābhañjanaṃ kṣauraṃ mṛttikā snāna vāruṇam ॥
mānasā snānabhasmaṃ ca tvaksāraṃ pañcagavyakam । (i.35a)
rājābhiṣeka kumbhāntaṃ snānaṃ daśavidhaṃ kramāt ॥
taila snānaṃ gṛhe kṛtvā śeṣaṃ kuryānnadītaṭe । (2)
kṣaurādi navadhā snānaṃ pañcagavyādhikaṃ caret ॥ (3)
viśeṣatarpaṇaṃ sandhyāṃ gurūnnivṛtya sarvadā ।
adhiropyātha śibikāmācāryān prāpayedgṛhān ॥ (4)
yātrādānaṃ gṛhaprītiṃ kuryādācāryavittamaḥ ।
devālayamayaṃ gatvā devānujñāṃ samācaret ॥ (5)
rakṣoghnakarmaṇe vidvān praveśabali karmaṇe ।
ācāryāvarayedanyān deśiko grāmaśāntaye ॥ (6)
trīṇi kāryāṇi cācāryaiḥ kārayitvā stribhatyajet । (7)
bimbasya ratnavinyāsaṃ cakṣurunmīlanaṃ tathā ॥
pratimāśodhanaṃ śuddhiḥjaladhānyādivāsanam । (8)
grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ paścādbimbasya śayanaṃ smṛtam ॥
garbhagṛhe padanyāsaṃ pīṭhasthāpanamatravai । (9)
bimbasya jaṭiratnāni bimbasthāpana bandhanam ॥
evameva prakāreṇa vimānādiṣu kārayet । (i.35b)
yāgakṣetra viśuddhyarthaṃ brāhmaṇān bhojayet purā ॥
śilpikāryaṃ prakurvīta paścācchilpi visarjanam । (2)
vāstuśāntyarthamācāryaṃ pūrvavadvārayedguruḥ ॥
pradhāna dīkṣitonaiva vāstuśāntiṃ samācaret । (3)
vimānaṃ bimbaśuddhiṃ ca mṛtsaṅgrahaṇamācaret ॥
yāgaśālāṃ ca saṃskṛtya sthāpayedaṅkurārpaṇam । (4)
bimbasya sarvacāryāṇāṃ rakṣābandhaṃ samācaret ॥
dhānyādibhiralaṅkṛtya dīkṣāvastraṃ tu dhārayet । (5)
mūrtihomāya tatkuryādanyutpādanamatra hi ॥ (6)
prasannapūjanaṃ kuryāt kumbhālaṅkāramapyathā ।
mūrtihomaṃ diśāhomaṃ trikhaṇḍapaṭhaśodhanam ॥ (7)
ācāryaḥ prathamaḥ kuryāt tatvasaṃyojanaṃ sudhiḥ ।
nāḍīsandhāna homaṃ ca paścāt pūrṇāhutiṃ hunet ॥ (8)
agnistha devatāmūrtau yojayedagni rakṣaṇam ।
śivatatvaikyamantrasyād bimbāt kumbhe suyojayet ॥ (9)
yātrāhomaṃ ca dānaṃ ca yāgadeśaṃ ghaṭānnayet । (i.36a)
tasmin pūrvoktamūrtyugnau prāyaścittaṃ hunetsudhiḥ ॥
udvāsanaṃ tataḥ kuryād yāgānyutpādanaṃ tathā । (2)
yāgahomaṃ diśāhomaṃ śāntikumbhābhiṣekakam ॥
liṅge trikhaṇḍasaṃjñe tu tatvapūjāṃ samācaret । (3)
nāḍīsandhāna homaṃ tu mahāpūrṇāhutiṃ caret ॥
vahnimūrtighaṭe yojya yātrāhomaṃ samācaret । (4)
tatra dānāni sarvāṇi kārayedvidhivatsudhiḥ ॥
kumbhānmūlālayaṃ nītvā śivatatvaikyamācaret । (5)
gurūpadiṣṭamārgeṇa bimbāt kumbhe suyojayet ॥
vimāne mūladevecāpyabhiṣeko yathākramam । (6)
yāgaśālāṃ punargatvā prāyaścittāhutiṃ hunet ॥ (7)
tato yāgaṃ samudvāsya sarve rakṣā visarjanam ।
snātvā cāvabudhaṃ sarve dikṣitāstādanantaram ॥ (8)
sarvedevālayaṃ gatvā devatānāṃ ca kārayet ।
mahābhiṣekaṃ tataḥ paścāt utsavaṃ tu samācaret ॥ (9)
gṛhītvā dakṣiṇā sarvepyācāryāstatra bhāgaśaḥ ।
pratiṣṭhāphala dānaṃ tu dīkṣitasyotsavaṃ tathā ॥ (10)
tripakṣayajanaṃ kuryāt yathāśāstraṃ yathāvidhi ।
idameva catuḥṣaṣṭi karmalājaṃ prakīrtitam ॥
eta catuḥṣaṣṭi karma pratiṣṭhā tridaye bhavet । (i.36b)
nayanonmīlanaṃ ratnanyāsaṃ grāma pradakṣiṇam ॥
jaladhānyānyādhivāsaṃ cetya tatkarma catuṣṭayam । (2)
nastyeva punarāvarta pratiṣṭhāyāmidaṃ guhā ॥
ācāryādgehamānīyya tvāśīrvādaṃ samācaret । (3)
catuḥṣaṣṭi kriyāmālā yathākramamihocyate ॥
ārābhyācāryavaraṇamā tripakṣāntamatra hi । (4)
ācāryavaraṇaṃ pūrṇaṃ devānujñāṃ dvitīyakam ॥
tritīyaṃ brāhmaṇānujñā vighneśārcā turiṣyakam । (5)
pañcamaṃ dravyabhāgasyācchaṣṭaṃ tu madhuparkakam ॥
saptamaṃ daśadhāsnānaṃ tvaṣṭamaṃ pañcagavyakam ।
viśeṣatarpaṇaṃ ceti navamaṃ parikīrtitam ॥ (6)
yātrā dānaṃ tu daśamaṃ gṛhaprīta samanvitam ।
ekādaśaṃ tu rakṣoghnaṃ dvādaśaṃ tu baliṃ kṣipet ॥ (7)
trayodaśaṃ grāmaśāntiḥ ratnanyāsacaturdaśā ।
tatvaṃ ca daśakaṃ proktaṃ netronmīlanamatrahi ॥ (8)
ṣoḍaśa pratimāśuddhiḥ pratimāśodhanaṃ tataḥ ।
jalādhivāsamantroktamathāṣṭādaśasaṃjñakam ॥
ekonaviṃśakaṃ proktaṃ tathā grāmapradakṣiṇam । (9)
viṃśastu śanaṃ pīṭhasthāpanaṃ tvekaviṃśakam ॥
dvāviṃśo jaṭiratnānāṃ nyāsamuktaṃ vicakṣaṇaiḥ । (10)
bimbasthāpana mantroktaṃ trayoviṃśati saṃjñakam ॥
svarṇādibandhanaṃ proktaṃ caturviṃśati karmatat । (i.37a)
pañcaviṃśoyāgaśālā sthāpanaṃ parikīrtitam ॥
ṣaḍviṃśastu tataḥ proktaṃ tataḥ śilpi visarjanam ।
saptaviṃśaṃ vāstuśāntimanyācāryeṇa kārayet ॥ (2)
aṣṭaviṃśaṃ bimbaśuddhiḥ vidvadbhiḥ parikīrtitā ।
ekonatriṃśakaṃ proktaṃ mṛtikāgrahaṇaṃ tviti ॥ (3)
yāgāśālā susaṃskāraḥ triṃśatsaṃjñīkamucyate ।
aṅkurārpaṇamantroktamekatriṃśākhya karmatat ॥ (4)
rakṣābandhakarmedaṃ dvātriṃśatsajñakaṃ vidu ।
yāgadhānya sthāpanaṃ syāt trayastriṃśa prakīrtitam ॥ (5)
catustriṃśadidaṃ karma dīkṣāvastrasya dhāraṇam ।
pañcatriṃśadidaṃ karma mūrtyutyutpādanaṃ viduḥ ॥ (6)
prasannapūjā ṣaṭtriṃśatkarmaṃ ceti prakīryate ।
saptatriṃśadidaṃ karma kumbhālaṅkārameva hi ॥ (7)
mūrtihomā diśāhomacāṣṭatriṃśadudāhṛdam ।
idamekona catvāri ghaṭatatva praśodhanam ॥
idaṃ ca catvāridamaṃ kumbhe nāḍī suyojanam । (8)
pūrṇāhutāgnimevātha mūrtyāṃ samyak suyojanam ॥
idasyādeka catvāri śive caikamitaḥ param । (9)
dvicatvāridamaṃ karma bimbātkumbhe suyojanam ॥
yātrāhomamidaṃ karma tricatvāridamaṃ bhavet । (i.37b)
yātrādānamidaṃ proktaṃ catuḥcatvārikaṃ bhuvi ॥
yāgaśālāpraveśaśca pañcacatvāri kīrtitam । (2)
tasmin pūrvoktamūrtyugnau prāyaścittāhutiṃ hunet ॥
udvāsettamevāgniṃ ṣaṭcatvāridamaṃ tvidaṃ । (3)
idaṃ ca saptacatvāri yāgānyutpādanaṃ bhavet ॥
yāgahoma diśāhoma cāṣṭacatvāri kīrtitam । (4)
ekonapañcāśadidaṃ śāntikumbhābhiṣekakam ॥
trikhaṇḍaśodhanaṃ ceti pañcāśatkarma kathyate । (5)
bimbanāḍī susandhānamekapañcāśaducyate ॥
pūrṇāhutāgni rakṣāntaṃ devaṃ kumbhe suyojanam । (6)
tat kuṇḍāgni tu saṃrakṣye dvepañcāśadidaṃ viduḥ ॥
tripañcāśadidaṃ karma yātrāhoma prakīrtitaḥ । (7)
yātrādānamidaṃ karma catuḥpañcāśaducyate ॥
makhālaya praveśaṃ ca pañcapañcāśaducyate । (8)
ṣaṣṭa pañcādidaṃ kumbhādbimbe saṃyojanaṃ bhavet ॥
kumbhābhiṣekaṃ tataḥ paścād gatvā yāgālayaṃ guruḥ । (9)
tasmin pūrvokta yāgāgnau prāyaścitāhutiṃ hunet ॥
udvāsayektamevāgniṃ saptapañcāśaducyate । (i.38a)
paścādavabudhasnānamaṣṭapañcāśaducyate ॥
mahābhiṣekaṃ tatpaścāt ṣaṣṭirekona saṃjñakam ।
idaṃ ṣaṣṭitamaṃ karma cotsavaḥ parikīrtitaḥ ॥ (2)
ācārya dakṣiṇādānamekaṣaṣṭitamaṃ viduḥ ।
pratiṣṭhāphaladānaṃ syāt dviṣaṣṭitamamīritam ॥ (3)
triṣaṣṭitamamācāryotsavamāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ ।
catuḥṣaṣṭitamaṃ proktaṃ tripakṣayajanaṃ tviti ॥ (4)
āhatyakarma jālaṃ tu catuḥṣaṣṭi prakīrtitam ।
pratiṣṭhākriye caitat catuṣaṣṭi prakīrtitam ॥
nayanonmīlanaṃ caiva ratnanyāsa tathaiva ca । (5)
grāma pradakṣiṇaṃ caiva jaladhānyādhivāsanam ॥
etat karma catuṣkaṃ tu punarāvarta karmaṇi । (6)
nastīti munayaḥsarve vadatyāgamahovidāḥ ॥

॥ pratiṣṭhānukramaṇi samāptam ॥
śubhamastu	
ācāryalakṣaṇamidaṃ vakṣyate śāstrasaṃmatam । (i.38b)
śastrijñaṃ satyasandhaṃ ca sakalāgamapāragam ॥
arogiṇamakāmañca bhaktimantaṃ kuṭumbinam । (2)
śrotriyaṃ śīlasaṃpannaṃ snānāhnikaparāyaṇam ॥
dayālaṃ śāntamanasaṃ surūpañcalakṣaṇam । (3)
vedādhyayanasaṃpannaṃ śivapūjā parāyaṇam ॥
dīkṣāvantaṃ sapatnīkaṃ bhaktamanyūnāṃgamabālakam । (4)
anatyaṅgamavṛddhaṃ ca sthalasthaṃ karmako vidam ॥
etādṛśaṃ guruṃ samyak samiṣya parayet prabhuḥ । (5)
dharmādhikāri pūrvoktamācārya parayetkramāt ॥
sad brāhmaṇaścedācāryaṃ parayet svayameva hi । (6)
śūdraścedbrāhmaṇenaiva guruṃ ca parayetprabhuḥ ॥
gṛhasthayeva varayeddīkṣāvān śāstravittamaḥ । (7)
prātasnātvā śucirbhūtvā svīryairbandhubhirāvṛtaḥ ॥
lagne ca śubha muhurte ca gītavādyādi saṃyutaḥ । (8)
akṣatāśca bhanaṃ puṣpaṃ svarṇapātre yathāvidhi ॥
ādāyadhīmānācārya gṛhā gatvātisatvaram । (9)
patitvā pādayo samyagācāryasya subuddhimān ॥
ādiśaiva svarūpajña sakalādhvaravallabha ।
lokeśaṃ janarakṣārthaṃ pratiṣṭāpaya sarvadhā ॥ (i.39a)
iti saṃprārthya gurave nālikera dvayaṃ tathā ।
datvā tu vadanaṃ kuryāddharmiṣṭo gurupādayoḥ ॥ (2)
dṛṣṭvadharmikamācāryaḥ pratibrūyādvicakṣaṇaḥ ।
tathaivastu kariṣyehametakarma yathāvidhi ॥ (3)
anantaraṃ dharmikastu sādhakaṃ bodhakaṃ tathā ।
ṛtvijaṃ ca yathāpūrvaṃ parayedbhaktipūrvakam ॥ (4)
ācāryaṃ kartṛdaivatyaṃ bodhakaṃ gurudaivatam ।
sādhakaṃ brahmadaivatyaṃ ṛtvijo mantradaivatān ॥ (5)
sabhāṃ ca sarvadaivatyaṃ iti sambhāvya dhārmikāḥ ।
sunakṣatre tridhauvāre suyoge sumuhūrtake ॥ (6)
adhivāsaṃ prakurvīta brāhmanairvedapāragaiḥ ।

devadhījñā
…………………………………. । (i.39a, 6)	
svarṇādinirmite pātre taṇḍulānuḍa sannibhān ॥
nālikera phalaṃ ramya kuṅkumaṃ ratnasannibham ।
phalāni puṣpadāmāni tāmbūlaṃ rajanī tathā ॥ (7)
sadarbhān salakṣaṇān cūtapallavān praṇavān tathā ।
gandhapuṣpasuvarṇādi dukūladvayameva ca ॥ (8)
dravyānyetāni saṅgṛhya nṛttagītasamanvitaḥ ।
svastimaṅgalaghoṣeṇa vidhi bhramaṇamācaret ॥ (9)
sarvedevālayaṃ gatvā pādau prakṣālyabhaktitaḥ ।
pradakṣiṇatrayaṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi samāhitaḥ ॥ (i.39b)
dhṛvanterājāta mantreṇa praviśyāsthāna maṇṭapam ।
rucirāsanavaryeyamuttarābhimukho guruḥ ॥
upaviṣṭaśivaṃ dhyāyet pūrvābhimukhayevavā । (2)
sadguruṃ prārthayeddhīmān sarvakarmaśubhāvaham ॥
gurubrahmā guruviṣṇu gururudro maheśvaraḥ । (3)
gurusākṣātparaṃ brahma tasmai śrīgurave namaḥ ॥
ajñānatimirāndhasya jñānāñjana śalākayā । (4)
cakṣurunmīlitā yena tasmai śrīgurave namaḥ ॥
evaṃ dhyātvā guruṃ samyak mantraprāsāda siddhaye । (5)
mastakaṃ tāḍayedvidvān prāṇāyāma tataḥ param ॥
vighneśa pūjanaṃ kuryāttataḥ saṅkalpamācaret । (6)
svastivācana ghoṣeṇa puṇyāhaprokṣaṇaṃ kramāt ॥
vīkṣaṇādyaiśca saṃśodhya pūjayedravyadevatāḥ । (7)
prakṣāḻya cāstramantreṇa sthāliṃ vidvān yathāvidhi ॥
hāridralepanaṃ kuryāt paścāt sthāliṃ samaṃ tataḥ । (8)
tatrasthālyāṃ vinikṣipya sarvadravyāṇi deśikā ॥
sthāliṃ ca śaktirūpāṃ ca taṇḍulānuḍudaivatān । (9)
puṣpe sarasvatiṃ caiva patraṃ gāyatridaivatam ॥
akṣatāśca mahāmantrān dukūlaṃ śaktidaivatam । (10)
kuṅkumaṃ sūryadaivatyaṃ arcayedvidhivadguruḥ ॥
dhūpadīpādikaṃ datvā brahmāṅgairabhimantrayet ।
hastābhyāṃ pātramādāya garbhagṛhamathāviśet ॥ (i.40a)
deśikaḥ sakalīkṛtyamātmaśuddhiṃ samarcanam ।
arghyaṃ ca vidhivatsthāpya dravyaśuddhiṃ samācaret ॥ (2)
garbhāvaraṇa pūjādi śaktyāntaṃ pūjayetkramāt ।
pañcāvaraṇapūjāṃ ca yathoktaṃ pūjayedguruḥ ॥ (3)
dhūpadīpaṃ ca naivedyaṃ cāṣṭapuṣpaṃ samarcayet ।
svarṇapuṣpaṃ tato datvā sāṣṭāṅgaṃ sapraṇamya ca ॥ (4)
hastābhyāṃ pātramādāya sadarbhaṃ deva sannidhau ।
tvamagne mantramuccārya mantroktaṃ prārthayedguruḥ ॥
deva deva jagannātha jagadānanda kāraṇa । (5)
śivādhvara pravṛttohamanujñāṃ dehi me prabho ॥
śaktyālayamathogatvā pūjayektāṃ yathāvidhi । (6)
pūrvoktaṃ pātramādāya sadarbhaṃ devasannidhau ॥
devī gaurī jagannāthe jagadānandakāriṇi । (7)
śivādhvara pravṛttohaṃ anujñāṃ dātumarhasi ॥
āsthāna maṇṭapaṃ gatvā cottarābhimukho guruḥ । (8)
hastābhyāṃ pātramādāya brāhmaṇān prārthayetkramāt ॥
tāmbūla dakṣiṇāṃ datvā brāhmaṇebhyo yathāvidhi । (9)
brāhmaṇān prārthaye samyak karmakartṛtva siddhaye ॥
he brāhmaṇamahābhāgaḥ vedavedāṅga pāragaḥ । (10)
śruti smṛti purāṇajña sarva śāstrṛ suśikṣitaḥ ॥
śivadīkṣā supūtāgāḥ śivapūjāparāyaṇa । (i.40b)
śivādhvarakartumehyanujñāndātumarhathā ॥
brūyāddevaṃ brāhmaṇāśca deśikaṃ pratisatvaram । (2)
śivādhvaraṃ ca kaṃrtu cet yogyatāsiddhirastviti ॥
vighneśapūjanam - re 15540
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi vighnarājasyapūjanam । (i.40b,2)
avighne samāptyarthaṃ tattatkarmasu pūjayet ॥ (3)
vighneśvarālayaṃ gatvā cāstrarāja samanvitaṃ ।
puṇyāhaṃ vācayettatra brāhmaṇairvedapāragaiḥ ॥ (4)
gavyaṃ snapana homaṃ ca prakuryāddeśikottamaḥ । (5)
dvāradevān samabhyarcya garbhāvaraṇa pūjanam ॥
pañcāvaraṇa pūjāṃ ca pūjāmāvaraṇasya ca । (6)
tailādi snapanāntaṃ tadabhiṣekaṃ samācaret ॥
vastragandha bhūṣāṇāṃ ca dhūpaṃ dadyādyathāvidhi । (7)
apūpaṃ cāpi citrānnaṃ modakān prathukaṃ tathā ॥
lājān mudgān karambhāśca nivedya vidhivadguruḥ । (8)
phalāni cekṣudaṇḍaṃ ca taṇḍulān gulamiśritān ॥
urvārukaṅkatriphalaṃ yathāvidhi nivedayet ।
yathāvidhi pravakṣye'haṃ nālikerasalakṣaṇam ॥ (9)
śvetaṃ tu brahmajāti syāt kṣatrajātistu raktakam ।
mañjiṣṭaṃ vaiśyajātiṃ ca śyāmalaṃ śūdrajātikam ॥ (10)
suvṛttaṃ jalasaṃpūrṇaṃ śakaṭākṛtisambhavam ।
adīrghamakraśaṃ ramya asthūlaṃ cāpyabālakam ॥ (i.41a)
avṛttaṃ samareghāḍhyaṃ suśikhaṃ ca sunetrakam ।
evaṃ parīkṣya bahudhā nālikeraṃ salakṣaṇam ॥ (2)
saśikhaṃ tāḍyedvidvān viśikhantu nivedayet ।
triyambakena mantreṇa brahmaregurusattamaḥ ॥ (3)
ekena karmasādguṇyaṃ dvāsātkarma sādhanam । (4)
tṛtīye tāḍane prāpte mūlamantraśataṃ japet ॥
caturtha tāḍane prāpte vaiphalyaṃ karmaṇo bhavet । (5)
dvikhaṇḍaṃ karma sāphalyaṃ śakalaṃ karmanāśanam ॥
bhinne pradhāna nayane deśikasya bhayaṃ bhavet । (6)
dakṣiṇe nayane bhinne rājñenaśasamīritaḥ ॥
uttare nayane bhinne grāmaṃ naśyati takṣaṇāt । (7)
taddoṣaśāntayetkuryāt punarpūjāgaṇeśituḥ ॥
punaḥ pūjāṃ vidhiścātra vakṣyate śāstrasaṃmatam । (8)
nālikera śataṃ tatra tāḍayecca gaṇeśituḥ ॥
gajānana suraśreṣṭa śivaputra namostute ।
nārikelaṃ tāḍaye'haṃ nirvighnatvaṃ prasīda me ॥ (9)
khaṇḍāni nālikerāṇāṃ tāmbūlādi nivedayet ।
aṣṭapuṣpaṃ samabhyarcya suvarṇapuṣpaṃ pradāpayet ॥ (10)
vedena drāviḍestotrai gaṇeśatālanāṭyakaiḥ ।
sāṅgopāṅgaṃ ca saṃpūjya stutvā saṃprārthayedguruḥ ॥ (i.41b)
yatra yatra kriyārambhaḥ muṣṭibhyāṃ mūrdhnideśikaḥ ।
vināyakamanuskṛtya kuṭṭanaṃ pañcavārakam ॥ (2)
amṛtaṃ yatsuṣumnāyāḥ sāgarena divatsṛtam ।
mūlādhārastha vighneśṃ tarpayet tena vai guruḥ ॥ (3)
gururhastau vāmasavyau muṣṭimudrāṃ prakalpayet ।
madhyamāṅgulibhiścaiva mastake saṃpratāḍayet ॥ (4)
mastakasthāmṛtenaiva śarīrāṇi ca secayet ।
sarvadevāgraja śreṣṭa vighneśa gaṇanāyaka ॥
tvāmupāsya ca yatkarma tatkarma saphalaṃ kuru । (5)
ityevaṃ tu kṛte bhaktyā vighneśasaṃ prasīdati ॥
anujñāpātramādāya sadarbhaṃ deva sannidhau । (6)
skandāgraja gaṇādhīśa devyā vallabhayā saha ॥
śivādhvarapravṛttohaṃ vighnaśāntaṃ kuruṃ prabho । (7)
laḍḍukāmodakānanaṃ phalādīni yathāvidhi ॥
śiveditāni sarvāṇi vighneśāya purairvahi । (8)
tatsarvaṃ brahmacāribhyo dadyāddakṣiṇayā saha ॥
tataḥ pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā vighneśaṃ varadaṃ guruḥ । (9)
dharmādhikāri rājecedeyādravyaṃ yathāvidhi ॥
dhanikaścettāyai vāsau devācchaktyānurodhataḥ । (10)
aśaktaścedyācayitvā deyāddravyaṃ tu dhārmikaḥ ॥
dhaniko vā daridro vā vittaśāṭhyaṃ na kārayet । (i.42a)
vittaśāṭhye kṛte karma niṣphalaṃ bhavati dhruvam ॥
śivadharmaṃ samuddiśya dhātāro yācakastathā । (2)
sādhakā bodhakāścāpi modakāśca naraṃlapi ॥
prerakapreritaścāpi teṣāṃ saṃsargiṇojanaḥ । (3)
sarve śivaprasādena śivasāyujyamāpnuyuḥ ॥
ācāryo mantrapūrṇaśca dhanapūrṇaśca dharmikaḥ ।
kriyāpūrṇe yatraśilpi tatra syācchivasannadhiḥ ॥ (4)
śivasannidha mātreṇa sarveṣāṃ ca gihaṃ bhavet ।
ācāryo mantrahīnaścet dhanahīnaśca dharmikaḥ ॥ (5)
śilpi kriyāvihīnaśca tatra no deva sannadhiḥ ।
asannidhānā devasya sarveṣāṃ vaṃśanāśanam ॥ (6)
śivadharma samuddiśya yaddravyaṃ dīyate'pi vā ।
taddravyaṃ tāmrapātre tu gṛhītvā dharmikasvayam ॥ (7)
śivārpaṇaṃ ca kṛtvaiva gururhaste samarpayet ।
guruścānantaraṃ kuryād dravyaśuddhi yathāvidhi ॥ (8)
pañcagavyena puṇyāhatīrthena prokṣayeddhanam ।
mūlena ca śataṃ japtvā brahmāṅgaiśca tadardhakam ॥ (9)
tenaiva dravyaśuddhisyācchuddhadravyaṃ śivārpitam ।
kartāvittānusāreṇa bhāgaṃ kuryādyathāvidhi ॥ (10)
dravyabhāgakramam - re 15540
dravyabhāgakramaṃ vakṣye śrṛṇu ṣaṇmukha vistarāt । (i.42a, 10)
vibhajecca śivadravya tatvaṃ ca daśadhāpunaḥ ॥
bhāgatrayaṃ tu yāgārthamekabhāgobhiṣecane । (i.42b)
gurvādinā tatrabha tritayaṃ samudīritam ॥
brahmasantarpaṇayātra dvibhāgaḥ parikīrtitāḥ । (2)
japārthaṃ tu dvibhāgasyādekasya bhūridānayoḥ ॥
yāgaśālā vinirmāṇe śilpinaścaikabhāgakam ।
pratiṣṭādi tripakṣāntaṃ dvibhāgaḥ parikīrtitāḥ ॥ (3)
ataisya kramaṃ vakṣye sāvadhānāmanā śṛṇu ।
yāgadravyaṃ tu pūrvoktaṃ vibhaje dravidhā sudhiḥ ॥ (4)
tatra dvibhāgo bandhārthaṃ jaṭiratnādināmapi ।
ghaṭaṃ ca kalaśāścaiva bhāṇḍāgaṇḍiśarāvakāḥ ॥ (5)
pālikā ghaṭikāścaiva ye cānye pātrabhedakāḥ ।
te sarve kāñcānāḥ proktāścāthavā rājadhā smṛtaḥ ॥ (6)
tatrāpi śaktyābhāve tu tāmbrapātrāṇi yojayet ।
athavācārakruṭṭāni pātrāṇi parikalpayet ॥ (7)
tatrāpi śaktyābhāve tu mṛṇmayaṃ pātramucyate ।
arghyapātrāṇi nāḍī ca dhūpadīpādi pātrakam ॥ (8)
etāni kāṃsyapātrāṇi kathitāni munīśvaraiḥ ।
kūrcaṃ ca nālikeraṃ ca cūtapallavameva ca ॥
etāni kāñcanātyāhurathavā rājanāni ca । (9)
hemotapalaṃ hemaśūlaṃ hemapaṅkajameva ca ॥
navaratnāni caiteṣāṃ dvibhāgaḥ parikīrtitaḥ । (10)
(śālitaṇḍula dhānyānāmardhabhāgaḥ prakīrtitaḥ)
ājyārthaṃ kathitaṃ bhāgatritayaṃ ca ṣaḍānanā ॥ (i.43a)
homadravyamathoddiśya ceka bhāgaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ।
śālitaṇḍuladhānyānāmardha bhāgaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ॥ (2)
vedivastraṃ kumbhavastraṃ dukūlatantureva ca ।
pañcavarṇaṃ ca yatsūtraṃ tadardhaṃ caikabhāgakam ॥
ekabhāgaṃ tu rūpyaṃ syāt abhiṣekāya caikakam । (3)
naivedyaṃ cārdhabhāgasyāditi dvādaśadhā smṛtāḥ ॥ (4)
aṣṭabandhakramehyeṣaḥ niyamaḥ parikīrtitaḥ ।
svarṇādibandhane cānya dvidhiradya pravakṣyate ॥
svarṇabandha tribhāgasyāt ghṛtebhāra dvayaṃ smṛtam । (5)
pūrvoktamabhiṣekārtha dravyaṃ ṣoḍā vibhājayet ॥
japasnapanayorardha tailaṃ cārya prakīrtitaḥ । (6)
abhiṣeca niyadravyameka bhāgaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ॥
ekabhāgastu vastraṃ syāt naivedyaṃ caikabhāgakam । (7)
avaśiṣṭaṃ cotsavāya viniyogaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ॥
ekabhāgamidaṃ dravyaṃ dvidhā kṛtvā guru svayam । (8)
mahābhiṣeke ca tathā prasanne cābhiṣecane ॥
viniyogaṃ prakurvīta deśikosabuddhimanyaśi । (9)
ācāryādi tribhāgasyākramaṃ vakṣye yathāvidhi ॥
vastrābharaṇapātrāṇāṃ bhāgadvitayamīritam । (10)
dakṣiṇāścaikabhāgastu kathito munisattamaiḥ ॥
etadravyaṃ ca navadhā vibhajyagururādarāt । (i.43b)
ācāryāya tribhāgasyāt ṛtvigbhyaśca dvibhāgakam ॥
parivārācārya bhāgastu dvibhāgaḥ parikīrtitaḥ । (2)
bodhakācāryabhāgastu sādhyaikaṃ sādhakāya ca ॥
brahmasantarpaṇāyoktaḥ dvibhāgaḥ pañcadhābhavet । (3)
brāhmaṇebhyaścānnadānerbhū yadbhāgaṃ catuṣṭayam ॥
avaśiṣṭaṃ caikabhāgaṃ pradadyāt bhukti dakṣiṇām । (4)
japārthasyaikabhāgasya viniyoga ihocyate ॥
tadravyaṃ vibhaje ṣoḍā yathāvidhi gurusvayam । (5)
caturvedeṣu catvāro bhāgaḥ proktā vicakṣaṇaiḥ ॥
ekabhāga śaiva jape purāṇe cārdhabhāgakam । (6)
navagrahajapepādastataḥ syātbhānuvandane ॥
ācārya ṛtvijaścaiva yāvat saṅkhyā bhavanti hi । (7)
ekai vedajapāstāvat saṅkhyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ॥
pūrvaṃ yatkapitaṃ bhūridānārthaṃ caikabhāgakam । (8)
taccaturthā vibhajyaiva dānārthaṃ caikabhāgakam ॥
avaśiṣṭaṃ dakṣiṇāyai sarveṣāṃ parikalpayet । (9)
tripakṣayajanārthaṃ yadbhāgadvitayamīritam ॥
tatsaptadhā prakurvīta dhīmānācārya sattamaḥ ।
nityābhiṣeke ca tathā tribhāgaḥ parikīrtitaḥ ॥ (10)
brahmasantarpaṇāyaika bhāgastu parikīrtitaḥ ।
tripakṣādine cābhiṣekāyavibhāgakam ॥ (i.44a)
