brahmasantarpaṇāyaika bhāgastu parikīrtitaḥ ।
mahābhiṣekoyaccoktaṃ prasanne cābhiṣecane ॥ (2)
tatho pātrābhiṣeke'pi saṃpradāya samīritam ।
brahmasantarpaṇe pūrvaṃ yo vidhiḥ parikīrtitaḥ ॥ (3)
tadvadatrāpi kurvīta brahmasantarpaṇe sudhiḥ ॥
madhuparkakramam
madhuparkakramaṃ vakṣye yathāśāstraṃ yathāvidhi । (i.44a, 3)
sodhakastu prakurvīta madhuvargaṃ guro gṛhe । (4)
puṇyāhavācanaṃ pūrvaṃ śālibhittaṇḍulaṃ caret ॥ (5)
vilikhya nalinaṃ tatra bodhako buddhimānyaset ।
śarāvaṃ karṇikā madhye kadalīphala pūritam ॥ (6)
taddakṣiṇe śarāvaṃ ca nikṣipet madhupūritam ।
tadvāma bhāge vinyasya śarāvaṃ ghṛtapūritam ॥ (7)
nirīkṣaṇādibhiḥ samyak catuḥsaṃskāramācaret ।
tadbahiḥparitaḥ samyak paristaraṇamācaret ॥ (8)
madhyastha kadalīpātraṃ śivatatvaṃ ca pūjayet ।
madhupātre pūjayittvā vidyātatvaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥
ātmatatvaṃ pujayittvā ghṛtapātre vicakṣaṇaḥ । (9)
rudrasūktaṃ viṣṇusūktaṃ brahmasūktaṃ yathāvidhi ॥
japitvābrāhmaṇaissākaṃ bodhakaḥsvamadhādharāt । (10)
pratadyādakṣiṇāṃstebhyo brāhmaṇebhyotha bodhakaḥ ॥
ghṛtaṃ madhuni saṃyojya tadvayaṃ kadaḻīphale । (i.44b)
āpyāyasveti mantreṇa phalaṃ kṣīreṇa pūrayet ॥
madhuvāteti mantreṇa sarvaṃ hastena poṣayet । (2)
tatvatrayaṃ ca saṃpūjya śivaikyaṃ kārayedbudhaḥ ॥
tatvamantreṇa vidhivat ācāryebhyo yathāvidhi । (3)
badarīphalamātraṃ tu deyā tatbodhakasvayam ॥
tatprāśanaṃ prakurvīta ācāryāśca trivārakam । (4)
paścādācamanaṃ kuryāt madhuparkakramohyayam ॥
athocyate snānavidhi samāhitamanāśṛṇu । (5)
nirīkṣaṇādibhistailaṃ saṃskṛtya vidhivadguruḥ ॥
tāmbūlacarvaṇaṃ kuryāstailābhyañjamācaret । (6)
snānamuṣnodakaiḥ kuryādathavāsītavāribhiḥ ॥
tadāniṃ vāparedyurvā deśiko ṛtvijastathā । (7)
rakṣāṃ kurvanti ye sarve teṣāṃ kṣauraṃ vidhīyate ॥
snānaṃ nadījalekuryā taṭākābhasivāthavā । (8)
paścācca mṛttikāsnānaṃ teṣāmeva vidhīyate ॥
puṇyāhaṃ vācayettatra nadītīre tu bodhakaḥ । (9)
snāyāttonaivatīrthena snānametaddhivāraṇam ॥
paścānmānasikaṃ snānaṃ bhasmasnānamataḥ param । (10)
tejo vṛkṣatvaṃ dhīmān nikṣipejjalapūrite ॥
bhāṇḍe tu padamantreṇa japtvā snānaṃ tadaśvaret । (i.45a)
pañcatvat sāratoyena vāthavā snānamācaret ॥
aśvattha jambūnyagrothamaudumbaramahīruham । (2)
tvakpañcakamidaṃ śāstre tvaksāramitikathyate ॥
paścāt snānaṃ pañcagavyaiḥ rājasnāna mataḥ param । (3)
catuṣpādyaṃ tripādyaṃ vācopaviśyakramādime ॥
vāsanādravyamīlitaiḥ snāyāmuṣṇodakairapi ।
etadrājābhiṣekākhyaṃ vadantyāgama kovidāḥ ॥ (4)
kumbhasnānaṃ tataḥ kuryāt ṛtvijo deśikastathā ।
puṇyāhavācanaṃ kṛtvā śucaudeśe nadītaṭe ॥ (5)
saṃsthāpya kumbhavidhivattatra pāśupatāstrakam ।
arcayitvaiva vidhivaddhomaṃ kurya tu bodhakaḥ ॥ (6)
japaṃ tatra prakurvīta bodhako brāhmaṇaiḥ saha ।
rudrasūktādi mantreṇa datvātebhyaśca dakṣiṇām ॥ (7)
tenakumbhodakenaiva sarve snānaṃ samācaret ।
idamastrābhiṣekākhyaṃ kumbhasnānamatīyate ॥ (8)
nindadoṣa nivṛtyarthaṃ madhuparka vidhīyate ।
dehārogyakṛte proktaṃ tailasnāna vicakṣaṇaiḥ ॥ (9)
kṣauramaṅgakaṃ proktaṃ tadanācāra śāntaye ।
mṛttikāsnānamākhyātaṃ sarveṣāṃ ātmaśuddhaye ॥
vyādhidoṣa nivṛtyarthaṃ tvaksārasnānamucyate । (10)
nityakarmādi pūrvārthaṃ bhasmasnānamihocyate ॥
saṅgadoṣa nivṛtyarthaṃ snānaṃ syāt pañcagavyakam । (i.45b)
rājābhiṣeka mantroktaṃ manodoṣanivṛttaye ॥
kumbhābhiṣeka mantroktaṃ śivabhāvaikya siddhaye । (2)
pañcagavyaprāśanena vākśuddhisamudīritā ॥
evaṃ daśavidhaṃ snānaphalamuktaṃ vicakṣaṇaiḥ ।
tasmātsarvaprayatnena snānaṃ daśavidhaṃ caret ॥ (3)
viśeṣatarpaṇam
viśeṣatarpaṇaṃ vakṣye śṛṇu ṣaṇmukhavistarāt । (i.45b, 3)
vaidīkaṃ tu puraḥ kṛtvā paścācchaivaṃ samācaret ॥ (4)
anantaraṃ viśeṣākhyaṃ tarpaṇaṃ kartumārabhet ।
ācamya prathamaṃ paścātsaṅkalpaṃ tu samācaret ॥ (5)
karāṅganyāsamathavā jalaśuddhi prakīrtitā ।
svāhāntena ca mūlena devatātarpaṇaṃ caret ॥ (6)
tatvatrayāṇāmekaikaṃ kalāḥ pañca śivatrayam ।
brahmāṅgānāṃ tathaikaikaḥ tatvanātṛtayaṃ tathā ॥ (7)
śivamūlenāṣṭottaraśataṃ paścāt samīritam ।
manonmanye tadardhasyādityāgamavido guruḥ ॥ (8)
daśottaraśataṃ prokta śivāṅgairavatarpaṇam ।
tatvatrayaṃ tataḥ proktaṃ sakalāgamapāragaiḥ ॥ (9)
brahmātadaṅga devaśca viṣṇurudrau tathaiva hi ।
rudraṃ caikādaśaḥ proktaḥ sūryadvādaśa kīrtitāḥ ॥ (10)
aṣṭau ca vasavaḥ proktāmarutasaptayeva hi ।
navagrahāḥ lokapālārāśayaḥ tārakāstathā ॥ (i.46a)
saptarṣayaḥtathā proktāścatvārasanakādayaḥ ।
dvādaśāpi samākhyātā dūrvāsādyā maharṣayaḥ ॥ (2)
tathaiva pūrvācāryāṇāmekādaśa samīritam ।
gurubhyaḥ saptasaṃkhyākaṃ paścāsyātpitṛtarpaṇam ॥ (3)
darśatarpaṇavatkuryāt pitṛtarpaṇamatra hi ।
devānāṃ devatīrthena ṛṣīṇāṃmṛṣitīrthataḥ ॥ (4)
pitṝṇāṃ pitrṛtīrthena tarpaṇaṃ tu samācaret ।
upavītena devānāṃ ṛṣīṇāṃ ca nivītataḥ ॥ (5)
prācītāṃ pitṛtaḥ proktaṃ pitṛtarpaṇa karmaṇi ।
akṣatādevatānāṃ tu ṛṣīṇāṃ ca tilākṣate ॥ (6)
pitṝṇāṃ tilamātraṃ hi tarpaṇe samudīritam ।
svāhākāreṇa devānā tarpaṇaṃ samudīritam ॥ (7)
tarpayāmīti kartavyaṃ ṛṣīṇāṃ tarpaṇaṃ sudhiḥ ।
svadhāntenaiva kartavyaṃ pitṛtarpaṇamādarāt ॥ (8)
brahmāstraṃ ca tathā pāśapatāstraṃ ca ghorasaṃjñakaṃ ।
pratyaṅgirāstraṃ rudrāstramiti pañcāstramucyate ॥ (9)
daśasaṅkhyāṃ prakurvīta ekaikāstra tarpaṇam ।
huṃ phaḍityāntamantreṇa devatīrthena tarpaṇam ॥ (10)
kuśāgrena prakurvīta cāstrāṇāṃ tarpaṇaṃ sudhiḥ ।
evaṃ śivapratiṣṭhāyāṃ tarpaṇaṃ samudīritam ॥ (i.46b)
anyadevapratiṣṭāyāṃ tarpaṇaṃ tu samācaret ।
tasyāvaraṇadevānāṃ tasyāpyaivaṃ krameṇa hi ॥ (2)
viśeṣa tarpaṇa vidhireṣā munibhirīritā ।
viśeṣasandhyā prakurvīta paścāddeśikasattamaḥ ॥ (3)
rudraṃ ca camakaṃ paścātpadamantraṃ japedguruḥ ।
pañcākṣara sahasraṃ tu japeśca su samāhitaḥ ॥ (4)
manonmanyāṣṭottaraśatasaṅkhyā japedguruḥ ।
brahmāṅgānāṃ tadardhaṃ syāddastrāṇāṃ ca tadardhakam ॥ (5)
evaṃ japitvā tatpaścādyathāvidhi samāhitaḥ ।
yasminkāle tarpaṇaṃ syāt dhyāyetat śaktimādarāt ॥
anantaraṃ ca śibikāmārūpya gurusattamaḥ । (6)
ṝtvigbhiḥ saṅghṛtaṃ samyak nṛttagītairalaṅkṛtaḥ ॥
āhatya svagṛhaṃ tatra gṛhaprītiṃ samācaret । (7)
yātrādānaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā nṛttagītādisaṃyutaḥ ॥
gatvā devālayaṃ tatra devaṃ saṃpūjayedguruḥ । (8)
pūjayitvā namaskṛtya stutvā saṃprārthayedguruḥ ॥
rakṣoghna karmaṇe vidvān praveśabali karmaṇe । (9)
ācārye varayedanyān deśiko grāmaśāntaye ॥
anyācāryai kārayitvā trīṇi kāryāṇi deśikaḥ ।
trinācāryāntataḥ paścāt deśikastu parityajet ॥ (10)
etāni trīṇi kāryāṇi na kuryāddeśikasvayam ।
prāyaścitta prakurvīta kārayediti deśikaḥ ॥
rakṣoghnam
rakṣoghnaṃ vakṣyate skanda sarvavighnanivāraṇam । (i.47a)
devālayasya naiṛtyāṃ rakṣoghnaṃ tu samācaret ॥ (2)
rakṣoghnārthaṃ prakurvīta maṇṭapaṃ tatra deśikaḥ ।
caturdvāra samāyuktaṃ vedikuṇḍa praśobhitam ॥ (3)
raṅgavalyācca saṃyuktaṃ nānālaṅkāraśobhitam ।
guruṇā yo vrataḥ pūrvaṃ rakṣoghnārthaṃ tu deśikaḥ ॥ (4)
sa dhṛtvā na vyavasanaṃ gītavāditra saṃyutaḥ ।
astradevaṃ puraskṛtya brāhmaṇairvedapāragaiḥ ॥
astradevaṃ ca saṃsthāpya caiśānyāṃ diśi maṇṭape । (5)
puṇyāhaṃ vācayettatra maṇṭape śalyaśodhanam ॥ (6)
pañcakumbhaṃ ca saṃsthāpya tatra vedyā hi deśikaḥ ।
tatrāstrapañcakaṃ samyakpūjayeddeśikasvayam ॥ (7)
tadvadagnau ca saṃpūjya pañcāstraṃ ca yathāvidhi ।
tairastrai saṃprakurvīta rākṣasoccāṭanāya hi ॥ (8)
homaṃ tatra prakurvīta nimbatailena sarvavaiḥ ।
kārpāsabījaistu valai puṣpaiśca karavīrakaiḥ ॥ (9)
śoṇārkasya samidbhiśca hunettatrāstramantrakaiḥ ।
pratyekamastradevānāṃ kūṣmaṇḍairhomamācaret ॥ (10)
pūrṇāhutiṃ prakurvīta yathāśāstraṃ tu deśikaḥ ।
agnimūrtiṃ samādāya kumbhesaṃyojayet kramāt ॥ (i.47b)
kumbhamūrtiṃ samādāya cāstradeve ca yojayet ।
astradevāya dhūpaṃ ca dīpaṃ datvā samāhitaḥ ॥ (2)
rakṣoghnajapa kartubhyaḥ brāhmaṇebhaśca dakṣiṇām ।
datvā devālayaṃ gatvā cāstradevena deśikaḥ ॥ (3)
tataḥ paścāddeśikoyaṃ snānaṃ kuryādyathāvidhi ।
rātrau kuryācca rakṣoghnaṃ na kuryādahnideśikaḥ ॥
akṛtesati rakṣoghne rākṣatāpiśitācanaḥ । (4)
yāgavighnaṃ prakurvanti tato rakṣoghnamācaret ॥
praveśabaliḥ
praveśabalimārasye vaktumadyaṣaḍānana । (i.47b, 5)
guruṇā yo vrataḥ pūrvaṃ praveśabali karmaṇe ॥
sacalā yadvinirgatya cāstradevena deśikaḥ । (6)
paridhāya navaṃ vastraṃ gītavāditra saṃyutaḥ ॥
anādini ca saṅgṛhya balidravyāṇi tatra hi । (7)
daśadikṣubaliṃ kuryādbrahmādibhyo yathāvidhi ॥
sthalaśiddhiṃ vidhāyaiva tatra kuryāt baliṃ sudhīḥ । (8)
tattat disthapiśācānāmarcanaṃ gandhapuṣpakaiḥ ॥
bhūtaṃ baliṃ dadāmīti baliṃ datvā yathā vidhi । (9)
annaṃ bhakṣyaṃ phalaṃ nālikeramanyaphalādikam ।
mañjarikramukasyāpi nālikerasya mañjari ॥ (10)
nimbatailāvasittāṃ ca dīpikāṃ tatrapādayet ।
dhūpaṃ guggula saṃyuktaṃ raktamāṃse tathaiva ca ॥
tadbhāve tu kūṣmāṇḍaṃ kuṅkumaimiśritaṃ bhavet । (11)
evaṃ pratidiśaṃ dadyādbalimācārya sattamaḥ ॥
rākṣasāśca piśācāśca ye cānye vighnakārakaḥ । (i.48a)
svīkṛtyaināṃ baliṃ yūyaṃ gacchatvadyaśivājñayā ॥
ukterthaṃ tatradigdevānarcaye dhūpadīpakaiḥ ।
svāhāntena baliṃ datvā digdevebhyo videśikaḥ ॥ (2)
ajñāpyāyaṃ ca digdevānālasya pradakṣiṇam ।
kṛtvā devālayaṃ gatvā snāyādācārya sattamaḥ ॥ (3)
niśiyekhalaṃ kartavyaṃ praveśabali karma tat ।
praveśabalyakaraṇe rākṣasāṃ bādhakāmuhuḥ ॥
yāgavighnobhavet tasmāt praveśabalimācaret ॥ (4)
grāmaśāntiḥ
grāmaśāntikramaṃ vakṣye sarvadevapriyaṃ guha । (i.48a, 4)
ālyātpaścimebhāge grāmaśānti samācaret । (5)
vidhiṃ cābhidāsyāmi sāvadhānamanā śṛṇu ॥
bhūmiṃ samatalaṃ kṛtvā nimnonnataṃ vivarjitam । (6)
navahastaṃ pañcahastaṃ saptahastamathāpi vā ॥
caturaśraṃ kārayitvā caturdvārasamanvitam । (7)
maṇṭapaṃ tatra nirmāya dvādaśastaṃbha saṃyutam ॥
idaṃ kṣetraṃ dvidhā kṛtvā madhye vediṃ prakalpayet । (8)
vistārārtha samutseyaṃ vedi tatra pralkalpayet ॥
tatpūrva bhāgemadhye tu kuṇḍaṃ ca caturaśrakam । (9)
vedisaumyapade tatra navakumbhaṃ ca vinyaset ॥
aiśānyāṃ astrarājaṃ ca puṇyāhaṃ pāvakepade । (10)
taddakṣiṇe gurusthānaṃ naiṛtyāṃ pañcagavyakam ॥
audumbarādigrathitaṃ darbhamuṣṭiṃ ca vāruṇe ।
tadvāyavyaṃ samākhyātamupasādhana mandiram ॥ (i.48b)
alaṅkṛtya vidhānena tathaiva kadalīdrumaiḥ ।
gomayālepanaṃ kṛtvā raṅgavalyādi śobhitam ॥ (2)
cītapallava puṣpāḍhyaṃ sugandhidravya vāsitam ।
evaṃ kṛtvā tu matimān grāmaśāntimathācaret ॥ (3)
ācāryo guruṇā yastu pratobhavati śāntaye ।
parīdhāya navaṃ vastraṃ sottarīyamayaṃ śuciḥ ॥ (4)
anagnaṃ prātarārabhya nityakarma samarpya ca ।
astradevaṃ puraskṛtya nṛttagītasamanvitaḥ ॥
rātrau prathamayāmānte śāntidravyasamanvitaḥ । (5)
nirgatya devāyatanāt sagatva śāntimaṇṭapam ॥
puṇyāhavācanaṃ kṛtvā pañcagavyaṃ samācaret । (6)
śālyāvanuttaye kuryāt saṃskārān maṇṭapasya tu ॥
astradevaṃ samabhyarcya dhūpadīpādikaṃ caret । (7)
deśikaṃ ca tataḥ paścāt vedyāṃ cakraṃ samālikhet ॥ (8)
likhitvā bhūpuraṃ dvāraṃ dvandvaṃ vṛttamataḥ param ।
likhedaṣṭadalaṃ vidvān vṛttadvandvāntarālake ॥
navakoṣṭaṃ samālikhya caturaśraṃ vicakṣaṇa । (9)
śvetapitādimaistatra piṣṭacūrṇaisamālikhet ॥
athavā taṇḍulekuryā tileścakrasya kalpanam । (10)
cakrapūjā kramāścātra vakṣyate cāstrasammatam ॥
madhyakoṣṭe kṣetrapālamarcayedvidhipūrvakam । (i.49a)
pūrvādiṣvaṣṭakoṣṭeṣu pūjayedaṣṭabhairavān ॥
mahendrādyaṣṭaśaktiśca daleṣvaṣṭasupūjayet । (2)
bhūpurasya caturdvāreṣvasurādīn samarcayet ॥
kṣetreśādīn samabhyarcya navakumbheṣu deśikaḥ । (3)
agnikāryaṃ prakurvīta kṣetreśān tatra pūjayet ॥
samidannājyalājādīn hunettatra yathāvidhi । (4)
vapāhomaṃ prakurvīta na cetkūṣmāṇḍahomakam ॥
pācako hastakhaṇḍena dvidhā bhitvā ca tatphalam । (5)
nirasya tasya bījāni bījasthānaṃ hunedbudhaḥ ॥
ājyābhighāramādāya sandhānāhutimācaret । (6)
rudhirānnaṃ baliṃ dadyāṃ na cenmāṣānameva ca ॥ (7)
pūrṇāhutiṃ tataḥ kṛtvā dhūpa dīpaṃ samācaret ।
kuṇḍāgniṃ mūrtitatpaścāt cakre saṃyojayet sudhiḥ ॥ (8)
cakrātkumbhe tu saṃyojya kumbhādācārya mānase ।
ādāya darbhagradithamaudumbarasamiccayam ॥ (9)
prajvālya gītavāditraiḥ paricāramukhā sudhiḥ ।
ta ca paryagnikaraṇaṃ vidhibhramaṇa mācaret ॥ (i.49b)
divikābhiranekābhirastradeva samanvitam ।
puṇyāha pañcagavyena vidhiṃ saṃprokṣyabuddhimān ॥ (2)
alātamenamāgneryaṃ muratastu visarjayet ।
astraśemalayaṃ nītvā tvācāryo snānamācaret ॥ (3)
astreśāya tataḥ paścāt prāyaścittaṃ samācaret ।
grāmaśānti vihīne tu pīḍāsyādrakṣaserihā ॥ (4)
kiñcāśuddhiṃ grāmapuraraṣṭānāṃ sambhavediha ।
durbhikṣaṃ ca bhavettasmāt grāmaśāntiṃ samācaret ॥ (5)
ratnanyāsavidhiḥ
ratnanyāsavidhiṃ vakṣye śṛṇutāraka mardana । (i.49b, 5)
dvividhaṃ vakṣyate ratnanyāsamāgamakovidaiḥ ॥ (6)
aṅgaratnanyāsamekamihānyā jaṭiratnakam ।
jaṭiratnamataḥ paścādyakṣyate bandhanātpurā ॥ (7)
idāniṃ vakṣyate cāṅgaratnanyāsamidaṃ śṛṇu ।
bimbapratinidhi pañcakathyate śāstravittamaiḥ ॥ (8)
lohajaṃ śailajaṃ dārubhāvaṃ saudhamathotyapi ।
paṭhādi kāmike pañcadeva sannidhihetavaḥ ॥ (9)
bimbaṃ tu prāṅmukhaṃ kṛtvā puṇyāhaṃ ca tadagrataḥ ।
kṛtvā navapadaṃ tatra taṇḍule gururādarāt ॥ (i.50a)
kumbhānnikṣipya navasupadeṣu nacavān sudhiḥ ।
aṣṭanāgānaṣṭakumbhe śeṣaṃ prapūjayet ॥ (2)
biṃbamūrdhnī tu māṇikkyaṃ lalāṭe candranīlakam ।
vajraṃ tu hṛdaye nābhau vaiḍūryaṃ vinyasetsudhiḥ ॥ (3)
jānudvaye tu sphaṭikaṃ padmarāgaṃ ca pādayoḥ । (4)
karṇayugme mauktikaṃ ca bāhvaurmarakatanyaset ॥
pravālaṃ pāṇitalayoḥ ācāryo vinyaset kramāt । (5)
ekaikāṅge ratnayugmaṃ gandhasyopari vinyaset ॥
mūrdhāratnetvādiśeṣaṃ anantaṃ ca lalāṭake । (6)
vāsukī hṛdaye nābhau takṣakaṃ tu prapūjayet ॥ (7)
kārkoṭakaṃ jānuyugme padmaṃ pādadvaye tathā ।
karṇayugme mahāpadmaṃ bhujayo śaṅkhapālakam ॥ (8)
hastayogulikaścaiva liṅganyāsamathocyate ।
mūrdhni māṇikyamevasyāt liṅgayonyaṣṭadikṣvapi ॥ (9)
aṣṭaratnāni vinyasya pūrvavatpūjayedguruḥ ।
tattatkumbhān tattadaṅge prokṣayedgururādarāt ॥ (i.50b)
dhūpadīpādikaṃ datvā tāmbūlaṃ ca nivedayet । (2)
etatbimbaṃ ratnamayaṃ dhyāyeddeśikasattamaḥ ॥
ratnanyāsa vidhiḥ proktā netronmīlanamucyate । (3)
netronmīlanam
bimbasya purataḥ kuryāt puṇyāhaṃ tatra deśikaḥ । (i.50b, 3)
triṇetrāṇāṃ tu devānāṃ kalaśatṛtayaṃ nyaset ॥ (4)
dvinetrāṇāṃ tu devānāṃ tatrasyāt kalaśadvayam । (5)
mukhasaṅkhyā nakāryātra netrasaṅkhyaivakāraṇam ॥
netraye ca bhusūrya pāvakān pūjayet kramāt । (6)
netradvau dve candrasūryau pūjayet tatra deśikaḥ ॥
carulājādimaidravyairhomaṃ kumbhavadācaret । (7)
snātāya śilpane tatra vastraṃ devānavaṃ guruḥ ॥
bhūṣaṇaṃ ca pradātavyaṃ ..drākṣādikameva hi । (8)
svarṇādi rajataṃ tatra śilpyāyutha mayaṃ guruḥ ॥
saṃprokṣayecca sahasā puṇyāhodaka sindubhiḥ । (9)
gṛhītvā..yuyaṃ tatra śilpi gurukarāsvayam ॥
bimbasya paritaḥ kṛtvā tiraskaraṇimādarāt । (i.51a)
prathamaṃ phālanetraṃ tu ravinetratataḥ param ॥
anantaraṃ candranetrame(va)munmīlanakramaḥ ।
dvinetrāṇāñca devānāṃ prathamaṃ ravinetrakam ॥ (2)
anantaraṃ candranetramevamunmīlanakramaḥ ।
tathādhikamukhānāntu dhāvannetrāṇi santibhiḥ ॥ (3)
tāvaṃtunmīlaye śilpi dhyāyan devaṃ samāhitaḥ ।
āsyaṃ nāsye śrotrayugmaṃ pāyūpastha tathaiva ca ॥ (4)
nabhinaghāgrakañcaiva navarandhraṃ prakalpayet ।
puṇyāhapañcagavyena bimbaśuddhiṃ samācaret ॥ (5)
pūrvokta kumbhānetreṣu abhiṣicya yathā kramam ।
dhūpadīpādikaṃ datvā yathā dvādaśadarśanam ॥ (6)
ka(kā)rayet buddhīmān tatra deśikasvayameva hi ।
kṣīraṃ ghṛtaṃ madhuṃ tataḥ dhānyaṃ darpaṇameva ca ॥ (7)
gajaṃ dvijān tathā kanyāṃ dīpaṃ jālāṃ tathaiva ca ॥
gāvamārārtikañcaiva yajamānaṃ tataḥ param । (8)
darśanaṃ kārayettatra tattanmantrapurassaram ॥
tiraskaraṇikāṃ tatra sisarja khalu deśikaḥ ।
sarveṣāṃ darśanaṃ samyak ka(kā)rayet bhakti pūrvakam ॥ (9)
ratnanyāsanavidhivannayanonmīlane na ca ।
bimbaṃ saṃpurṇatā yāti nacenaiva prapūrṇatā ॥ (10)
bimbaśodhanam
idānī vakṣyate vatsa bimbaśodhanamatrahi । (i.51a, 10)
nayanonmīlanāt paścāt kuryāt tadbimaśodhanam ॥ (i.51b)
śilpihastabhaveddoṣaḥ jñāyate yena hetunā ।
tenaiva śodhayedvidvān vakṣyamāṇa krameṇa ca ॥ (2)
yathokta lakṣaṇāt boraṃ viṣareghā ghṛtaṃ yadi ।
tatberaṃ tu parityajyamanyaberaṃ prakalpayet ॥ (3)
śilābereṣu savṛtre navanītaṃ vilo(le)payet ।
lohabereśvañjanena lepaṃ kārayet budhaḥ ॥ (4)
evaṃ saṃśodhya berāṇi yathā vidhi gurusvayam ।
gra(grā)hyaṃ nirdoṣaberaṃ ca sadoṣaṃ tu parityajet ॥ (5)
bimbaśuddhiḥ
bimbaśuddhiṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇvidaṃ vatsa sādaram । (i.51b, 5)
viśeṣa laghubhedena sā śuddhirdvividhā matā ॥ (6)
laghuśuddhiṃ pravakṣyāmi vistārāt ṣaṇmukhādhunā । (7)
ciñcāphalena tailenābhiṣekaṃ ca kārayet ॥
puṇyāhavāriṇā pañcagavyena ca tataḥ param । (8)
aśvatthajambūnyagrodhāmaudumbaramahīruham ॥
pañcatvak sāratoyaṃ ca tatra snābhiṃ ca pañcabhiḥ ।
teṣāṃ pūrvokta vṛkṣāṇāṃ pallavāni ca deśikaḥ ॥ (9)
kumbhodake tu sakalaṃ miśrīkṛtya su buddhimān । (10)
japtvā ca saṃhitāmantramabhiṣeka samācaret ॥
dhūpadīpaṃ ca naivedyaṃ dadyāddeśikasattamaḥ ।
jalādhivāsaḥ
jalādhivāsamadhunā vakṣyate vistarādguha । (i.52a)
nṛttavāditra ghoṣeṇa taṭākaṃ vā nadīṃ guruḥ ।
nītvā bimbaṃ toyamadhye maṇṭapaṃ kalpayedbudhaḥ ॥ (2)
athastāt pīṭhamāveśya beraṃ tadupariṃ nyaset ।
pūrvābhimukhamevāthāberaṃ saṃprārthya buddhimān ॥ (3)
strīberaṃ yadi karṇāntaṃ jalaṃ nikṣipya deśikaḥ ।
puṃberaṃ cet stanāntaṃ tu nikṣipeddeśikottamaḥ ॥ (4)
gaṅgādi saptasaritāṃ kumbhān tīre tu pūjayet । (5)
kumbhodakaiśca tairevāhyabhiṣekaṃ samācaret ॥
dhūpa dīpaṃ ca naivedyaṃ dadyādgururatarbhutaḥ । (6)
naye devālayaṃ beraṃ gītavādya purassaram ॥
dhānyādhivāsaḥ
paścāt jalādhivāsasya dhānyavāsaṃ tu kārayet । (i.52a, 7)
dhānyaṃ saṃśodhya vidhivat prokṣya puṇyāhavāribhiḥ ॥ (8)
dhānyesmin karṇadaghne tu strīberaṃ nikṣipetsudhiḥ ।
puṃberaṃ nikṣipettatra stanāntaṃ deśikottamaḥ ॥ (9)
aṣṭapuṣpaṃ samabhyarcya tāmbūlādi nivedayet ।
evaṃ muhūrtaṃ nikṣipya paścādgrāmapradakṣiṇam ॥
grāmapradakṣiṇam
bhūṣaṇairvāsanai puṣpairalaṅkṛtya subuddhimān । (i.52b)
beramāropya śibikāṃ gajam vā gītavādyakaiḥ ॥ (2)
grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ kuryāt tradārārtiṃ samācaret ।
gopuradvārāṇi saṃsthāpya beraṃ dṛṣṭi vimocanam ॥ (3)
kārpāsadyai prakurvīta svayaṃ diśikasattamaḥ ।
tato mahārārtikaṃ ca kṛtvā devālayaṃ nayet ॥ (4)
pañco vā staraṇe vāpi śayanaṃ kārayedbudhaḥ ।
gandhukai rūpayanaiścāraṃ gavai śobhite tathā ॥ (5)
kṣīraṃ pāṇitamevātha phalaṃ naiveddyamācaret । (6)
gītaṃ tatra viśeṣeṇa kārayedbuddhimān guruḥ ॥
padanyāsavidhiḥ
padanyāsaṃ pravakṣyāmi vistāreṇa guhā śṛṇu । (i.52b, 6)
garbhagṛhaṃ viṃśatīya kṛtvā garu rataṃ tritaḥ ॥ (7)
garbhagṛhasya madhye tu sthāpayecchivamevahi । (8)
devasya purato bhāgaḥ daśatyājyaṃ vicakṣaṇaiḥ ॥
liṅgaṃ paścātyabhāgastu vidhi viṣṇuḥ padaṃ bhavet । (9)
tatpaścātya padaṃ vatsa gaṇeśasya tavāpi cā ॥
tatpaścātyaṃ durgāyāḥ lakṣmī pāṇyai samīritam । (10)
tatpaścātyaṃ parityājyaṃ sarvadādi vicakṣaṇaiḥ ॥
tatpaścātyaṃ tu caṇḍeśamukhaṃ bhaktapadaṃ bhavet । (i.53a)
tatpaścātyaṃ maheśasya bhairavādi padaṃ bhavet ॥
tatpaścātyaṃ tu saṃproktaṃ cāmuṇḍādi padaṃ tviti । (2)
kṣudradevādi nilayaṃ tatpaścātpadaṃ bhavet ॥
bhutyevaṃ tu padanyāsaṃ kuryāddeśikasattamaḥ । (3)
pīṭhasthāpanam
śṛṇu vatsamāsena pīṭhasthāpanamucyate । (i.53a, 3)
sthiralagne prakurvīta pīṭhathāpanamuttamam ॥ (4)
puṇyāhaṃyitvaiva pīṭhaśuddhiṃ samācaret ।
sthāpayettatra taṃ devaṃ yasya yadyatpadaṃ smṛtam ॥ (5)
dhūpadīpādīkaṃ datvā dadyānnaivedyamapyathā ।
jaṭiratnanyāsa iti pūrva yatpratipādikam ॥ (6)
tamidāniṃ pravakṣyāmi śruṇuṃ krauñcavibhedinā ।
pīṭhasya madhyadeśe tu navarandhreṣu deśikaḥ ॥ (7)
ratnānivinyaseddhīmān navāpi gurusattamaḥ ॥
ratnanyāsaḥ
madhyarandhre tu māṇikya maindramaratakanyaset । (i.53a, 7)
āgneye khalu vaiḍūryaṃ yāmyosyāndranīlakam ॥ (8)
mautikam niṛte deśe sphāṭikaṃ vāruṇe tathā । (9)
gomedhakaṃ tu vāyavye saumye kīlapravālakam ॥
heśānye vajramākhyātamevaṃ ratnāni vinyaset । (10)
lokapālānaṣṭaranghre madhye brahmāṇameva cā ॥
puṃ beraṃ cetaghānyāya strīberaṃ cetathocyate । (i.53b)
pūjayenmadhyarandhre tu brāhmī diśikasattamaḥ ॥
aṣṭadikṣvādi dikpālapatnī saṃpūjyedguruḥ । (2)
taddevatā mantravarṇānvilikhya svarṇapaṭṭake ॥
tatpaṭṭaṃ vinyaseddhīmān madhyadeśe samāhitaḥ । (3)
nānā vidyaiśca kusumai bilvaiśca svarṇanirmite ॥
nava ranghreṣu vinyasya dhīmān deśikasattamaḥ ॥ (4)
bimbasthāpanam
śubhakāle śubhelagne vedaghneṣa purassaram । (i.53, 4)
brāhmaṇaigītavāditraiḥ ṛtvigbhyaścāpi deśikaḥ ॥ (5)
pīṭhe bibaṃ tu saṃsthāpya silpaśāstrokta mārgataḥ ।
dhūpadīpādikaṃ daddhyāddhyathāśāstraṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥ (6)
pratiṣṭāyā vidhiṃ vakṣye śṛṇu ṣaṇmukhavistarāt ।
trividhā yaḥ pratiṣṭāyāḥ bhedaḥ pūrvokta yeva hi ॥ (7)
idāniṃ tadvidhiṃ vakṣye samāhitamanāśṛṇu ।
brahmotsavo yatra nāsti nāsti pakṣotsavo'pi cā ॥ (8)
yatra nāsti śivārcanāpi tatra ne buddhimānyaset ।
grāme jīrṇālayo nāsti purāṇo vāpinūtanaḥ ॥ (9)
grāme tatra navastavyamātmāna śubhamiccayā ।
jīrṇo devālayo yatra liṅgaṃ vā yatra hīnakam ॥ (10)
pīṭhaṃ bandhahīno vā yatra devālayo bhavet ।
tatra pūjādikaṃ sarvaṃ viphalaṃ parikīrtitam ॥ (i.54a)
grāme naśyati taddoṣā rāṣṭranāśā vinaśyati ।
pīṭhabandhe kṛte samyak tatphalaṃ śṛṇu ṣaṇmukhā ॥ (2)
sarvayāgeṣu yatpuṇyaṃ sarvatīrtheṣu yatphalam ।
sarvadāneṣu yatpuṇyaṃ taḥ sarvabandhanā bhavet ॥ (3)
sarvaroga praśamanaṃ sarvasaubhāgya vardhanam ।
ataḥ sarva prayatnena pīṭhabandhanamācaret ॥ (4)
svarṇena rajatenāpi badhniyātsīsako na cā ।
aṣṭadhādbandhanaṃ proktaṃ budhairāgamapāragaiḥ ॥ (5)
tatrā dātu caturthā syādbandhanaṃ munisaṃmatam ।
ekabandhastribandhaṃ ca pañcabandhamataḥ param ॥ (6)
aṣṭabandhañca kathitaṃ sakalāgamapāragaiḥ ।
tadbandhanaṃ dvidhāṃ proktamapi tvaṃ pakvamityapi ॥
pakvantu pācitaṃ proktaṃ apakvaṃ mardanaṃ bhavet । (7)
pakvapakvamidaṃ jñātvā badhniyāt sudṛḍhaṃ budhaḥ ॥
apakvaṃ devikaṃproktaṃ pakvaṃ mānuṣamucyate । (8)
tadbandhanakramaṃ vakṣye samāhitamanā śṛṇu ॥
svarṇabhūṣā samārepya santarpya jalavadguhā । (9)
tat pīṭha paritaḥ samyak nirandhraṃ nikṣipedbudhaḥ ॥
caturdikṣu samaṃ kuryād nyūnādhikameva hi । (10)
talliṅga pīṭhaparitaḥ aṅgulīyakavadguhā ॥
ninmi kuryādāyudhena liṅgāligitamevahi । (i.54b)
atha meva svarṇabandhaḥ pakvabhutyucyate budhaiḥ ॥
athā pakvasvarṇabandhaḥ procyate śraddhayā śṛṇu । (2)
drutu svarṇamādāya tūlavattāḍayenmahuḥ ॥
samyak santāḍyate naiva veṣṭayetparitaḥ śivam । (3)
antarbahi caturdikṣu gharṣayedāyudhena hu ॥
talliṅgaparitaḥ samyak snigdhi kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ । (4)
apakva svarṇa bandho'yaṃ kathitaśāstrayedibhiḥ ॥
ayameva kramaḥ proktaḥ apharājatasīsayoḥ ।
athaikabandhavidhiṃ vakṣye śṛṇu ṣaḍānanā ॥ (5)
ekabhāgaṃ kundariṣkaṃ tadardhaṃ tailamīritam ।
miśrikṛtyobhayaṃ samyak āpyagnau pācayedbudhaiḥ ॥ (6)
badhniyācca tadādāya caitatpakvaṃ samīritam ।
jalena kundariṣkaṃ tu miśrikṛtya sabuddhimān ॥ (7)
śilāyāṃ tu śilākhaṇḍād gharṣayet prathamaṃ budhaḥ ।
anantaraṃ ca tailena pūrvavaddharṣayet kramāt ॥ (8)
snigdhikṛtya ca tatpiṇḍādbadhniyā pīṭhamādarāt ।
evameva prakāreṇā pakve bandhasamīritaḥ ॥ (9)
athocyate tribandhasya vidhānaṃ śāstrasammatam ।
śarkarākundariṣkaṃ ca navanītamitikramaḥ ॥ (10)
dvibhāgaśarkarā proktā tribhāgaḥ kundariṣkakam ।
dvibhāge nanītaṃ tailaṃ vā parikīrtitam ॥ (i.55a)
miśraitatrayaṃ samyagagnau santarpya buddhimān ।
tenaiva bandhanaṃ kuryādetat pakva tribandhanam ॥ (2)
pūrvoktavastu tṛtayaṃ śilāyāṃ gharṣayedbudhaḥ ।
tenaiva pakvabandhenañcaitadapakvaṃ tu tribandhanam ॥ (3)
kramādanantaraṃ vatsa pañcabandhanamucyate ।
kāṣāyaṃ śarkarālākṣa kundariṣkaṃ ca kīrtitam ॥ (4)
tailaṃ vā navanītaṃ vā syādetpakvaṃ ca bandhanam ।
lākṣā ca nava nītaṃ ca tripādo bhāgaraṃ ridaḥ ॥ (5)
ekabhāgastu kāṣāyaṃ śarkarāpi tatho vahi ।
dvibhāgaḥ kundariṣkaṃ syādayaṃ bhāgakramasmṛtaḥ ॥ (6)
agnau santarpya pūrvoktaṃ vastu pañcakamādarāt ।
tenaiva bandhanaṃ kuryāt pakvasya pañcabandhanam ॥ (7)
śilāyā gharṣaye samyak pūrvoktaṃ vastupañcakaṃ ।
tenaiva bandhanaṃ kuryādapakvamidamucyate ॥ (8)
idāniṃ maṣṭabandhaṃ sya saṃpradāya pravakṣyate ।
śarkarā raktakārpāsaṃ kāṣāyaṃ kulndariṣkakam ॥ (9)
madhuściṣṭaṃ ca lākṣā ca navanītaṃ ca hiṃ gulam ।
tribhāgaṃ śarkarā proktā kundariṣkaṃ tathaiva hi ॥ (10)
ekabhākastu lākṣāsya tadardhaṃ tūlamucyate ।
dvibhāgastu madhuściṣṭaṃ kāṣāyaṃ tu tadardhakam ॥ (i.55b)
tathaiva navanītaṃ syārghiṅgulaṃ pāducyate ।
ayaṃ bhāgakramaḥ proktaḥ pūrverāgamakovidaiḥ ॥ (2)
etatsarvamardayitvo lūkhale musalenahi ।
etanmardanakālastu daśayāmā prakīrtitaḥ ॥ (3)
athavānyaprakāreṇa procyate cāṣṭabandhanā ।
dvibhāgaśarkarā proktā tribhāgaḥ kundariṣkakam ॥ (4)
ekabhāgastu lākṣā syāt kāṣāyaṃ ca tripādakam ।
ekabhāko madhuściṣṭaṃ tadardhaṃ raktatūlakam ॥ (5)
navanītaṃ tripādaṃ ca hiḍgulaṃ cārdhamucyate ।
hiṅgulena vihīnaṃ tu bandhanaṃ balahīnakam ॥
etatsarvaṃ mardayetvo lūkhale musalena hi । (6)
bandhanaṃ tu prakurvīta yathāśāstraṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥
etanmardanakālastu śatayāmāḥ prakīrtitaḥ । (7)
śatayāmaṃ mardayitvā paścadbandhanamācaret ॥
paścāddhiṃ gulacūrnena lepayedbandhanopari । (8)
pūrvoktānāṃ tu bandhānāmāyurdāyu pravakṣyate ॥
āyuḥ suvarṇabandhasya śatasaṃvatsarāsmṛtaḥ । (9)
rajatasya tadardhaṃ syādāyurdāyaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ॥
tadardhaṃ sīsabandhasya payoniścitamucyate । (10)
tadardhaṃ maṣṭabandhaṃ syāpayoḥ proktaṃ vicakṣaṇai ॥
bālālayapratiṣṭhāyāmrkabandhādi kīrtitam । (i.56a)
pūrvoktānāṃ tu bandhānāmāyurmadhye tu bandhanam ॥
bhinnaṃ bhavati ce kiñcit punarbandhanamācaret । (2)
tadevabandhanaṃ loke sadyobandha itīyyate ॥
bandhastu trividhaproktaḥ pūrverāgamapāragaiḥ । (3)
sadyodbandhaḥ kramādbandhaḥ bandhaścātyantikasyathā ॥
sadyebandhastu kathitaḥ kramādbandha ihocyate ।
aṅguṣṭādhika vicchihne kramādbandhaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ॥ (4)
parito bandhahīne tu bandhasyātyatrikasmṛtaḥ ।
vinaivakarṣaṇaṃ vidvān sadyobandhanamācaret ॥ (5)
prāyaścitta krameṇeti prāhurāgamapāragāḥ ।
kṛtvaivākarṣaṇaṃ vidvān kramādbandhanamācaret ॥ (6)
balālayaṃ cātra nāstityāhurāgamakovidāḥ । (7)
śilpi doṣa viśudhyarthaṃ vāstuśānti punaścaret ॥
bālālayakrameṇaiva bandhamātyantikaṃ caret । (8)
bālālaya pratiṣṭāyāścāyurbheda bhuhocyate ॥
ekamāsastu kumbhe syāt khaḍgemāsadvayaṃ smṛtam ।
ṣāṇmāsaṃ darpaṇe proktāḥ paṭasaṃvatsarasmṛtaḥ ॥ (9)
trivarṣadāriṇī proktamanyatra dvādaśāsmṛtaḥ ।
kumbhādanyatra khaḍgādau devaścāvāhito yadi ॥ (10)
yāgakramastathā nāstityāhurevaṃ manīṣiṇaḥ ।
vatsatsaratvekabandhasya tṛbandhasya trivarṣakam ॥ (i.56b)
ṣaḍvarṣā pañcabandhasya caivamāyuḥ prakīrtitam ।
bālālayaṃ śilāyāṃ cedṛ āyurdvādaśavatsaraḥ ॥ (2)
bālālaye yadā ligaṃ kartavyaṃ dāruṇā yadi ।
nyagrodhavakulāśvardha punnāgaudumbaraidrumaiḥ ॥
nirmāya liṅgaṃ vidhivatsudhirākarṣaṇaṃ caret । (3)
ākarṣaṇa śilāyāṃ ced bāṇāliṅge samācaret ॥
sakalānāṃ tu berāṇāmākarṣaṇa vidhau guhā । (4)
paṭhaṃ vā yadi vā bimbe pūrvoktaivastubhiścaret ॥
citralekhanamatrāhuḥ paṭheryānāṃ tu rodhataḥ । (5)
atra pūjāvidhau kiñcidviśeṣosti sakathyate ॥ (6)
citrāṇāmabhiṣekastu kuryā bhānayā sudhiḥ ।
bālālayapratiṣṭā tu dvādaśābdāṃ tamevahi ॥ (7)
yāgaśālānirmāṇam
lakṣaṇaṃ yāgaśālāyāḥ vakṣyate śṛṇu ṣaṇmukhā । (i.56b, 7)
yāgāyamaṇṭapaṃ tatra prāsādevātha pārśvakoḥ ॥ (8)
aindre vā pāvake vāpi yāmye vā rākṣasepi vā ।
vāruṇe vāyubhāge vā some ceśānakopi vā ॥ (9)
ālasyekakoṇe vā yāgakṣetraṃ praśasya te ।
devi yāgapradeśopi pūrvoktenaiva vartmanā ॥ (10)
athavā śivaśālāyāḥ vāmabhāge viniścitaḥ ।
ālasya vinirdeśe yāgaśālā bhavedyadi ॥
tadāgraśodhanaṃ samyakkartavyamiti niścitaḥ । (i.57a)
ālasyāntare yatra yāgaśālā viracyate ॥
tatra bhū śodhanaṃ naiva kartavyamiti niścayaḥ । (2)
bhūmiṃ saṃśodhaye samyaghastadaghnaṃ ṣaḍānanā ॥
tathaikahastetseyaṃ ca caturaśru prakīrtitam । (3)
uttamaṃ manusūtraṃ syāt madhyamaṃ ravisūtrakam ॥
adhamaṃ daśasūtraṃ cetyevaṃ syāt sūtranirṇayaḥ । (4)
manusūtraśca hastāśca proktāścaikoṇa viṃśatiḥ ॥
ravisūtrasya hastāśca proktā saptadaśeti cā । (5)
daśasūtrasya śāstresmin hastā pañcadaśasmṛtāḥ ॥
pūrvokta sūtra tṛtaye hastāsyuḥ sāntarālakāḥ । (6)
vedi vistāratulyaṃ tadantarālamihānta vā ॥
tadaryaṃ vāntarālaṃ syādyathāsambhavamucyate । (7)
prāk paścimavidhānena manu saṅkhyākasūtrakam ॥
tathaiva sūtrasaṅkhyātaṃ dakṣiṇottarameva cā । (8)
caturaśraṃ prakurvīta maghālayamataḥ param ॥
padamekonaṣṭottaraṃ śataṃ ca samirīritam ।
sarveṣāṃ ca padānāṃ tu viniyogātha kathyate ॥ (9)
madhye navapadaṃ vedi pradhānaṃ tatra vardhanim ।
madhyakoṣṭe nyaseddeva yathāśāstraṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥ (10)
tadbāhye cāṣṭavidyeśān tadbahiḥ ṣoḍaśaṃ padam ।
pūjāsthānaṃ caturdikṣu vidikṣu stambhamucyate ॥ (i.57b)
śiṣṭe cāṣṭapade proktamaṣṭamaṅagalamatrahi ।
sañcārārthapadaṃ proktaṃ caturviṃśati tadbahi ॥ (2)
dvātriṃśat kuṇḍakoṣṭāni tadbahi procyate budhaiḥ ।
tadbahiśca padaṃ proktaṃ catvāriṃśadvicakṣaṇaiḥ ॥ (3)
padeśveteṣu kartavyamaṅkurasthāpanaṃ budhaiḥ ।
astraṃ ca pañcagavyaṃ ca caruṃ ca parikīrtitāḥ ॥
śiṣṭaḥ tṛpadaṃ sarvamācāryapadamucyate । (4)
tadbahiścāṣṭacatvāri padaṃ tatprocyate budhaiḥ ॥
saptaviṃśati kumbhāśca vinyasettatra deśikaḥ । (5)
stambhādvā daśasaṃkhyākān caturdikṣu ca vinyaset ॥
śiṣṭaviṃśatikoṣṭeṣu caturdikṣu samaṃ budhaḥ । (6)
caturdvāraṃ prakurvīta yathāśāstraṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥
tadbahiścāntarālaṃ syāccaturdikṣu prakīrtitam । (7)
yetāvatā ca grandhena manusūtraṃ prakīrtitam ॥
ravisūtrakramaḥ
pūrvāgraṃ ravisūtraṃ syāduttarāgraṃ tathaiva cā । (i.57b, 8)
ekaviṃśādhikaśataṃ padabhāgasamīritaḥ ॥
madhye cekapadaṃ brāhme pradhānaṃ vardhanī tathā । (9)
devikoṣṭapade kumbhān paiśācaṃ ṣoḍaśaṃ bhavet ॥
pūjārthaṃ caturdikṣu vidikṣu stambhamucyate । (10)
śiṣṭe cāṣṭapade tu syādaṣṭamaṅgalamīritam ॥
ṣaṭ pañcāśattato bāhye mānuṣaṃ padamīritam । (i.58a)
koṇe koṇe viśeṣeṇa vidhivadvidhayācaret ॥
pūrvādi navako madhye tvaṣṭakuṇḍaṃ tu kārayet । (2)
rudrā khaṇḍalayormadhye kuryādvṛttaṃ pradhānakam ॥
catvāriśatpade bāhye rākṣase ghaṭatoraṇam । (3)
ācāryaśiṣyasañcārayogya śiṣṭapadaṃ bhavet ॥
stambhastānaṃ padādbāhye ityevaṃ pada saṃstitaḥ ॥ (4)
daśasūtra kramaḥ
pūrvādi daśasūtraṃ syā tathā sadakṣiṇotrakam । (i.58a, 4)
ekāśīti padanyāsān viniyogāśca kathyate ॥ (5)
madhye navapadaṃ vedi tadbahiścāṣṭamaṅgalam ।
tadbahi kuṇḍakoṣṭāni tadbahi dvāramucyate ॥ (6)
athavānyaprakāreṇa maṇḍapaṃ krama ucyate ।
navahastādadhastā cedṛ evaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ ॥ (7)
yāgakṣetraṃ tridhā kṛtvā madhyabhāge tu vedikā ।
tadbahiśca tṛdhā kṛtvā tanmadhye kuṇḍakalpanam ॥ (8)
ṣoḍaśastambhasaṃyuktaṃ caturdvārasamanvitam ।
vediṃ caturdikṣu sudhi catustambhā ca vinyaset ॥ (9)
maṇṭapādbahiratrasya dvādaśātstambha kalpanam ।
vedikālakṣaṇaṃ vakṣye śṛṇu ṣaṇmukha sodaram ॥
daivaṃ ca koṣṭa navakaṃ paiśācaṃ ṣoḍaśaṃ padam । (10)
āhatya pañcaviṃśatyā koṣṭarvediṃ prakalpayet ॥
ekahasta samutsedhaṃ tāvadvistāramucyate । (i.58b)
hastatrayasya vistāraṃ mupavediṃ prakalpayet ॥
tadūrdhvamupavediṃ ca kuryadhasta dvayādhikā । (2)
ṣaḍaṅgulasamutsedhaṃ tavadvistāramucyate ॥
mūlapaṭyāgra paṭṭībhyāṃ saṃyuktaṃ tatra kalpayet । (3)
athavānya prakāreṇa vedilakṣaṇamucyate ॥
vistāratulya samutsedhamathavāpi tadardhakam ।
mukurodaka saṃkāsaṃ vedikālakṣaṇaṃ vidum ॥ (4)
vedi pīṭhayutā samyaggupavedi bhavedyati ।
mahāvedīti vijñāyā sā sarvāgamakovidaiḥ ॥ (5)
vedī pīṭhavihīnā sā loke sāmānya vedikā ॥ (6)
kuṇḍa lakṣaṇam
vatsa skandasamāsena vakṣyatekuṇḍalakṣaṇam । (i.58b, 6)
navāgnyāyatane cāṣṭadikṣu kuṇḍaprakalpanam ॥ (7)
indreśānayormadhye pradhānaṃ kuṇḍamucyate ।
pañcāgnyāyatane kuṇḍaṃ caturdikṣu prakalpayet ॥ (8)
pradhāne ceśadigbhāge kuṇḍaṃ tu parikīrtitam ।
ekāgnyāyatane kuṇḍaṃ vedyāḥ paścimato nyaset ॥ (9)
caturaśracendradiśi cāgneyāstu bhagā kṛti ।
dakṣiṇe cārdhacandrākhyaṃ naiṛtyāṃ tu trikoṇakam ॥ (10)
vāruṇe vartulākāraṃ vāyavye tu ṣaḍaśrakam ।
saumye padmākṛti proktamīśe cāṣṭāśrakuṇḍakam ॥ (i.59a)
indreśānayormadhye vṛttakuṇḍaṃ pradhānakam ।
yathā kuṇḍākṛtiḥ proktā pradhānaṃ kuṇḍamucyate ॥ (2)
caturaśraṃ prakurvīta kuṇḍakṣetraṃ hi deśikaḥ ।
pūrvādi paścimāntaṃ ca dakṣiṇādyuttarāntakam ॥ (3)
ekasūtraṃ tu vinyasya madhye vidvānataṃtritaḥ ।
tatsūtraṃ hastamānaṃ syāttaccadvi dvādaśāṅgulam ॥ (4)
matsyaṃ vanyasyāṣṭadikṣu teṣu sūtraṃ catuṣṭayam ।
ṣaṇṇāmamīṣā sūtrāṇāmasītpadacatuṣṭayam ॥
ṣaṇṇavatyā gulāyāmaṃ sarvaṃ kuṇḍabhavedguhā । (5)
ekahastamekhalāntaṃ ghātapañca prakīrtitam ॥ (6)
navāṅgulotsedhamatra meghalā tṛtayaṃ bhavet ।
taddhāstātkramāt proktaṃ ghātaṃ pañcadaśāṅgulam ॥ (7)
meghalāntya pradeśetu navamāgulasīmahi ।
bhūmerūrdhvaṃ prakurvīta kaṇṭhamekāṅgulaṃ sudhi ॥ (8)
caturagulavistārā kīrtitā cordhvamekhalā ।
aṅguladvayavistārā madhyamā meghalā smṛtā ॥ (9)
aṅguladvayavistārā kīrtyate cāntya mekhalā ।
taterdhve mekhalāyāṃ tu dakṣiṇe paścime'pi vā ॥ (10)
yoni prakalpayedvidvān dvādaśāgulamāyatam ।
aṣṭāṅgulāyāmamandū dīdamaśvartha patravat ॥ (i.59b)
agraṃ bhave kaṇṭhagata ārabhyerdhvaga mekhalām ।
ekāṅgulotsedhamidaṃ yoniṃ kalpamucyate ॥ (2)
kuṇḍamadhye bhavetpadmā sāṣapatraṃ sakarṇikam ।
dvādaśāṅgulamāyāmaṃ karṇikā caturaṅgulam ॥
caturaṅgulamutsedhaṃ karṇikā ca dalāni cā ॥ (3)
karṇikā mūlamānaṃ tu dvyaṅgulaṃ nāhamucyate ।
caturaśrākhya kuṇḍasya caitalakṣaṇamīritam ॥ (4)
dvibhāgaṃ tu kṛtaṃ kṣetraṃ mūlamagramitīyyate ।
takṣetraṃ pañcadhā kṛtvā caikāṃśo bahiragrataḥ ॥ (5)
mūlabhāgaṃ dvidhākṛtvā dakṣiṇaṃ savyamucyate ।
karṇasūtraprakurvīta dakṣe vāmapade'pi cā ॥ (6)
śrṛṅgeṇa bhrāmayedyugmamardhakṣetrāntapārśvayoḥ ।
tadārabhyaṃ tu sūtrābhyāṃ bahirekāṃśayojanam ॥ (7)
idaṃ ca yonikuṇḍākhyaṃ vipralasya talākṛtim ।
navabhāgakṛtakṣetramardhacandraṃ prakalpayet ॥ (8)
mūle cāgre padavaṃdvaṃ vihāya bhramaṇaṃ caret ।
etaccadhanarākārakuṇḍamityucyate budhaiḥ ॥ (9)
ṣaḍbhāgaṃ tu kṛtaṃ kṣetraṃ dvibhāge pārśvayo nyaset ।
ekabhāgaṃ tadagre tu mūlātsāt sāt sūtrayugmakaḥ ॥ (10)
tatsūtraṃ yodayedagre trisūtraṃ tatrikoṇakam ।
navabhāgakṛtaṃ kṣetramaṣṭakoṇa vyapohya cā ॥ (i.60a)
śiṣṭakoṇaṃ paribhrāmya kuṇḍaṃ vṛttanibhaṃ bhavet ।
ṣaḍbhāgaṃ tu kṛtaṃ kṣetra dvibhāgaṃ cāgramūlayoḥ ॥ (2)
ekabhāgaṃ dvidhākṛtvā vinyaseddakṣiṇottaram ।
ṣaṣṭikoṇākhyamidaṃ proktaṃ kāmikāgama ko vidaiḥ ॥ (3)
vṛttavatpadmakuṇḍākhyaṃ bahiraṣṭadalaṃ smṛtam ।
karṇikā vedavistāramunnataṃ tu tadardhakam ॥ (4)
pañcāṅguladalaṃ dīrghamunnataṃ caikamaṅgalam ।
mekhalādvyaṅgulaṃ proktaṃ kuṇḍaṃ padmanibhaṃ bhavet ॥ (5)
aṣṭabhāgakṛtaṃ kṣetramekabhāgaṃ bahirnyaset ।
koṇe koṇe ca madhye tu karṇasūtraṃ padatraye ॥ (6)
sūtramekadaśaprokta kuṇḍamaṣṭāśrakaṃ bhave ।
padme nābhīn kartavyonau yoniṃ na kalpayet ॥ (7)
itareṣā ca kuṇḍānāṃ nābhiṃ yoniṃ ca kālayet ।
yāgaśālācaturdikṣu bhūpuraṃ tu prakalpayet ॥ (8)
hastamātraṃ tu vistāramunnataṃ ca ṣadaṅgulam ।
vāyavyeśāna naiṛtyāṃ vediṃ tṛtayakalpanam ॥ (9)
āgneya chullimātraṃ tu kalpayeddeśikottamaḥ ।
utsedhaṃ bhūpurasamaṃ vistaraṃ cekahastakam ॥
kuṇḍavedimaṇṭapānāmitamuktaṃ hi lakṣaṇam । (10)
kuṇḍanirmāṇataḥ pūrvaṃ takṣetre brahmabhojanam ॥
kṛtvoścāṣaṃ samudvāsya paścātpuṇyāhamācaret ॥ (i.60b)
sṛksṛva lakṣaṇam
sṛksṛva lakṣaṇaṃ vakṣe śṛṇu ṣaṇmukha sādaram । (i.60b)
navabhistarubhiścaiva srusruvāṇā prakalpanam ॥ (2)
vaṭāśvatthapalāśa ca jabvodumbaracūtakaḥ ।
plakṣaṃ śrīvṛkṣakhadirāstaravo nava kīrtitāḥ ॥ (3)
palāśaudumbaravaṭāśvatthai syā sruk ccatuṣṭayam ।
sru catuṣkamidaṃ vatsamagra vaktranibhaṃ bhavet ॥ (4)
plakṣakhādirajambūcūtakaisyā śrukccatuṣṭayam ।
etaccatuṣṭayaṃ vatsa kalpayedyāli sannibham ॥
gajāśca bilvataścaikaṃ kuryāddhīmān na tantritaḥ । (5)
yena yena ca vṛkṣeṇa kṛyate srugvicakṣaṇaḥ ॥
tadvidhenaiva khaṇḍena sruvaṃ kuryāt vicakṣaṇaḥ । (6)
srucāṃ ca navakaṃ tatra sruvāṇāṃ navakaṃ tathā ।
yathā yāgaṃ sru sruvau ca tattatkuṇḍanibhaṃ bhavet ॥ (7)
pūrvoktānāṃ ca vṛkṣāṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ kathyate'dhunā । (8)
grāhyaṃ ca taravo vṛddhā krami koṭaravarjitāḥ ॥
mūlāgraṃ ca vijñāya sruksruvau kalpayedbudhaḥ ।
ṣaṭ triṃśāṃguladīrghaṃ tu sruglakṣaṇamidaṃ jaguḥ ॥ (9)
guṇḍivedāṅgulaṃ proktaṃ daṇḍo mārtāṇḍamānataḥ ।
vedivedāṅgulaṃ proktaṃ kaṇṭhamekāṅgulaṃ bhavet ॥ (10)
vasvaṅgulā mahāvedi madhye syādvedikaṇṭhayoḥ ॥
mukhaṃ saptāṅgulaṃ proktaṃ sarvairagamako vidaiḥ । (i.61a)
mahāvedī pṛṣṭabhāge vedi dvitayamucyate ॥
tadadhaḥ padmamākhyātaṃ tadūrdhvaṃ ca tathaiva hi । (2)
mahāvedi kartamuktamāyāmaṃ cāṅgulatrayam ॥
dhātaḥ pañcāgulaṃ proktaṃ paṭṭi pādaṅgulaṃ bhavet । (3)
gartāgraṃ karṇikātulyaṃ tadbahi sāṣṭapatrakam ॥
pādāgulasamutsedhaṃ gattapaṭṭi samaṃ bhavet । (4)
caturaśraṃ prakurvīta kaṇṭhaṃ vediṃ ca deśikaḥ ॥
rasāṅgulaṃ daṇḍanāhaṃ gaṇḍyāgraṃ caturaṅgulam ।
ghṛtadhāra mārgamānaṃ kaniṣṭā gulamātrakam ॥ (5)
yālīmukhaṃ gajāsyaṃ ca mṛgāsyaṃ kalpayedbudhaḥ ।
mahāsena śrṛṇuṣvādya sruvalakṣaṇamucyate ॥ (6)
caturviṃśatyāṅgulaṃ tattaddīrghamuktaṃ vicakṣaṇaiḥ ।
aṅgulatrayamagraṃ syādāyāmaṃ ca tathaiva hi ॥ (7)
kaṇṭhamekāṅgulaṃ daṇḍaṃ ṣoḍaśāṅgulamucyate ।
ekāṅgulaṃ mūla kaṇṭhamūlaṃ tryaṅgulamucyate ॥ (8)
kaṇṭhadvayasya nāhaṃ syādekāṅgulamitīrayate ।
aṅguṣṭāgulamātraṃ syādaṇḍanāhamidaṃ guha ॥ (9)
gartaṃ gopuravatkāryaṃ pṛṣṭaṃ tu gajaśīrṣavat ।
nyagradhodumbarāśvattha plakṣaistoraṇamācaret ॥ (10)
eṣu vṛkṣeṣu pratyekamekaikaṃ teraṇaṃ caret ।
pañcahastasamutsedhaṃ vistāraṃ tu trihastakam ॥ (i.61b)
phalakasya samutsedhaṃ triyaṅgulaṃ samudīritam ।
ṣaḍaṅgulaṃ tu vistāraṃ caturṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ samam ॥ (2)
ūrdhvaśūlatrayaṃ proktaṃ tathā madhye ca pārśvayoḥ ।
launnatyantataḥ proktaṃ navāṅgulamiti sphuṭam ॥ (3)
tat dhanadvayaṅgulyaṃ proktatoraṇalakṣaṇam ।
kūrmāsanasya dīrghaṃ tu hastamātramitīryate ॥ (4)
vistāraṃ tatsamaṃ proktamāsyaṃ vedāṅgulaṃ bhavet ।
trayaṅgulaṃ catuṣpādaṃ pucchādyaṅgulamucyate ॥ (5)
vistāraṃ pañcadhā kṛtvā caikāṃśaṃ taddhanaṃ bhavet । (6)
āsya vyālākṛti proktamiti kūrmasya lakṣaṇam ॥
urvaśī menakārambhā sumukhī ca tilottamā । (7)
sundarī kāmukī kāmavardhanītyaṣṭadhā striyaḥ ॥
dvātriṃśadaṅgulaṃ cāsāṃprati maunnatyamucyate ।
yathā śāstrī lakṣaṇaṃ samyak pratimā kalpanaṃ caret ॥ (8)
darpaṇaṃ pūrṇakumbhaṃ ca vṛṣabhaṃ yugmacāmaram ।
śrīvatsaṃ svastikaṃ śaṅkhaṃ dīpaṃ caivāṣṭamaṅgalam ॥ (9)
urvaśyāṃ darpaṇaṃ proktaṃ menakā pūrṇakumbhakam ।
rambhayā vṛṣabhaḥ prokta cāmaraṃ tu tilottamā ॥ (10)
śrīvatsaṃ sumukhī proktaṃ svastikaṃ sundarī tathā ।
śaṅkhaṃ tu kāmukhī proktaṃ dīpaṃ syāt kāmavardhanī ॥ (i.62a)
haste pūrvoktanārīṇāṃ darpaṇādikamucyate ।
vajraśaktiṃ ca daṇḍaṃ ca khaḍgaṃ pāśaṃ dhvaja gadā ॥ (2)
triśūlī padmacakraṃ ca vasyedye taddaśāyudhaḥ ।
etadāyudhavoḍāraḥ pratimā rūpiṇo daśa ॥ (3)
tattadikpālavarṇāsyuḥ śironyastadaśāyudhaḥ ।
hṛdayañjalinaḥ sarve dvibhujāḥ patikīrtitāḥ ॥
pratimā kṛtyābhāve tu citralekhanamācaret । (4)
pūrvoktatoraṇādīnaṃ deśikāṅgulimānataḥ ॥
nirmaṇāṃ kārayettatra sarveṣāmapi deśikaḥ ॥ (5)
yāgaśālaṅkāram
alaṅkuryādyāgaśālā yathā śāstraṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ । (i.62a, 5)
yāgaśālā caturdikṣu catustoraṇamācaret ॥ (6)
pūrvadvāre tu nyagrodhaṃ yame caudumbaraṃ tathā ।
aśvatthamuttaradvāre paścime plakṣameva hi ॥ (7)
dvārordhvaṃ ta caturdikṣu dhvajānaṣṭau vinikṣipet ।
tatpārśvayo ṣoḍaśakān dhvajān sūkṣmani nikṣipet ॥ (8)
dvādaśe vā sṛṣṭiḥbadhnīyāt stambhapārśvayoḥ ।
dhvajaśāṭīṣvaṣṭagajān mahokṣān kṣullakeṣu cā ॥ (9)
vilikhed dviliyai varṇaiḥ suraṭīṣu ca paṅkajam ।
bṛhadhvajānāmaṣṭānāṃ vastrabhedastu kathyate ॥
pūrvadhvajaṃ nīlavastramāgneryā raktacailakam । (10)
kṛṣṇacelaṃ tu yāmye syānnaiṛtyāṃdhūmrame śukam ॥
vāruṇeśaṅkhaśāṭī syādvāyavye śabalaḥ paṭaḥ । (i.62b)
saumye svarṇavasanaṃ ceśāne pītavastrakam ॥
ūrdhva vedyā vitānaṃ syāccaturdikṣu taraṅgakam । (2)
vitāne caiva padmaṃ ca sahasradalasamyutam ॥
vedī caturdikṣu paraṃ taraṅgaṃ kalpayetsudhiḥ ।
mahāvedyūrdhvapīṭhe ca taraṅgaṃ kalpayetsudhiḥ ॥ (3)
dvārapārśve tu sarvatra kadalī nālīkerakai ।
kramukairikṣubhiścaiva kharjūraisaphalai tathā ॥ (4)
darbhamālā cūtapatrairnānā varṇai prasūnakai ।
tadbāhiścāntarāleṣu śākairnānā vidhairapi ॥ (5)
payedikālābumukhaiḥ kūṣmāṇḍai phanasairapi ।
cūtavārtāgajambīrai śākhairanyai vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥ (6)
kaṇiśorvividhaiścāpi dīpai nānā vidhairapi ।
alaṅkṛtya sudhistatra paścādyāgaṃ samācaret ॥ (7)
dhanabhūṣaṇa vastrādyai tvoṣādhitvaiva śilpinam ।
toṣayitvā tu tatpaścād vāstuśāntiṃ samācaret ॥ (8)
vāstuśāntikramaḥ
atha vakṣye viśeṣeṇa vāstuśāntimanuttamam । (i.62b, 8)
pratiṣṭā prokṣaṇe kāle cotsavenyatra maṅgale ॥ (9)
sarvadoṣa nivṛtyarthaṃ vāstuśāntiṃ samācaret ।
gopurādbāhya deśe tu pīṭhāgre yāgamaṇṭape ॥ (10)
eteṣu yatra kutrāpi vāstuśāntyarthamādarāt ।
nirmāya maṇṭapaṃ tatra vāstuśāntiṃ samācaret ॥ (11)
grāmaśāntau maṇṭapasya pūrvamuktaṃ hi lakṣaṇam ।
tadvanmaṇṭapanirmāṇaṃ kuryādatrāvideśikaḥ ॥
yāvṛto vāstuśāntyartham ācārya subuddhimān । (i.63a)
astradevai tu sahitaḥ gītavāditra samyutaḥ ॥
vāstumaṇṭapamāsādya caiśānye cāstradevatāḥ । (2)
nikṣipya kṛtya puṇyāhaṃ pūrvavaccalyaśodhanam ॥
dvidhā proktaṃ vāstu cakraṃ nikhilāgamapāragaiḥ । (3)
maṇḍūkapadamityeva tathā paramaśādhikam ॥
śivādi sarvadevānāṃ maṇḍūkapadakalpanam । (4)
sarvāsāmapi śaktīnāṃ padaṃ paramaśācikam ॥
maṇḍūkasya vidhiṃ vakṣye śṛṇu ṣaṇmukhasādaram ।
navasūtraṃ prakurvīta catuḥṣaṣṭipadaṃ bhavet ॥ (5)
karṇasūtradvayaṃ kṛtvā koṇāgre yatpadaṃ bhavet ।
tatpārśve ca pade karṇasūtraṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ ॥ (6)
karṇasūtrābhya deśābhyāṃ vaṃśadvayamathācaret । (7)
padmadvādaśakopeta koṇaśūlacatuṣṭayam ॥
pūrvādiṣu caturdikṣu vṛttaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ । (8)
tanmadhye padmamālikhya sāṣṭapatraṃ sakarṇikam ॥
vidhidvaye ṣaṭpadaṃ tu miśrīkṛtya vicakṣaṇaḥ । (9)
ityetadvidhivadgrāhyaṃ maṇḍūkapada lakṣaṇam ॥
athocyate paramaśāyi padalakṣaṇamādarāt । (10)
pūrvādi paścimāntaṃ tu dakṣiṇādyuttarāntakam ॥
daśasūtraṃ prakurvīta ekāśīti padaṃ bhavet ।
pūrvavatkarṇasūtrāṇi vaṃśarekhā catuṣṭayam ॥ (11)
varmaṇā viṃśatiṃ caiva kalpayeddeśako .......... ।
madhye navapadaṃ padmaṃ sāṣṭapatraṃ sakarṇikam ॥ (i.63b)
aṣṭadikṣvapi śūlaṃ syāddaṣṭakoṇe tu miśritam ।
etaddeva hi kālasmin tyucyate paramaśāyikam ॥ (2)
brahmaṇā sakalīkṛtya bhūtaśuddhiṃ samācaret ।
cakravastu samabhyarcya cakrādho daśasaṃsthitam ॥ (3)
lakṣmī yaśo patīkānta supriyā vimalā śivā ।
subhagā sumatī sārā sūtra pūrvādiṣu sthitaḥ ॥
dhanyā prāṇaviśālāṃ ca sthirā bhadrā jayārajā । (4)
vibhavā cetanā caiva rakṣasūtrādi pūjāyet ॥
etat śaktiśca maṇḍūkaṃ padasūtreṣu pūjayet । (5)
śāntī yaśovatī kāntavimalā prāṇanāhinī ।
satrī ca sumatī nandasubhadrācamanoramā ॥ (6)
pūrvādi daśasūtreṣu arcayecca yathākramam ।
hiraṇyāsu prbhā lakṣmī vibhūti vimalāsthirā ॥ (7)
dharā jvālā viśokāśca iḍāsyādyuttarādiṣu ।
evaṃ vidhīti saṃkhyākāḥ cakre paramaśāyike ॥ (8)
kīlinī madanī caiva suvṛttā cā nivṛttakā ।
sadbhāvā rājanī caiva pakṣiṇī yakṣiṇī tathā ॥ (9)
kīleṣu cārcayeddhīmān vaṃśe durjaya durdharau ।
he devān sandhyatāṃ vāstupuruṣaṃ ca śivājñayā ॥
padme kūrcaṃ tu vinyasya brahmāṇaṃ tatra pūjayet । (10)
anantādīnaṣṭadaleṣvarcayedvidhivadguruḥ ॥
marīciṃ ca vivasvāntaṃ pitraṃ cāpi dharādharam । (11)
pade miśre ca pūrvādau pūrvoktaṃ vārcayetsudhi ॥
āpamāvakavatsaṃ ca savitraṃ sāvitrakaṃ tathā । (i.64a)
indramijayaṃ caiva rudraṃ tadu samarpyathā ॥
koṇeṣu vipade tatra caturdikṣvapi pūjayet । (2)
īśānaṃ cāpi parjanyaṃ jayantaṃ māhendramapyathā ॥
ādityaṃ satyakaṃ bhraṃśamantarikṣaṃ tadhānikam । (3)
pūṣāṇaṃ ca gṛhakṣaṃ ca yamaṃ gandharvameva cā ॥
bhṛṅgarājamṛgādhīśaṃ nāgaṃmukhyaṃ yathāvidhi । (4)
bhallāṭakaṃ ca somañca ṛgaṃ cāditameva cā ॥
aditaṃ cakramādetān pūjayecca yathāvidhi । (5)
īśānādi padamārabhya pūjayecca yathāvidhi ॥
maḍūkerdhapade pūjā tāpatparamaśāyike । (6)
carakiñca vitārī ca pūtanā pāparākṣasīm ॥
etaśūleṣvarcayitvā skandamaryameṇaṃ tathā । (7)
jṛbhaṃ ca bali piñchaṃ ca etan vṛtteṣu pūjayet ॥
paramaśāyikacakraṃ ce tathā śūleṣu pūjayet । (8)
vāstuvedyuttare bhāge sthāpayeddaśa kumbhakān ॥
brahmāṇaṃ bhāratī caiva vidyeśādīn samarcayet । (9)
dhūpadīpādikaṃ datvā baliṃ dadyādyathā kramam ॥ (i.64b)
kṣīrānnaṃ brahmaṇedeyaṃ kapitthaphalamātṛkam ।
marīcyādi devānāṃ gulonnaṃ balirīritā ॥
āpādīnāṃ ca devānāmudgānnaṃ balimācaret । (2)
īśānādi devānāṃ tilānnaṃ balimācaret ॥ (3)
carakyādyaṣṭadevānāṃ māṃsānnaṃ balimācaret ।
māṃsārthaṃ māṣasammiśraṃ baliṃ dadyādyathā vidhi ॥ (4)
brahmaṇe padamadhye tu marīcībhyasthataiva cā ।
āpādibhyaḥ koṇabandhau īśānādibhyaśca sūtrake ॥ (5)
carakyādyaṣṭadevebhyaḥ śūlavṛtte baliṃ kṣipet । (6)
agnikāryaṃ prakurvīta brahmādīn tatra pūjayet ॥
samidannājya dravyādīn brahmamantrādghutsudhiḥ । (7)
ājyāhuti sahasrasyādanya dravyaṃ yathāvidhi ॥
sarvāvaraṇadevānāṃ daśājyāhutimācaret । (8)
tattadevanusāreṇa tattadannāhutiṃ caret ॥
paścāt pāśupatādyaiśca prāyaścittāhutiṃ hunet । (9)
carakyādikadevānāṃ kūṣmāṇḍairjuhuyādguruḥ ॥
paścāt pūrṇāhutiṃ hutvā dhūpadīpādikaṃ caret । (10)
kuṇḍāgnimūrtiṃ ca pade yojayitvā tataḥ sudhiḥ ॥
tasmātkumbhe ca saṃyojya tasmādyodastra devake । (i.65a)
astradevaṃ samabhyarcya dhūpa dīpādikaṃ caret ॥
vāstvācāryāya datvātha dakṣiṇādikamādarāt । (2)
japtṛbhyo brāhmaṇebhyaśca datvā tāmbūladakṣiṇām ॥
palāśa śuṣkadarbhādyai palāśaudumbaraśuṣkakaiḥ । (3)
badhvolkāṃ daṇḍamānena tasminnagnau pradahya ca ॥
ājyena secayitvā tāṃ tatvatrayamathārcayet । (4)
tasmaicidbrahmaṇāyātha datvā vastraṃ tathā dhanam ॥
ulkāṃ ca tatkare datvā vādyaghoṣa purassaraṃ । (5)
astradevaṃ puraskṛtya dīpikābhi sahānugaḥ ॥
yāgadeśaviśudhyarthaṃ mūlaliṅgādi śuddhaye । (6)
sarvālaya viśudhyarthamālaye bhramaṇaṃ caret ॥
vidhi bhramaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā ulkāṃ bāhye visarjayet । (7)
snātvācāryastataḥkuryāt puṇyāhaṃ ca tataḥparam ॥
prāyaścittaṃ cāstradevaṃ yathākṛtvā yathāvidhi । (8)
avaśyametatkartavyamanyathā cetphalaṃ śṛṇu ॥
vāstupuruṣadoṣeṇa yāgavighne bhavedhruvam । (9)
tasmādavaśyaṃ kartavyaṃ vāstuśāntiḥ kramādiha ॥
bimbaśuddhiḥ
bimbaśuddhikramaṃ vakṣye vistarāccṛṇu putrakā । (i.65b)
āstānamaṇṭape vāpi yāgadeśapurelavi ॥
maṇṭapaṃ tatra kurvīta kuṇḍa vedi samanvitam । (2)
puṇyāhaṃ vācayettatra pañcagavyaṃ samācaret ॥
darbhamūle tu valmīke jambūnyagrodhamūlake । (3)
bilvamūle tu tulasī mūle cāśvatthamūlake ॥
audubarasya mūletu mṛdgṛhītvā vicakṣaṇaḥ ।
tejo vṛkṣatva pañcāpi tadalābhedavā sudhiḥ ॥ (4)
aśvatthavaṭa rājadra bilvajaṃ svaṃ mṛśākhinaḥ ।
tulasyaudumbarayoḥ pallavāścatvacastathā ॥ (5)
sarvāmavimṛdaṃ bhāṇḍe nikṣipeddeśikasvayam ।
bhaṇḍenyasmin tvacaḥ sarvāḥ nikṣipeddeśikottamaḥ ॥ (6)
anyasmin bhāṇḍake sarvapallavāni ca nikṣipet । (7)
mṛdbhāṇḍe cātmatatvaṃ ca pallave śivatatvakam ॥
vidyātatvaṃ ca tvagbhāṇḍe cārcayedvidhivadbudhaḥ । (8)
namakaṃ camakaṃ cāpi śrīsūktaṃ viṣṇusūktakam ॥
brahmasūktaṃ pañcaśāntiṃ brāhmaṇairjapamācaret । (9)
deśikaḥ padamantrāṇi jape tatvasahasrakam ॥
kuṇḍe homaṃ prakurvīta vidhi viṣṇvādi mantrakaiḥ ।
astrahomaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā pūrṇāhutimathācaret ॥ (10)
agnimūrtiṃ ca bhāṇḍeṣu yojayedeśikasvayam ।
śilpidoṣa nivṛtyarthaṃ vimānaṃ mūlaliṅgakam ॥ (i.66a)
anyānyapi ca berāṇi pūrvokte kumbhavāribhiḥ ।
pañcagavyena ca śuddhirabhiṣekaṃ samācaret ॥ (2)
etasya karmaṇe bhāve bimbaśuddhirbhavetkatham ।
bimbaśuddherabhāve tu devatā sannidhiḥ katham ॥ (3)
devasānnidhyabhāve tu yāgopyartha bhavetkilam ।
tasmātsarvaprayatnena bimbaśuddhiṃ samācaret ॥ (4)
pañcāmṛtaṃ pañcagavyaṃ sumavārī phalodakam ।
gandhodakaṃ patrajalaṃ dhānyaratnodakaṃ tathā ॥ (5)
lohodakaṃ dhāntavāri cāstrodakamataḥ param ।
mūlodakaṃ ca kūrcāmbu pratyekaṃ kramaśaḥ sudhiḥ ॥ (6)
kumbhānekādaśasthāpya pūrvoktajalapūritān ।
japtvā rudraṃ rudrāya brāhmaṇairdeśikasvayam ॥ (7)
abhiṣekaṃ tataḥ kuryādekaikaṃ prati kālakam ।
nityayāgasya pūrvaṃ syādetat kumbhābhiṣecanam ॥ (8)
nityayāgasamāptau tu śānti kumbhābhiṣecanam ।
evaṃ pratidinaṃ kuryāddeśikastu yathāvidhi ॥ (9)
evaṃ te bimbaśuddhistu vistāreṇa prakīrtitā ।
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyami mṛttikā saṅgrahaṃ guha ॥ (10)
uttamaṃ prāgudīcī syānmadhyamaṃ paścimāsmṛtā ।
adhamaṃ dakṣīṇāḥ proktā mṛtsaṅgrahaṇakarmaṇi ॥
nadītīre taṭāke vā kedāre darbhamūlake । (i.66b)
goṣṭe vā parvate vāpi valmīkārāmayorapi ॥
anyatra śuddhadeśe vā gṛhṇīyā mṛttikā sudhī । (2)
nirmāya maṇṭapaṃ tatra kadalī kramukādibhiḥ ॥
vighneśamastrarājaṃ ca caṇḍeśaṃ ca tathaiva hi । (3)
etāśca purataḥ kṛtvā khanitreṇa samanvitaḥ ॥
gītavāditraghoṣeṇa gatvā cāryotha maṇṭapam ।
maṇṭape paścime bhāge prāṅmukhaṃvighnarājakam ॥ (4)
pūrvabhāge cāstrarājaṃ paścimābhimukhaṃ nyaset ।
saumyabhāge tu caṇḍīśaṃ dakṣiṇābhimukhannyaset ॥ (5)
khanitramīśānadiśi nikṣipeddeśikottamaḥ ।
dakṣiṇe deśikasvasti puṇyāhaṃ śalya śodhanam ॥ (6)
madhye saṃlikhyabhūdevīṃ citrākāreṇa deśikaḥ ।
prāk śīrṣamūrdhvavaktrāṃ ca tattadvarṇopaśobhitam ॥ (7)
tataśca paścime bhāge navakoṣṭaṃ prakalpayet ।
koṣṭasya paścime bhāge saptakumbhān vinikṣipet ॥ (8)
paridhīn pūjayedatra koṣṭebrahmādikān yajet ।
dadhyānnaṃ ca baliṃdadyādbrahmāṇaṃ prārthayedguruḥ ॥ (9)
mṛdamīśvarakāryārthaṃ gṛhīṣyāmṛtavājñayā ।
bhūmidevi bahirdeśe paristaraṇamācaret ॥
indrādīnarcayettatra mudgānnaṃ dāpayedbalim । (10)
helokapālā śṛṇvantu yāgakāryārthamevahi ॥
mṛttikāṃ parigṛhṇāmi dīyyatā mamā .............. । (i.67a)
lokapālānidaṃ prārthya bhūmidevīṃ samarcayet ॥
bhūsūktaṃ japitvā'tha dhūpa dīpaṃ samācaret । (2)
khanitraṃ malaśāntyarthaṃ tailagavyena śodhayet ॥
khanitradaṇḍaṃ kūrcaigra cūtapatraiḥ sumairapi ।
alaṅkṛtya ca vastrādyaiḥ keśavādī samarcayet ॥ (3)
dhūpadīpādikaṃ datvā pātre śaktiṃ samarcayet ।
devasyatveti mantreṇa gṛhītvā ca khanitrakam ॥ (4)
bhūmidevi kukṣibhāge tu khaḍgamantrān khanetsudhiḥ ।
tatra trivāraṃ nikṣipya pātre mṛsnāṃ ca deśikaḥ ॥ (5)
mṛcchaddhā kukṣideśe tu anya mṛtsnā tu hānidā ।
bhūmiṃ samatalaṃ kṛtvā khātadoṣa nivṛttaye ॥ (6)
saptavāridhi kumbhaiśca tatra deśebhiṣecayet ।
vrīhīṃ tatra vikīryātha sarvānu dvāsayetsudhiḥ ॥ (7)
mṛttikopari vinyasya kūrcapuṣpākṣatān gurum ।
dukūlavastrairācchādya brahmāṅgairabhi mantrayet ॥ (8)
tatvaijapakartṛbhyaḥ brāhmaṇebhyaśca dakṣiṇām ।
śibikāyāṃ gaje vāpi paricārakamūrdhni ॥ (9)
nikṣipya mṛttikādvidvān kuśenāspṛśya deśikaḥ ।
pradakṣiṇa krameṇaiva devālayamathāviśet ॥
ācāryo yāgaśālāyāṃ gatvā puṇyāhamācaret । (10)
saṃskāramaṣṭādaśadhā kuryācchalyāpanuttaye ॥
aṅkurārpaṇa vidhiḥ
bījā vāpakramaṃ vakṣye śṛṇu vatsa samāhitaḥ । (i.67b)
aṅkurārpaṇamārabhya yāgārambhaḥprakīrtitaḥ ॥
aṅkurādi pratiṣṭhāntaṃdinasaṅkhyā trayodaśa । (2)
ayam uttamayāgasyādityāgamavido jaguḥ ॥
navāhaṃ madhyamaṃ proktaṃ saptāhamadhamaṃ bhavet । (3)
adhamādhamamityāhu tryahaṃ pañcāhamapyathā ॥
sadya saṃprokṣaṇe mātramaṅkuraṃ tu na kārayet । (4)
trayodaśadinādūrdhvaṃ pratiṣṭāmakṛñcaraṃ care ॥
ākarṣaṇadinapūrvaṃ kautukaṃ kāramācaret । (5)
aṅkurārpaṇataḥ pūrvaṃtripañcadivasā guruḥ ॥
devānujñāṃ prakurvīta pīṭhabandhanamācaret । (6)
tatpaścādvāstuśāntiṃ ca bimbaśuddhiṃ samācaret ॥
anujñāṅkurayormadhye cāmāsaṃ kramaṇādikam । (7)
yavai dhambhavettatra kṣauradoṣo na vidyate ॥ (8)
pratiṣṭāṅkurayormadhye amāsaṅkaṇamādikam ।
yathā na sambhavedvidvān pratiṣṭhāṃ tu tathā caret ॥ (9)
dīkṣāyāmutsave yāge āsaṅkramaṇādikam ।
sambhavedyadi doṣāya na bhavedyevaṣaṇmukha ॥
anyasmin maṅgale kārye cāṅkurārpaṇamācaret । (10)
ākarṣaṇadine caiva ekakālaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ॥
abhiṣekadine cāpi ekakālastathaiva hi । (i.68a)
madhyeṣu sarvadivaseṣvapi yāgadvayaṃ bhavet ॥
trayodaśadineṣvāpi dekāhaṃ cāṅkurārpaṇam । (2)
śiṣṭānā dvādaśāhānāṃ viṃśatiḥ kāla īrita ॥
ekādaśāhapakṣe tu kāloṣṭādaśakīrtitaḥ । (3)
navāha yajane kālaḥcaturdaśa samīritaḥ ॥
saptāhe daśakālasyā pañcāhe kālaṣaṭkakam ।
tridine tu dvikālasyādekāhe divi kārayet ॥ (4)
homaṃ gururyathāśāstraṃ bhaktidhyānaparāyaṇaḥ ।
aṅkurārpaṇa karme tanmadhyāhnenācaretsudhiḥ ॥ (5)
sūryakālo divā proktaḥ candrasyādrātri nāyakaḥ । (6)
candrasauṣadhinā dhatvādaṅkuraścandravaitaḥ ॥
tasmādastaṃ gate bhānau pūrvayāme sulagnake । (7)
pūrvayāme sudhiḥ kuryādyevaṃ syācchāstranirṇayam ॥
pālikāṣṭā gulotsedhaṃ mukhaṃ vṛttaṃ tathaiva ca । (8)
tadardhapādavistāramoṣṭamekāṅgulaṃ bhavet ॥
triyaṅgulaṃ tu biliṃ proktaṃ pālikālakṣaṇaṃ tvidam । (9)
vāyukoṇe tu śālāyāmaṅkurārpaṇmācaret ॥
prākpratīcyoḥ pañcāsūtraṃ tathāsyādakṣiṇottaram । (10)
vedyāṃ tatra prakurvīta śālibhiḥ sthaṇḍilaṃ caret ॥
madhye kamalamullikhya tatra kumbhatrayaṃ nyaset । (11)
dvimukhaṃ madhya kumbhaṃ tu vinyasetatra deśikaḥ ॥
palālaiḥ pālikā randhramācchādya vidhivadguruḥ ।
tantubhicūtapatraiśca dabhairāveṣyadeśikaḥ ॥ (i.68b)
mṛttikābhiḥ pūrayitvā bahi koṣṭeṣu vinyaset ।
puṇyāhavācanaṃ kṛtvā dhānya śuddhiṃ vidhācaret ॥ (2)
kubhe ca lāsanaṃ kalaya cauṣadhīśaṃ samarcayet ।
kṛttikāṃ rohiṇīṃ cāpi pūjyetpārśvakumbhayoḥ ॥ (3)
ekaikasyā pālikāyāṃ ṣaḍutthāsanamarcayet ।
vaikartanāṃ vivasvān ca mārtāṇḍo bhāskaro raviḥ ॥ (4)
lokaprakāśakaścaiva lokasākṣī trivikramaḥ । (5)
ādityaśca maha sūryaḥ aṃśumān ca divākaram ॥
etān hi dvādaśādityān pālikāsu prapūjayet । (6)
tilasarṣapa mudgāni māṣaśimbāḍhakāni ca ॥
kuluttha yava godhūmaṃ navadhānyāni deśikaḥ । (7)
ādau jalaiḥ kṣālayitvā paścāt kṣīreṇa secayet ॥
oṣadhī sūktamantraṃ tu brāhmaṇairjapamācaret । (8)
tattaddevatamantreṇa vāpayedbīja santatim ॥
paścātsumaṅgalībhiśca bījāpāpaṃ samācaret । (9)
candrakumbhajalaivyāpya pālikāstatra deśikaḥ ॥
punaḥ kuṃbhaṃ jalaiḥ pūrya punaḥ pūjāṃ samācaret । (10)
pāyasānnaṃ gulāpūpaṃ tāmbūlaṃ ca nivedayet ॥
evaṃ pratidinaṃ kuryātpūjāṃ yāgāntameva hi ।
aṅkurervṛddhimāpanne śubhameva bhavedguha ॥ (i.69a)
aṅkurāṇaṃ ca kubjatve dhūmatvenaṅkure tathā ।
prāyaścittaṃ prakurvīta yathāvidhi vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥ (2)
oṣdhīmantrajaptṛbhyaḥ brāhmaṇebhyo yathā vidhi ।
sumaṅgalībhyaśca tathāta tāmbūlaṃ dakṣiṇāmapi ॥ (3)
sarvebhyo brāhmaṇebhyaśca datvā tāmbūladakṣiṇām ।
yāgānte somamudvāsya cāṅkurānnikṣipe jalaiḥ ॥ (4)
rakṣābandhanam
rakṣābandhanakarmedaṃ kārtikeyādhunā śṛṇu । (i.69a, 4)
vinyasya vāruṇaṃ kubhaṃ tatsūktaṃ japamācaret ॥ (5)
hemantan mantrataḥ kṛtvā rakṣāśuddhiṃ samācaret ।
aṅguṣṭānāmikābhyāṃ tu vāmahastasya deśikaḥ ॥ (6)
rakṣāmūlaṃ tu saṅgṛhya bhasmanāmārjaye caret ।
rakṣāṅguṣṭanāmikābhyāṃ namo vaḥ kirimantrataḥ ॥ (7)
rakṣāmūrdhvamukhīṃ kṛtvā hṛdayeṇā visarjayet ।
tāmbūlaṃ nālikeraṃ ca phalaṃ bhasmaṃ ca taṇḍulam ॥ (8)
rakṣāmapi svarṇapātre vinyasye gurudarāt ।
vikṣaṇādyeśca saṃśodhya rakṣāyāṃ pūjayedguruḥ ॥ (9)
anantādī ca tanmadhye tattadravyaṃ tu pūjayet ।
tatvatrayaṃ samabhyarcya sandhānāhutimācaret ॥ (10)
āyusūktaṃ japaṃ kṛtvā rakṣāyai brāhmaṇaiḥ saha ।
agniṃ kumbhe tu samyojya tattīrthe śuddhimācaret ॥ (i.69b)
etatpātraṃ tu śibikāyāmāropya brāhmaṇaiḥ saha । (2)
dhāmapradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā gatvā devālayaṃ sudhi ॥
ṣaḍutthāsanamabhyarcya dhyātvā devaṃ prapūjayet ।
liṅgasya rudrabhāge tu pīṭhe nālasya madhyame ॥ (3)
maheśvarādi devānāṃ dakṣahaste tu bandhanam ।
śaktīnāṃ vāmahaste tu śūle syānmadhyapatrake ॥ (4)
maṇṭape madhyame stambhe vṛṣabhe śriṅgamūlake ।
rathasya kūbare kuryādrakṣābandhanameva hi ॥ (5)
rakṣāṅkure tu badhnīyādbodhako maṇibandhake ।
ṛtvijāṃ deśikaḥ kuryādrākṣāṃ maṇibandhake ॥ (6)
marjaneyā vidhiḥ proktā sā vidhiḥ bandhane'pi ca । (7)
triyambakena mantrena rakṣābandhanamācaret ॥
tadgrandhau bhasmanikṣipya cāstramantrena deśikaḥ । (8)
somakumbhau vināpakṣa bandhanaṃ tu kanīyasam ॥
ākarṣaṇadinātpūrvarātrau kautukabandhanam । (9)
abhāve taddine vāpi karṣaṇātpūrvamācaret ॥
prātiṣṭācotsavāṅgaṃ deve kautukabandhanam । (10)
rakṣābandhanamantroktaṃ deśikasyātmaśuddhaye ॥
śavasūtakadoṣebhyo yena śuddhati deśikaḥ । (i.70a)
rakṣābandhavihīnaṃ yatpratiṣṭā kriyate budhaiḥ ॥
tanniṣphalataḥ kuryādrakṣāṃ deśikasattamaḥ । (2)
rakṣā svarṇamayiṃ kuryā sarvavaddeśikottamaḥ ॥
athavārājate naiva sapuccha saphaṇaṃ kuryā sarvavadeśikottamaḥ । (3)
ardhāṅgulaṃ tathā yāmaṃ maṇibandhasamaṃ bhavet ॥
dhānyasthāpanam
vatsa śṛṇu viśeṣeṇa dhānyasthāpanamucyate । (i.70a, 4)
yavatṇḍulagodhūmāścāḍhakaṃ māṣamevaca ॥ (5)
sarṣapaṃ cā vimudgaṃ ca caṇakaṃ tilamapyathā ।
niṣpāpaṃ dhānyamapita navadhānyaṃ prakīrtitam ॥
yavārdhastaṇḍulaḥ proktata triṃśāṃśaṃ prakīrtitaḥ । (6)
godhūmaścāḍakaścāpi māṣamudgāvapi śṛṇu ॥ (7)
niṣpāpaścakaścaiva pratidhānyamidaṃ bhavet ।
pūrvoktadhānya ṣaṭketu tribhagastilasarṣapau ॥ (8)
vedyāṃ tu kadalī patre pratyekaṃ nikṣipesudhiḥ ।
prati dhānyaṃ tu kamalaṃ sāṣṭapatraṃ sakarṇikam ॥ (9)
tiletu reghā tadanuvajrārpaṇamataḥ param ।
nyāsaṃ catuṣpadaṃ proktaṃ tadūrdhvantu vikīrya ca ॥ (i.70b)
lājacandana siddhārtha bhasmapuṣpa kuśākṣatān ।
śuddhalājā sudhistatra viniryāstramanukramāt ॥
vastrabhūṣṭādyarpaṇaṃ tu dīkṣitānāmihocyate । (2)
ravivastraṃ tvadhovastraṃ tatsamaṃ cottarīyakam ॥
khaṇḍavastraṃ trikhaṇḍaṃ tu śirovastraṃ tu ṣaṭkaram । (3)
kauśeyamuttamaṃ proktaṃ madhyamaṃ paṭṭavastrakam ॥
vastraṃ kanyayasaṃ proktaṃ kāmikāgamako vidaiḥ । (4)
navīnavastrahīnaṃ tu niphalaṃ karma tadbhavet ॥
pratidīkṣitamekaṃ tu yogapaṭṭaṃ pradāpayet । (5)
caturaṅgulamāyā mama dhātaṃ ṣaṭkaraṃ bhavet ॥
athaivastraṃ cottarīyaṃ prati karma pṛthakpṛthak । (6)
kaṇṭharudrākṣamālāṃ ca śiromālā tathaiva ca ॥
bāhumālā tathaiva syāt sarveṣāmṛtvijāmapi । (7)
hastekasūkṣamālāṃ ca nābhi deśāntalambikām ॥ (8)
ekājapa vaṭi prokta ratnakāñcanaśobhitāḥ ।
kuṇḍalaṃ karṇayoścāpi athavā karṇaveṣṭanam ॥
pavitraṃ tarjanī caiva kaṅkaṇaṃ cāgulīyyakam । (9)
aṅguṣṭamudrikā cāpi śivamantropaśobhitaḥ ॥
tarjanyā vilikhet prājña śaktimantraṃ satārakam । (10)
hemayajñopavītaṃ ca hemaṃ tu kaṭisūtrakam ॥
muktāpravālaracitāḥ nānā ratnopaśobhitaḥ । (i.71a)
pāduke bhasma bhastriṃ ca karakābhasvarṇanirmitān ॥ (2)
śārdūlacarmavānocet kṛṣṇājīnamathāpivā ।
piñca barhāsanaṃ cāpi devā pṛdhyaikamādayat ॥ (3)
etāni sarva vastrāṇi pañcagavyadibhiḥ sudhiḥ ।
saṃśodhya bokaḥ paścād dīkṣitebhyaḥ pradāpayet ॥ (4)
mūtyunyutpādanaṃ vakṣye śṛṇuṣva gurusādaram ।
nityāgnau tu huneddhīmān tadābhāve tu deśikaḥ ॥ (5)
agnimutpādya vidhivattatra homaṃ samācaret ।
vakṣyenyutpādanaṃ sūryakāntādeva viśeṣataḥ ॥ (6)
araṇairmathanaṃ kuryād athavā sā dvijagṛhāt । (7)
puṇyāhavācanaṃ cānyutpādanārthaṃ care sudhiḥ ॥
āgneyamantrān japtvā brāhmaṇaiḥ saha deśikaḥ । (8)
sūryābhimukhato vidvān sūryakāntaṃ pradarśya cā ॥
taścādhayāṃ tu vinyasya tūlaṃ stokamataḥ param । (9)
agnirutpādyate tatra tamādāya vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥
sūryakāntodbhavo vanhi praśasta sarvakarmasu । (10)
tadbhāreraṇerjātaḥ praśasta kathyaterbudhaiḥ ॥
evamagnimutpādya datvā ca japadakṣiṇām । (i.71b)
nityāgnireva kathitaḥ mūrtyagniriti ko vidaiḥ ॥
prasannapūjā vidhiḥ
prasannasyābhiṣekasya vidhānamiha kathyate । (i.71b, 2)
āsthānamaṇṭape ramye gomayādyavalepite ॥ (2)
raṅgavalyā ca samyukte vitānadhvajaśobhite । (3)
kadalī stambhasaṃyukte pūgagucheralaṅkṛte ॥
maṇṭape vedikā madhye navadhānyai prasāritai ।
trisūtrairveṣṭitān kumbhān kṣālitān dhūpadhūpitān ॥ (4)
salilaiḥpūritān kṛtva kūrcapallava śobhitān ।
nālikeraṃ lambakūrcaṃ vastragandhādi śobhitān ॥ (5)
sahasraṃ vātha kalaśāḥ athavā śatameva vā ।
adhamā daśa kumbhāsyuḥvidhivatsthāpayedguruḥ ॥ (6)
puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā tu pañcagavyaṃ samācaret ।
snapanokta prakāreṇa pūjāṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ ॥ (7)
ghaṭāgre homakāryaṃ tu kṛtvaiva vidhivadguruḥ ।
mūlenaśataṃ hutvā saṃhitena daśāṃśakam ॥ (8)
aghoreṇa śataṃ hutvā lājai brāṅgamāhutiḥ ।
pañcapañcamūlena hunedravyāntaraṃ sudhiḥ ॥ (9)
pañcapañcāhutiṃ kuryādekaikāstreṇa deśikaḥ ।
jayādinā ca juhuyāt pūrṇāhutiṃ samācaret ॥ (10)
bhavantī kalaśā yatra śataṃ vātha sahasrakam ।
etaḥ saṅkhyānurodhena homaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ ॥
śaktyādi śaktiparyantamarcayenmūlaliṅgake । (i.72a)
pañcāvaraṇa pūjāñca yathoktaṃ pūjayecchivam ॥
abhiṣekaṃ yathoktena kurya snapana vāribhiḥ । (2)
mahāhaviśca naivedyaṃ dhūpadīpādikaṃ caret ॥
evaṃ prasannapūjāṃ ca kuryāddeśikasattamaḥ|| (3)
kumbhālaṅkāraḥ
kumbhālaṅkāramadhunā vakṣyāmi śrṛṇu sādaram । (i.72a, 3)
garbhagṛhaṃ tato gatvā prāsādāgre tu buddhimān ॥ (4)
pūrvavatsaṃ prakurvīta dhānyastapanamatra hi ।
yavastu brahmadaivatya sarṣapo saṃradaivataḥ ॥ (5)
mudgaṃ varuṇadaivatya caṇakaṃ vāyu daivatam ।
niṣpāpaṃ somadaivatyaḥ tilamīśanadaivatam ॥ (6)
etaddhanyādi devānāṃ tattanmantreṇa pūjanam ।
reghā vajārpaṇaṃ sarvaṃ pūrvavatkalpayedbudhaḥ ॥ (7)
saurṇakalaśāṃ tatra nikṣipeddeśikottamaḥ ।
droṇadvayā pūrya jala śivakumbhaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ॥ (8)
tadardhaṃ vardhanīkumbhaḥ vidyeśāṃ ca tadardhakāḥ ।
saptaviṃśati kumbhāśca tadardhaṃ parikīrtitāḥ ॥ (9)
vidye kumbha sadṛśaścāstrakumbhaṃ prakīrtitaḥ ।
tadvardhanīṃ tadardhaṃ syād dvimukhī parikīrtitāḥ ॥ (10)
sarve kumbhāśca niścidrāṃ navīnaḥ parikīrtitāḥ ।
śaktyā bhāve mṛdghaṭānāṃ kṛṣṇavarṇaparityajet ॥
astramantreṇa saṃkṣālya kumbhā sarvāśca deśikaḥ । (i.72b)
tantutrayaṃ caikasūtraṃ trisūtraṃ veṣṭaye tathā ॥
uttamaṃ tilamātraṃ hi madhyamaṃ yavamānanam । (2)
adhamaṃ gajanetrābham evam syad sūtraveṣṭanam ॥
śivakumbe tu saṃveṣṭya padmā ṣoḍaśapatrakam । (3)
vardhanyāṃ veṣṭa dvidvān padmaṃ dvādaśapatrakam ॥
aṣṭavidyeśakumbhānāṃ padmaṃ syādaṣṭapatrakam । (4)
saptaviṃśtikumbhānāṃ catuṣpatraṃ prakīrtitam ॥
pañcavarṇaiśca sūtraiśca sundaraṃ veṣṭayetsudhiḥ । (5)
sīsena tantramantrena kavacasūtreṇa veṣṭnam ॥
gṛhītvā ghoramantreṇa dhūpadhūmaṃ ghaṭe sudhiḥ । (6)
hiranyapātramantreṇa ghaṭādīn sthaṇḍile kṣipet ॥ (7)
darbhadvayāgrasaṃyuktaṃ madhyagrandhi pavitrakam ।
aṅguṣṭānāmikābhyaṃ tu ghaṭoghvevinyasedguruḥ ॥ (8)
hime gandheti mantreṇa kumbheṣu jalapūraṇam ।
āpovā iti mantrena haste ne vyāvanaṃ tathā ॥ (9)
visṛjya kūrca grāndhiśca jaleśaṃ ca samarcayet ।
āpaḥ prāṇa ityeva jalaṃ tadabhimāntrayet ॥ (i.73a)
elālavaṅgakarpūrajātipatraṃ ca tatphalam ।
uśiraṃ cavakaṃ caiva hriberaṃ rocanā tathā ॥
sarvāṇyetani sammikṣya cūrṇikṛtya vicakṣaṇaḥ । (2)
gandhadvāretimantreṇa kalaśāmbuni nikṣipet ॥
himāmbugaṅgatoyaiśca pūrayitvā ghaṭā sudhiḥ ॥ (3)
kūrcalakṣaṇam
ṣaṭ triṃśadarbhasaṃyuktaṃ śivakumbhasya kūrcakam । (i.73a, 3)
pañcaviṃśatidarbhāścā vardhanī kūrcamīritam ॥ (4)
darbhapañcadaśopetaṃ vidyeśānāṃ tu kūrcakam ।
parivarādi sarveṣāṃ kūrcaṃ vidyeśa sannibham ॥ (5)
agraṃ vedāṅgulaṃ proktaṃ dvayaṅgulaṃ grandhirīritā । (6)
dakṣiṇāvartitaṃ kūrcaṃ nālamaṣṭādaśāṅgulam ॥
ādyantaṃ hastamātreṇa antaḥ kūrcasya lakṣaṇam । (7)
caturdaśāṅgulaṃ nālaṃ pañcaviṃśati darbhakaiḥ ॥
agragrandhipūrvasyādvardhanī kūrca lakṣaṇam । (8)
granthirekāṅgulaṃ rproktaṃ agraṃ tryaṅgulamucyate ॥ (9)
dvādaśā gulavannālaṃ vidyeśānāmidaṃ bhavet ।
ūrdhvāgraṃ syāt pratiṣṭāyāmathegraṃ prokṣaṇo bhavet ॥ (10)
kūrcagrandhau ca vāgdevī kūrcamūle tu pārvati ।
kūrcāgre sarva tīrthāni sāvitrī cādhidevatā ॥ (i.73b)
yo rudreti mantreṇa antaḥ kūrcaṃ vinikṣipet ।
vajraṃ vaiḍūryakaṃ cai māṇikyaṃ sphaṭikaṃ tathā ॥ (2)
indraṃ nīlamarakadaṃ mautikaṃ ca pravālakam ।
gomedhakaṃ ceti navaratnani ca vinikṣipet ॥
māṇikyaṃ vajravaiḍūryam indranīlaṃ pravālakam । (3)
pañcaratnamidaṃ dhīmān vardhanyāṃ tu vinikṣipet ॥
pravālaṃ mautikaṃ nīlaṃ sarvakumbheṣu nikṣipet । (4)
vidyeśādi kumbhānāṃ tattadvarṇaṃ vinikṣipet ॥ (5)
sahiratnani mantreṇa sarvaratnā vinikṣipet ।
śivakumbhe hemapadmaṃ vardhanyāṃ hemacotphalam ॥ (6)
vidyeśānāṃ hemaśūlaṃ sarveṣāṃ hemapaṭṭakam ।
tadbījaṃ tu saṃlikhyatoya madhye vinikṣipet ॥ (7)
kumbhamadhyesthitaṃ toyaṃ toyamadhye tu paṅkajam ।
paṅkajasya madhye tu pārvatīparameśvarau ॥ (8)
hiraṇyavarṇāmantreṇa nikṣiped hemapaṭṭakam ।
sarvatra kumbhatīrtheṣu dhanaṃ vinyasya deśikaḥ ॥ (9)
kumbheṣu cūtapatrāni kūrcatulyāni nikṣipet ।
aprāṇāni ca dīrghāṇi bahirdeśeṣu vinyaset ॥ (i.74a)
nālikeraṃ cordhvaśikhaṃ madhyanetraṃ tu vaktrakam ।
sarveṣāmapi kumbhānām uttamāgu prakīrtitam ॥ (2)
śire hīne bhavetkumbhaṃ nārikelamavedihā ।
śivasyābhimukhaṃ kuryācchāstraviddeśikasvayam ॥ (3)
kuśeyamuttamaṃ proktaṃ madhyamaṃ raktapaṭṭakam ।
kārpāsavastramadhamamityāgamavido jaguḥ ॥ (4)
śivakumbhasya kauśeyaṃ ṣaṭkaraṃ parikīrtitam ।
tadardhaṃ vardhanī vastraṃ vidyeśānāṃ tadardhakam ॥ (5)
saptaviṃśati kumbhānāṃ tadardhaṃ parikīrtitam ।
sarveṣāmapi kumbhānāṃ vastrayugmaṃ prakīrtitam ॥ (6)
antaḥ kūrcena sadṛśaṃ lambakūrcaṃ vinikṣipet ।
tvamagna iti mantreṇa lambakūrcaṃ vinikṣipet ॥ (7)
yajñasūtraṃ tu vidhinā śivakumbhe tu vinyaset ।
hemabilvadalaṃ vidvān nānābharaṇaśobhitam ॥ (8)
akṣataiścandanenāpi ṣaḍgandhairapi deśikaḥ ।
puṣpamālābhiravitānalaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ ॥ (9)
sāvadhānālaṅkṛtya badhvā deśikasattamaḥ ॥
mūrtihomaḥ
mūrtihomamidaṃ vakṣe śruṇu vatsa sakautukam । (i.74a, 10)
puṇyāhaṃ vācayedvidvān saṅgalpya ca tataḥ param ॥
nityāgnihomaṃ kurvīta mūrti mūrtīśvarārcanam । (i.74b)
navadhā vā pañcadhā vā athavā ekameva vā ॥
prākāre navadhā proktaṃ pañcakuṇḍaṃ tathaiva cā । (2)
athavā sannidhau proktaṃ mūrtihomau vicakṣaṇaiḥ ॥
sahasraṃ vā tadardhaṃ va hunecca śivamūlataḥ । (3)
manonmanyāstadardhaṃ syādbrahmāṅgena daśāhutiḥ ॥
ekaika brahmaṇaḥ proktaḥ daśāhutiriti kramam । (4)
vidikṣu dikṣu kuṇḍeṣu tattaddevān hunetkramāt ॥
ekaikasya devatāyā striśataṃ cāhutiṃ caret । (5)
athavā syādāhuti śataṃ kuryāddeśikasattamaḥ ॥
samidānnājya dravyādīn hunecca vidhipūrvakam । (6)
mūrtihomakramaścāyaṃ vidvadbhi parikīrtitaḥ ॥
trikhaṇḍaśodhanam
śivakumbhaṃ tridhā kṛtvā liṅgasyapi tathaiva cā । (i.74b, 7)
brahmaviṣṇuśca rudrasya trikhaṇḍamiti kathyate ॥
ūrdhvaṃ tu śivabhāgasyādviṣṇubhāgastu madhyamaḥ । (8)
brahmabhāgastvadhaḥ proktaḥ śāstrajñai liṅgakumbhayoḥ ॥
śivatatvaṃ rudrabhāge pañcatatvaṃ prapūjayet । (9)
viṣṇubhāge saptatatvapūjanaṃ tatra kārayet ॥
brahmabhāge pūjayecca caturviṃśati tatvakam । (10)
khaṇḍatraye'pi tatvāni ṣaṭ triṃśat pūjanaṃ sudhiḥ ॥
pratikhaṇḍaṃ pūjayitvā mūrtimūrtīśvarānapi । (i.75a)
śivatatvaṃ samārabhya dharāntaṃ pūjayedguruḥ ॥
pūrvoktenaivamārgeṇa piṇḍikāyāṃ ca pūjayet ॥ (2)
nāḍīsandhāna homaḥ
nāḍīsandhānahomākhyaṃ karma vakṣyāmi te guha । (i.75a, 2)
uttamā svarṇanāḍīṃ syāt madhyamā rajitā tathā ॥ (3)
kārpāsa nirmitā naḍī adhamā kathyate budhaiḥ ।
liṅgāgre lambakūrce tu kumbhāgre'pi tathaiva cā ॥ (4)
anusandhānamārgeṇa nāḍiṃ badhvā tu deśikaḥ ।
ātmakumbhāgni liṅgānamaitya nāḍhyā tu kārayet ॥ (5)
kuṇḍasya sarvabhāge tu kumbhamekaṃ vinikṣipet ।
evaṃ kuṇḍāgnivakṣe syādayaṃ śāstravidhikramaḥ ॥ (6)
anyatra sarvakuṇḍānāṃ kumbhamekaṃ pṛthakpṛthak ।
kumbhe cādhāraśaktaṃ tu pūjayedvidhivadguruḥ ॥ (7)
tatvatrayaṃ samabhyarcya dhūpadīpaṃ pradarśayet ।
ādhāraśaktikuṇḍe'pi arcayecca vicakṣṇaḥ ॥ (8)
kalāḥ pañca ca saṃpūjya sarvakuṇḍeṣvayaṃ vidhiḥ ।
tatvatrayaṃ samabhyarcya guru prādhānyakuṇḍake ॥
vidikṣu dikṣu kuṇḍeṣu mūrtimūrtīśvarānapi । (9)
nāḍīsandhāna hetośca astreṇa juhuyācchanam ॥
caturviṃśati tatvāni arcayetmūrtikuṇḍake । (10)
vidikṣu dikṣu kuṇḍeṣu pūrvavatpūjayedguruḥ ।
homaṃ tatra prakurvīta mūlena ca śataṃ hunet ॥ (i.75b)
puṇyāhatiṃ tataḥ kṛtvā śeṣājyaṃ śāntikumbhake ।
dhūpadīpādikaṃ datvā brahmāṇaṃ prārthayedguruḥ ॥
śāntikumbhān samādāya sarvepyācāryasattamaḥ । (2)
śivakumbhāntikaṃ gatvā brahmabhāge japetsudhiḥ ॥
homasaṅkhyā samaṃ vidvān tattanmantreṇa sādaram । (3)
śāntikumbhodakaiḥ prokṣya śivakumbhaṃ tu deśikaḥ ॥
homavatpūjayeddhīmān prārthayedātmatatvakam । (4)
śāntikumbhaṃ samādāya gatvā kuṇḍāntikaṃ guruḥ ॥
pūrvavatpūjayeddhīmān vidyātatvaṃ samarcayet । (5)
vidikṣu dikṣu kuṇḍeṣu pūrvavatpūjayedguruḥ ॥
pūrvavajjuhuyāccāpi pūrṇāhutimathācaret । (6)
vidyātatvaṃ caiva meva ghaṭe saṃyojayetkramāt ॥
śivatatvaṃ caiva meva kuryāddeśikasattamaḥ । (7)
pīṭhe caṇḍikāyāṃ ca evasparśāhutiṃ caret ॥
kṣmāgni yajamānārka jalavāyuniśākarāḥ । (8)
vyometi navakuṇḍākhya vakṣeṣṭau mūrtayastvime ॥
bhavaṃ cograśca śarvaśca mahādevastu rudrakaḥ । (9)
bhīmapaśupatiśceśa cāṣṭa mūrtīrsvarālame ॥
tamā mohā jayā ghorā rakṣāniṣṭāmayā tathā ।
rudrā cetyaṣṭadhā proktāḥ piṇḍikā śaktayasvimāḥ ॥ (10)
evameva prakāreṇa sparśāhutimathācaret ।
pūrṇāhutī tataḥ kuryāddhūpadīpaṃ samācaret ॥ (i.76a)
kuṇḍastha devatā mūrtiṃ mūlaliṅge suyojayet ।
paścadagniṃ tu saṃrakṣya sāvadhānaṃ tathā guruḥ ॥ (2)
ākarṣaṇam
ākarṣaṇa vidhiṃ vakṣye sāvadhānamanā śṛṇu । (i.76a, 2)
pūrvāhne vātha madhyāhne karṣaṇaṃ tu samācaret ॥
sāyāhne vā niśāyāṃ vā naiva karṣaṇamācaret । (3)
sadyodhivāsane mātraṃ divārātraṃ tu karṣaṇam ॥
bālālayaṃ karṣaṇaṃ ca caralagne samācaret । (4)
mūlālayapratiṣṭaikasthiralagne vidhīyyate ॥
liṅgātkumbhe tato liṅge karṣaṇaṃ sthāpanaṃ caret । (5)
kumbhe ca lāsanaṃ kalpya śaktyantaṃ pūjayetkramāt ॥
anusandhanamārgeṇa mūrtimāvāhayedghaṭe । (6)
devaṃ niṣkalamākṛṣya sakalaṃ pūjayetsudhiḥ ॥
niraṅgaṃ niṣkalaṃ proktaṃ sāgaṃ sakalamucyate । (7)
śāntyatītakalāmūrdhni śāntirvaktrasaroruham ॥
vidyā vidhita vakṣoṅgaṃ pratiṣṭhā guhyamaṇḍalam । (8)
nivṛttīrjānujaṅghāghriḥ bhuvanādhvā tanūruham ॥
varṇatvak mantrarudhiraṃ vada māṃsavirājitam । (9)
tatvāntamajjā śuklādi dhātu sadbhogamokṣatam ॥
śivāntaṃ tatvagarbhāntaṃ vyāpinaṃ sarvadāśivam ।
avibhaktāgaśca yajjotistanniṣkalamitīryate ॥ (10)
sakalastu vibhaktāṅga savidyādeha ucyate ।
tadeva niṣkalaṃ jñeyaṃ sakalaṃ bhavati dhruvam ॥ (i.76b)
yaddevāḥ sakalaḥ proktaḥ ṣaḍadhvāpi sa yeva hi ।
ṣaḍadhvā ddvividhaḥ proktaḥ sṛṣṭisaṃhāra bhedataḥ ॥ (2)
śāntyātītātsamārabhya nivṛtyāntaṃ nyasetsudhiḥ ।
ayaṃ sṛṣṭikramaḥ proktaḥ sakalāgamapāragaiḥ ॥
nivṛtyādi samārabhya śāntyatītā vidhinyaset । (3)
etatsaṃhāra ityāhuḥ śāsre'smin śāstravedinaḥ ॥
tatkalāsuca pratyekaṃ tatvaṃ yadbhuvanādikam । (4)
tatra tatra nyaseddhīmān yetanmūrtiṃ ca yojayet ॥
etāḥ kalāstṛdhā kṛtvā brahmaviṣṇu śivātmikāḥ । (5)
śaivīkalā śāntyatītā vaiṣṇavī śāntirucyate ॥
śeṣāḥ brahmakalāyāḥ etanmūrtiṃ ca yojayet । (6)
śivaṃ ca tatvamityuktaṃ mūrtiṃ caiva sadāśivaḥ ॥
tayoryogena yadrūpaṃ tadrūpaṃ bhāva ucyate । (7)
santataṃ śivabhāvatvad dādākhyamiti kathyate ॥
sarveṣāṃ dhyānapūjārthaṃ niṣkalaṃ sakalaṃ bhavet ।
etat sarvaṃ gururbhaktyā bhayenāpi samācaret ॥ (8)
deva deva jagannātha jagatkāraṇakāraṇā ।
tvalliṅgapīṭhasandhārthamāgacchakalaśe vibho ॥ (9)
na jāne mantratantrāṇi śāstraṃ śāstrārthamapyathā ।
āgamāśca na jānāmi tvatsvarūpaṃ bibodhakān ॥
kṛpayā maiyi deveśa āgaścha kalaśe japāt । (10)
sarvāvaraṇa saṃyuktaṃ sarvaśakti samanvitaḥ ॥
sarvaiśvarya samāyuktamagaccha kalaśe vibho । (i.77a)
tvatsvarūpaṃ na jānanti sphuṭaṃ vedāśca yoginaḥ ॥
sanakādyāśca ṛṣayaḥ ahaṃ jāne kathaṃ vibho । (2)
bhāgatyāgena te rūpaṃ vedāśca pravadantiḥ ॥
ahaṃ jāne kathaṃ devā mūḍhadhīrūdaraṃ bhariḥ । (3)
tasmātkṛtpāvaśāt kumbhetvamāyā hi kṛpānidhe ॥
itī saṃprārthya deveśaṃ gururbhakti samanvitaḥ ।
dhyāyennīrāñjanaṃ devaṃ sṛṣṭisthityāntakāraṇam ॥ (4)
sahasrādityasaṃkāśaṃ vidyutpuñjavirājitam ।
jyotirmayaṃ śivaṃ dhyātvā nādabindumayātmakam ॥ (5)
gurūpadiṣṭamārgeṇa mantramuccārayoccnaiḥ ।
brahmādityāgayogena liṅgātkumbhe suyojayet ॥ (6)
prāṇāpratiṣṭāṃ prakurvīta kumbhedeśikasattamaḥ । (7)
sṛṣṭinyāsaṃ prakurvīta upacārān tadhaścaret ॥
tatpaścāt liṅgamāścādya sāvasā deśikasvayam । (8)
kumbhe devaṃ ca saṃprārthya yātrāhomaṃ samācaret ॥
jñānato jñānato vāpi mayā karmādhunākṛtam । (9)
nyūnādhikakṛtaṃ vāpi kṣamasva karuṇānidhe ॥
itī samprārthya deveśaṃ yātrāhomaṃ samācaret ॥ (10)
yātrāhomam
yātrāhomākhyakarmedaṃ vakṣyate śṛṇu ṣaṇmukha । (i.77a, 10)
liṅgādākarṣaṇātpaścād yātrāhomaṃ caredbudhaḥ ॥ (i.77b)
sūryādiketvanta gṛhai homaṃ prathamamācaret ।
varṣairmāsai tathā vakṣairutabhiścāyanairapi ॥ (2)
titibhiḥrvāsaraiścaiva yogaiśca karaṇairapi ।
nakṣatrarāśibhiḥ ścaiva lagnai horābhireva cā ॥ (3)
dhāmai muhūrtai kṣaṇikaiḥ nimiṣairapi deśikaḥ ।
kāṣṭhābhiśca kalābhiśca homaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ ॥ (4)
pūrvoktānāṃ tu sarveṣāmadhi pratyadhi daivatān ।
homaṃ kuryādviśeṣeṇa uddeśatyāgapūrvakam ॥ (5)
yātrāhomavihīnesyuḥ yete devāśca bādhakāḥ ।
ataḥ sarva prayatnena yātrāhomaṃ sudhiścaret ॥ (6)
yātrādānam
yātrādānamidaṃ vakṣye śṛṇu vatsa sakautukam । (i.77b, 6)
dīkṣitānāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ yātrārthadānamācaret ॥ (7)
gṛhaprītiṃ tataḥ kuryādyathāvidhi vicakṣaṇaḥ ।
daśadānaṃ prakurvīta kannikādānamācaret ॥ (8)
nānāvidhāni dānāni sudhistatra samācaret ।
dīkṣitaṃ dṛḍhagātraṃ tu yuvānāṃ deśikasvayam ॥ (9)
āhūya tasya śīrṣe tu vastraṃ baddhvaiva sādaram ।
gandhapuṣpairaṅkṛtya ukṣaṃ tanmūrdhni pūjayet ॥ (10)
tatra kumbho tu vinyasya sāvadhānena cetasā ।
anyeṣāmapi kumbhānāmeva meva samācaret ॥ (i.78a)
sūryādiśī ca dhāmūrtiḥ tatkrameṇālayaṃ guruḥ ।
vikīryamāṇaiḥ puṣpaiśca rājatairavikāñcanaiḥ ॥ (2)
lājākṣatairgandhajalai himābru sahitairapi ।
chatracāmaramukhyaiśca nānāvādya purassaram ॥ (3)
brāhmaṇairvedaghoṣaiśca gāyakairveṇikorapi ।
śaṅkhamāpūrayadbhiścastāpako nāṭyakovidaiḥ ॥ (4)
rūpājīvābhaṇairyuktān dīpikābhiralaṅkṛtān ।
kumbhānudvāhatāṃ puṃsāmathāstādvaṭavastrakaiḥ ॥ (5)
prasārite pratipadaṃ śambhave brāhmaṇairyutān ।
ācāryo ṛtvijaścaiva darbhān saṅgṛhya pāṇinā ॥ (6)
mūlamantraṃ samuccārya pradhānācārya yeva hi ।
tanmantrānuccaranta spṛṣṭvā tān ṛtvijo ghaṭān ॥ (7)
dhāmapradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā syatvāmagha gṛhādbahi ।
arghyapādyādibhiḥ sarvairupacārai puraskṛtān ॥ (8)
nivṛttidvāramārgeṇa viśedyāgālayaṃ guruḥ ।
yāgavedighaṭā nītvānantādīn pūjatonyaset ॥ (9)
vardhanī savyabhāge tu vinikṣpya gurusvayam ।
sarvāṃśca kalaśāṃścaiva śivābhimukhatonyaset ॥ (10)
kṣīraṃ ca phāṇitaṃ caiva śuddhalājān phalāni ca ॥
tebhyaḥ kṛtvā tu naivedyamaśīrvādaṃ samācaret । (i.78b)
devālayayamadho gatvā pūrvesyācāryasattamāḥ ॥
tasmin pūrvokta mūrtyugnau prāyaścittāhutiṃ hunet । (2)
jayādimastrāhutimapi agneḥ pūrṇāhutiṃ caret ॥
agnerudvāsanaṃ kṛtvā yāgānyutpādanaṃ caret ।
pūrvoktenaiva mārgeṇa anyupādanamācaret ॥ (3)
sūryasyāstamayād deśiko yāgamārabhet ।
rātṛvagnaśca pūrṇatvādrātrāvesa samācaret ॥ (4)
divāhīnakālatvanna yāgārambhaṃ divācaret ।
pūjārambhaṃ pradoṣe ca kuryāddeśikasattamaḥ ॥ (5)
paredyurhomakāryāntu sāyaṃ prātasamācaret ।
udvāsanaṃ divā kuryā na rātrāviti niścayaḥ ॥ (6)
sadyodhivāsane mātraṃ divā vā niśi vā caret ।
udvāsanaṃ tathaiva syādyitthaṃ homavidhismṛtā ॥ (7)
yāgapūjāvidhiṃ vakṣye śṛṇu vatsa viśeṣataḥ ।
ācāryo ṛtvijaścaiva sāyaṃ snātvā samāhitāḥ ॥ (8)
praviśya yāgaśālāṃ tu nityapūjāṃ samācaret ।
nityapūjāṃ vidhiṃścātra kuryāt puṇyāhamādarāt ॥ (9)
snapanaṃ pañcagavyaṃ ca nirmālyasya visarjanam ।
nāḍisandhānamārgeṇa darpaṇe kūrcayogataḥ ॥
abhiṣekaṃ prakurvīta darpaṇe deśikasvayam । (10)
paścāt nāḍīṃ tu saṃyojya sakūrcaṃ devakumbhke ॥
dhūpadīṃ ca naivedhyaṃ nityavatkārayetsudhiḥ । (11)
catuṣkoṇe catuṣkumbhān vinyaseddeśikottamaḥ ॥
yāmya dvārasya pūrve'smin tvagni kumbhesya paścime । (i.79a)
tanmadhye kumbhayugmaṃ ca chulliṃ tatra vinikṣipet ॥
anyadvāravidik madhyetra kumbhāṃ nikṣipedbudhaḥ ।
saptaviṃśati kumbhāṃśca kṣipetteṣu padeṣu cā ॥ (2)
īśāne vedikāmadhye kumbhayugmaṃ ca nikṣipet ।
vedyāpūrvadyuttarāntamaṣṭamaṅgalaśobhitam ॥ (3)
daśāyudhaṃ tu nikṣipya punaḥ puṇyāhamācaret ।
niśi somasya pūjāṃ tu divā sūryasya pūjanam ॥
yetayoḥ prathamaṃ kuryātpūjanaṃ deśikottamaḥ । (4)
somasūryārcanaṃ kṛtvā dhagatva maṇṭapādbahiḥ ॥
pādau hastaṃ ca prakṣālya ācame bhasmadhārayet । (5)
yāgaśālāṃ punargatvā sāmānyārghyaṃ samācaret ॥
tanmadhye nagganaṃ prokṣya śāntyātīto samarcayet । (6)
sadāśivadinotrāstramarcayedantarikṣake ॥
pūrvaṃ yāmyaṃ ca paścimāntaṃ prapūjayet । (7)
toraṇeṣu tathā dvāre cārcayedvidhivadguruḥ ॥
śāntiṃ hutiṃ tathārogyaṃ paścime balatoraṇam । (8)
bhadraṃ ca vijayaṃ caiva nīlalohitamapyathā ॥
vajraṃ ca paścime deśe tvadhidevān samarcayet । (9)
caturvedān caturdvāraṃ caturyaṅjamathācaret ॥
vasūśca marutaścaiva guruḥśūleṣu pūjayet । (10)
praśāntaṃ śiśiraṃ caiva parjanyañca jayantakam ॥
dhanadaṃ śrīpradaṃ caiva bhūtañcāmṛta jīvanam । (i.79b)
etān patākamadhye tu pūjayedaṣṭasevatāḥ ॥
śākhāmūle tu lokādīn agre somādi pūjayet । (2)
kavargādi kṣavargānta dhvajavastre prapūjayet ॥
dvāraṇāṃ savyapārśve tu kalākumbhān samarcayet । (3)
dvāramadhye maheśādīn mantrānāmapi samarcayet ॥ (4)
kumudaṃ kumudākṣaṃ ca puṇḍarīkaṃ ca vāmanam ।
śaṅkhukarṇaṃ sarvanetraṃ sumukhaṃ supratiṣṭhitam ॥ (5)
dvārordhvemarcayedetān nityadvāraṃ prapūjayet ।
paścimadvārabāhye tu dvāradevān samarcayet ॥ (6)
bhuvaṅgaśca pataṅgaśca nasyordhvādhaḥ pradeśataḥ । (7)
tayormadhye ramā vāṇiṃ vighnarājaṃ samarcayet ॥
nandinaṃ ca mahākālaṃ gaṅgā kālindimeva cā । (8)
dvārasya dakṣiṇe vāme daṇḍi muṇḍisamarcanam ॥
dvārāgre vṛṣabhaṃ pūjya candrasūryau kavāṭayoḥ ।
śilāyāṃ medinīṃ pūjya suṣire mantradevatāḥ ॥ (9)
vasūn kīleṣu saṃpūjya marutaḥ kampadeśataḥ ।
arcayetsarvadevānāṃ dhūpadīpaṃ pradāpayet ॥ (10)
bhūmyantarikṣa svargeṣu vighnasaṅgha nivāraṇam ।
huṃphaḍantāstramantreṇa bhūmau pārṣṇi prakuṭṭanam ॥ (i.80a)
trivāradakṣa parṣṇīkuṭṭanaṃ kārayedbudhaḥ ।
svarge nirīkṣya mūlena cāntarikṣe tathācaret ॥ (2)
puṣpaṃ nikṣipe tarjanyā humphaḍantāstra mantrataḥ ।
vighnarājaṃ samabhyarcya anyadvārāṇi bandhanam ॥ (3)
śilāñca savyapādena saṃspṛśedvarma mantrataḥ ।
vāmaśākhaṃ samāśṛtya dakṣapādapurassaram ॥ (4)
antaḥpraviśya mūlena astradvāre samarcayet ।
yāgaṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā nairṛtyā tu deśikaḥ ॥ (5)
tṛṇādyā bandhavastrānāṃ kṣityādyai pūjajedguruḥ ।
antaḥ stambheṣu dharmādīn arcayecca pradakṣiṇam ॥ (6)
tadbahidvādaśastambhe dvādaśāditya pūjanam ।
dhvajānaṣṭau samabhyarcya aṣṭamaṅgala pūjanam ॥ (7)
sadāśivapadavyāpta maṇṭapaṃ bhāvayetbudhaḥ ।
vedi dakṣiṇapārśve tu vyāghrañjīnamukheṣvapi ॥ (8)
āsaneṣu guru samyagupaviśyottarāmukhaḥ ।
prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā bhūtaśuddhiṃ samācaret ॥ (9)
antaryāgārcanaṃ kṛtvā sthānaśuddhiṃ samācaret ।
kṛtvārghyasthāpanaṃ tatra dravyaśuddhiṃ samācaret ॥ (10)
śivahastaṃ samāśritya śivohamiti bhāvayet ।
jñānakhaḍgaṃ prakurvīta ṣaṭ triṃśaddarbha saṃyutam ॥ (i.80b)
kuryādveṇyākṛtī grandhiḥ darpyayaṃ gulaṃ karedbudhaḥ ।
agraṃ vedāṅgulaṃ proktaṃ pāṇiyugmaṃ gulaṃ bhavet ॥ (2)
pādau tathaiva kartavyau kaṇṭhamaṅgalaṣaṭkakam ।
dvādaśāṅgulamātrāhuḥ śiṣṭamaṅgamanīṣiṇaḥ ॥ (3)
āmūlāgraṃ tu kartavya hastamātrapramāṇakam ।
jñānakhaḍge cāstramantraṃ saptadhā cābhimantrayet ॥ (4)
svadakṣiṇakaṭyāṃmācāryaḥ dhārayeda pramattadhiḥ ।
naiṛtyā pañcagavyaṃ tu tvatvā cattadanantaram ॥ (5)
lājacandanasiddhārthān bhasmapuṣpakuśākṣatān ।
etān śarāve saṅgṛhya jñānakhaḍgaṃ tu nikṣipet ॥ (6)
vīkṣaṇādibhiropaitatsaṃśodhya vidhivadguruḥ ।
saṃhitā mantrataḥ samyagarcayeddeśikasvayam ॥ (7)
śalyaśedha nivṛtyarthaṃ śodhayedyāgamaṇṭapam ।
saṃskāramaṣṭādaśadhā kuryāddeśikasattamaḥ ॥ (8)
śarāvasthitadravyāṇi vikarān yāgamaṇṭape ।
arghyodakaṃ pañcagavyaṃ prokṣyedyāgamaṇṭape ॥ (9)
śuddhalājān kuśānarghya salilena samaṃ sudhiḥ ।
antarikṣentarāyāṇāṃ śāntyarthaṃ prokṣayedbudhaḥ ॥ (10)
arghya pāśupadaṃ cāstra vardhanīśāsīmani ।
niścalaṃ vinyasedvidvān jñānakhaḍgaṃ karedguruḥ ॥ (i.81a)
gṛhītvā pūrvadhṛtvā naiṛtyāṃ pūjayedvidhiḥ ।
vāyordakṣiṇakumbhe tu mahālakṣmīṃ prapūjayet ॥
athavā kṣetrapālaṃ vā īśānyestraṃ samarcayet । (2)
astrapañcavardhanīṃ caiva calāsanavidhānataḥ ॥
dvitīyyāvaraṇī bhūtanandyādīn pūjayetkramāt । (3)
indrādi viṣṇuparyantaṃ daśakumbhā prapūjayet ॥
daśāyudhān samabhyarcya śivājñāṃ śrāvayedguruḥ । (4)
ācāryo vardhanīṃ dhṛtvā cāstrakumbhaṃ ca śiṣyataḥ ॥
vardhanīnālataḥ stīrthamanavarccinna dhārayā । (5)
astramantraṃ japaṃ dvidvān yāgaśālā samantataḥ ॥
kṛtvā pradakṣiṇaṃ pūrvasthānenasya sthirāsanam । (6)
kṛtvā tatho jñānakhaḍgaṃ vardhanyāṃ nikṣipetsudhiḥ ॥
yāgasaṃrakṣaṇārthaṃ hi śivājñāṃ śrāvayettayoḥ । (7)
vāyoḥ prācī diśāṃ kubhe gaṇeśaṃ tu samarcayet ॥ (8)
somakumbhātpūrvakumbhe tu gurun saptasamarcayet ।
gurūn saṃprārthya vidhivat kuryādvijñāpanaṃ sudhiḥ ॥ (9)
nāmadheyaṃ tu kumbhānāṃ vakṣyate śāsrasammatam ।
pūrvadvārotttare pārśve svaṃyaṃ bhūnandiśāntayaḥ ॥ (10)
tasyeva dakṣiṇeparśve kāśendravarasmṛtaḥ ।
dakṣiṇdvārapūrve tu vidyā bhṛṅgyagnaya smṛtaḥ ॥ (i.81b)
tadvārepaścime caiva heramba yamaveghasaḥ ।
nairṛtiśca tathā viṣṇu vṛṣabhaśca nivṛttikā ॥ (2)
etāśca paścimadvāra dakṣabhāge prakīrtitāḥ ।
dvārasyottare bhāge vāyumāvaruṇā guruḥ ॥ (3)
pratiṣṭā devi vighnāḥ uttaradvārapaścime ।
tatpūrvabhāge caṇḍeśa so gurvīśamānakāḥ ॥
saptaviśati kumbhānāmevaṃ nāmaprakīrtitam । (4)
etasya śivayāgasya kumbhanāma prakīrtitam ॥
śivetareṣu yāgeṣu kumbhāścaiteṣi sammatāḥ ॥ (5)
sūryakalāśca brahmāca lakṣmī vighneśadeśikāḥ ।
astrāstra vardhanī ceti munibhiḥśāstrako vidaiḥ ॥ (6)
pratiṣṭeyasya devasya tritīyyaṃ caturiyykam ।
kumbheśvāvaraṇaṃ dvidvān pūjayecchāstravettamaḥ ॥ (7)
vedi dakṣiṇadeśaṃ tu gatvā deśikasattamaḥ ।
prokṣyācāryāmbunā vediṃ vidhivatpūjayedguruḥ ॥ (8)
pañcāsanaṃ samabhyarcya śivamūrtiṃ samarcayet ।
devasya vāmabhāge tu vardhaniṃ ca prapūjayet ॥ (9)
astivaktraṃ ca yadvāraṃ tadvaktraṃ puraṣānanam ।
tadābhyārcya yeddhīmān hṛdayādi tathaivacā ॥ (10)
dvitīyyāvaraṇadīnāṃ pūrvādyarcanamācaret ।
dhūpadīpaṃ ca naivedyam upacāraṃ samarpya cā ॥ (i.82a)
maghālayasya vadanaṃ paścimābhimukhaṃ kṛtaḥ ।
itī cetyucyate vatsa śṛṇivadānaṃ ca kāraṇam ॥ (2)
paścimābhimukhaṃ dvāraṃ sadyojātamitīrayate ।
nivṛttitvāca devasya mukhaṃ tatpuruṣaṃ matam ॥
anayoreva sambandhamapaśyaṃ parikīrtitam । (3)
phaṇiryādhābilam kṛtvā cāsti śambhustathā mukhe ॥
agnikāryavidhiṃ vakṣye śrṛṇu vatsehavistat । (4)
haste gṛhītvāgnipātraṃ vediṃ kṛtvā pradakṣiṇam ॥
upakuṇḍanābhideśe copaviśya gurusvayam । (5)
śodhayeddeśikaḥ kuṇḍaṃ caturbhīvīkṣaṇādibhiḥ ॥
ghananādyaṣṭasaṃskān kṛtvā deśikasattamaḥ । (6)
nyasya pañcakalāstatra madhyādiṣu ca pañcasu ॥
sūtreṇāveṣṭayetkuṇḍaṃ kalāmayāmathārcayet । (7)
kuṇḍe vā sthaṇḍile vāpi saikataṃ vā tuṣo'pi vā ॥
hastamātraṃ caturaśraṃ staṇḍilaṃ kalpayetsudhiḥ । (8)
prāgagramudagagraṃ ca dvikuśeṇa samālikhet ॥
brahmajajñānamantreṇa madhyarekhāṃ samālikhet ।
nākesuparṇamatreṇa dakṣiṇetu samālikhet ॥ (9)
āpyāsveti mantreṇa rekhāmuttaramālikhet ।
yo rudra iti mantreṇamadhye tiryak samālikhet ॥ (10)
tiryak paścimareghāṃ ca idaṃ viṣṇu samālikhet ।
indraṃ viśveti mantreṇa pūrvareghāṃsamālikhet ॥ (11)
adbhirabhyukṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā śakalaṃ tu visarjayet ।
darbhadvayaṃ triguṇitaṃ śūlākāraṃ vinikṣipet ॥
idaṃ ca vajrīkaraṇaṃ śivamantreṇa deśikaḥ । (i.82b)
darbhadvaya samāyuktaṃ pūrvāgraṃ cottarāgrakam ॥
idaṃ catuṣpadaṃ nyāsaṃ hṛdayena vinakṣipet । (2)
granthihīnaṃ viṣṭaraṃ tu saptaviṃśati darbhakam ॥
ṣoḍaśāguladīrgheṇa vartulaṃ veṣṭayetsudhiḥ । (3)
īśānāgraṃ kuṇḍamadhye nyasya vāgīśvarā vihā ॥
āvāhya pūjanaṃ kṛtvā dhūpa dīpaṃ tataścaret । (4)
yandi kūrcaṃ granthiyuktaṃ phalaṃ tasyocyate śṛṇu ॥
dagghā yadā bhavedgranthiḥ āśaucaṃsambhavedihā । (5)
tadrārkadoṣa viśudhyarthaṃ snānaṃ tatra sudhiścaret ॥
snānābhāve tu naiṣphalyaṃ karmaṇaḥ parikīrtitaḥ । (6)
etasmātkāraṇāt kūrcaṃ granthihīnaṃ prakalpayet ॥
mṛtpātre tāmrapātre vā granthi hitvā sudhistataḥ । (7)
āgneyeśāna nairṛtyāṃ mekhalāyā vinakṣipet ॥
śakalaṃ cāstramantreṇa nairṛtyāṃ deśikakṣipet । (8)
nirīkṣaṇādibhiḥ śodhya vahnimūrtiṃ samarcayet ॥
imamagniṃ ca nābhyagnau bindvagnau yojayet sudhiḥ । (9)
recakādi krameṇaiva paścādbāhyagni yojanam ॥
sahitābhyarcanaṃ kṛtvā saṃrakṣyābhyavakuṇṭhya ca । (10)
mūlena cāmṛtīkṛtya devasya tvotimantrataḥ ॥
hastābhyāṃ pātramādāya kuṇḍaṃ bhrāmya trivārakam । (i.83a)
dhyātvāpi śivabījaṃ tu nasya jānuyugaṃ lapi ॥
vāgīśabindvātmakaṭatamatvagniṃ deśikasvayam । (2)
vāgīśvarīṃ garbhadeśe śivamantreṇa yojayet ॥
nikṣipyāgniṃ svapurattaḥ juṣṭodamūneti mantrakam । (3)
japtayaṃ paridhānaṃ ca samarpya vidhivadguruḥ ॥
śaucārthaṃ ca jalaṃ datvā tathaivācamanīyyakam ।
nikṣipyāgna vindhanāni prajvālyapyastra mantrataḥ ॥ (4)
garbhāgniṃ samabhyarcya rakṣārthaṃ kautukaṃ caret ।
astramantrān mekhalāyāṃ dakṣiṇe vinyasetsudhiḥ ॥ (5)
caulāntopanayanaṃ bhūtakarma samācaret ।
samāvartakarmāpi vivāhāntaṃ samācaret ॥ (6)
paścādabhyarca pitarau hutvā pūrṇāhutiṃ caret ।
varṇajvālagandhaśabdān pariṣicya vidhitsudhiḥ ॥ (7)
eteṣā viparī tatra prāyascittaṃ vidhīyyate ।
udvāsya pitarau paścāt vyāhṛtiḥ pariṣecanam ॥ (8)
agneraṅgāhuti hutvā vibhajedagnimādarāt ।
kuṇḍānurūpaṃ śāstroktaṃ dhūpadīpaṃ caru kṛte ॥ (9)
tattatkuṇdeṣu saṃskāraṃ kuryāddeśikasattamaḥ ।
kuṇḍasaṃskārahīne tu agniṃ niṣphalatāmiyā ॥ (10)
caruṃ prakurvīta carvagnau śeṣāgni dhūpadīpayoḥ ।
carumādāya kuṇḍasya samīpe nikṣipetsudhiḥ ॥ (11)
atrāgniṃ prārthayedvidvān paścāddhyānaṃ samācaret ।
agnetvāmeśvaraṃ tejaḥ pāvanaṃ paramaṃ yataḥ ॥ (i.83b)
tasmātvatīyya hṛtpadme sthāpyeśantarpayāmyaham ।
saṃsthāpya devaṃ tatraiva pūjāṃ sāvaraṇāṃ caret ॥ (2)
kuryādvaktrānusandhānaṃ mūlamantraṃ samuccaran ।
śivanāsodbhavaṃ tejaḥ śikhālīnaṃ care sudhiḥ ॥ (3)
kumbhāgni deśikānāṃ tu nāḍīsandhānamācaret ।
mūlenāṣṭottaraśataṃ tarpaṇaṃ tu samācaret ॥ (4)
brahmāgaistaddaśāṃśaṃ tu hutvā pūrṇāhutiṃ caret ।
viśeṣatarpaṇaṃ cāpi kuryātpūrvavadevahi ॥ (5)
durnnimittādi doṣāṇāṃ yathāśakti hunedguruḥ ।
pavitrīkaraṇārthaṃ tu mūlamaṣṭottaraṃ hunet ॥ (6)
paścātpūrṇāhutiṃ kuryāt tarpaṇaṃ dīpanaṃ caret ।
evaṃ kuṇḍākhya pakṣe tu mūlāhutiritīritaḥ ॥ (7)
catuḥ sahasrahomaṃ tu sa pañcāśatakaṃ smṛtam ॥
āhutipramāṇam
karṣārthaṃ goghṛtenaiva ekāhutimathācaret । (i.84b)
dviparvaṃ ghṛtatarjanyā bindurityabhidhīyate ॥ (2)
binduṣaṭkaṃ tu karṣārthaṃ tadvayaṃ kuḍupaṃ bhavet ।
karṣadvayaṃ tu muṣṭiṃ syāt tadvayaṃ prasṛtirbhavet ॥ (3)
tadvayaṃ kuḍupaṃ proktaṃ tadviśaṃ dhāraṇaṃ bhavet ।
dhāraṇaṃ tu phalaṃ proktaṃ prastaṃ viṃśaphalaṃ bhavet ॥ (4)
sāntaṃ lāntaṃ ca sammiśraṃ miśrānte sāntasaṃyutam ।
tasyānte āhatiṃ vanhau etanmadhye vinā hutim ॥ (5)
mantrānte devatā tṛptiṃ madhye daitya priyaṃ bhavet । (6)
sṛvapūrṇaghṛtāgrāhya jvālāgre dvādaśāṃ gulam ॥
yavapramāṇena viśchinnaṃ viśchinnaṃ hutaniṣphalam । (7)
aṅguṣṭamūla parvāntaṃ ghṛtaṃ grāhyamadhomukham ॥
aṅguṣṭa lalitaṃ vāpi idaṃ bindumudīritam । (8)
dvātriṃśadbinduniṣkaikaṃ saṃpūrṇaṃ ghṛtagattayoḥ । (9)
prastaṃ sarpi śataṃ hutvā pañcaprastaṃ sahasrakam ॥
na vine daśalakṣaṃ tu āvarte pañcalakṣakam । (10)
punarāvartalakṣaṃ tu āhutim uttamocyate ॥
uttamaṃ ca tṛdhā kṛtvā madhyamaṃ adhamaṃ tathā । (i.85a)
adhamādhamatṛtayaṃ kālaṃ vittānusārataḥ ॥
dugdhasyamadhunā dadhya sṛva pūrṇāhutirbhavet । (2)
śaktimātreṇa juhuyāttilaṃ caiva tu sarṣapam ॥
vrīhīṇāṃ pañcaviṃśadbhirote dvayasamāyute । (3)
priyaṅgu gomaruścaiva alpaṃ tu tilavaddhṛnet ॥ (4)
pūritaṃ śuktirityuktaṃ mānāntenaiva kārayet ।
odanaṃ cākṣamātreṇa śaktyardhena kulutthakam ॥ (5)
niṣpāvannavabhiḥ proktaṃ yava veṇu kulutthavat । (6)
karpūrādīni gandhāni māṣamātraṃ tu homayet ॥
śaktimātreṇa śāliṃ tu viṃśati māṣamudgakau । (7)
pṛthukaṃ dhānyakaṃ saktumuṣṭimātreṇa homayet ॥
phalāni svapramāṇena patrāṇi śarasaṃkhyayā । (8)
triṃśadaṃśeka bhāgenā bhitvā tu phanasaṃ hunet ॥ (9)
vihitaṃ phalamātreṇa kandaṃ bhitvā tu homayet ।
tanmātreṇa gulaṃ proktaṃ mapūpaṃ svapramāṇataḥ ॥ (10)
kṣīrānnaṃ kṛsarānnaṃ ca gulānnaṃ śaktimātrataḥ ।
kareṇa juhuyādravyaṃ yuktadravyaṃ sṛveṇa ca ॥ (11)
sthālyāmannaṃ vinikṣipya hunedyuktapramāṇataḥ ॥
caruḥ
caruprastadvaṃ tacca homakarmaṇi kalpayet । (i.85b, 2)
adhamaṃ tadbhaveddhome dviguṇaṃ madhyamaṃ bhavet ॥ (3)
triguṇaṃ cottamaṃ proktaṃ naiva pātraprakalpitam ।
prastaṃ kuḍupasamyuktaṃ piśācāñceṣu pācayet ॥ (4)
pūrṇāhutī sruvaṃ kuryāt sarpiṣā dvīpalena tu ॥ (5)
samittu
ādityāṅgulamāyāmaṃ kaniṣṭāṅgulināhakam । (i.85b, 5)
avakraṃ nirṣaṇaṃ caiva śuṣkam caiva śubhāvaham ॥
paridhi
āyāmaṃ paridhīnāṃ ca vedayuk triṃśadaṅgulam । (i.85b, 7)
madhyamāṅguli parīṇāhaṃ savaktram saṃpraṇaṃ tyajet ॥
proktaṃ yena samittena paridhīdhmāni saṅgṛhet । (8)
ekamekhalakuṇḍaṃ cenmekhalāsu paristaret ॥
dvimekhale tritīye tri dvimekhalaṃ tu madhyame । (9)
kuṇḍe vā sthaṇḍile bāhye paristaraṇamucyate ॥
viṣṭaram
dhātukāśa samāyuktaṃ grandhitaṃ savyamāgrataḥ । (i.85b, 10)
ravyaṅgulantu mūlāgraṃ grandhirvedāgulaṃ bhavet ॥
ahavinnivedayoddhīmānyathāvittānusārataḥ । (i.86a)
pūjāgṛhṇādi liṅgastho vahnisthorhaviṣārhataṃ ॥ (2)
eka yeva śivattasmād ubhayatra sadāśivaḥ ।
iti sañcintya yatnenā luṅgagnau ca śivaṃ yajet ॥ (3)
bahiḥ bali
